# Controls catalog and API contract

Every QindaTK control is a QML type in `src/qml/` with the same name as the
file. Import with `import QindaTK as Tk`. This page is the contract: names,
properties, signals, sizes. `docs/catalog.json` is the machine-readable
mirror generated by `tools/scripts/gen_catalog.py`; keep both current.
The shared state, notification and accessible-action policy is in
[control-interaction.md](control-interaction.md).

## Conventions shared by all controls

| Convention | Rule |
| --- | --- |
| Base | `QtQuick.Templates as T` types for anything interactive; `Item`/`Rectangle` for passive pieces. |
| Height | `Tk.Theme.size.control` (24). `small: true` → `controlSm` (20). `large: true` → `controlLg` (28). Chips 32 (`size.chip`), island actions 36 (`size.action`), rows 22 (`size.row`), headers 24 (`size.header`), tabs 22 (`size.tab`). |
| Text | body 12px (`font.body`); captions 10px; overlines 10px uppercase semibold tracked. |
| Radius | `radius.sm` (4) for controls, `radius.md` (6) for chips/panels/popovers, `radius.full` for islands, badges, switches. |
| Colours | Only `Tk.Theme.color.*` roles. Rest: `controlBg`/`controlBorder`; hover: `controlHoverBg`/`controlHoverBorder`; active/checked: `controlActiveBg`/`controlActiveBorder`; inputs: `inputBg`/`inputBorder`/`inputFocusBorder`; chips: `chipBg`/`chipBorder`/`chipHoverBg`/`chipHoverBorder`. |
| Focus | Theme-coloured focus ring around the focused control or delegate. Custom composites use `activeFocus` so keyboard and assistive focus remains visible; each row below names any stable ring objectName. |
| Disabled | `opacity: Tk.Theme.opacity.disabled`. |
| Tooltip | `property string tooltip` on every control; show a child `Tk.ToolTip` on hover and use the text as the accessible name for icon-only controls. The attached form creates a QtQuick.Controls style tooltip. |
| Icons | `iconName` (Lucide name, see `qtk-preview --list-icons`) rendered by `Tk.Icon` at `size.icon` (14) or `size.iconSm` (12) when `small`. |
| Motion | colour/opacity transitions `Tk.Theme.motion.fast`; nothing longer than `motion.base` in chrome. |
| Naming | `iconName`, `trailingIconName`, `text`, `small`, `large`, `variant`, `checked`, `tooltip`, `value/from/to/stepSize`. Signals in past tense (`clicked`, `activated`, `valueModified`, `closed`). |
| objectName | Not set by the control; internal parts that tests need are named in the tables below. |

Layout primitives (`Flex`, `Grid`, `Stack`, `Box`, `Scroll`) are documented
in [layout.md](layout.md); theming in [theming.md](theming.md); docking in
[docking.md](docking.md).

## Text

`CodeEditor` and `CodeDocument` provide source editing, local UTF-8
save/reopen, revision-tagged syntax and diagnostics, line-number gutter,
find/navigation and native Qt undo/IME behavior. See [code-editor.md](code-editor.md)
for the provider contract and object names.

| Type | Base | Properties | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Label` | Text | `muted`, `disabled`, `accent`, `mono`, `selectable` | body 12px, `elide: ElideRight`, `NativeRendering`. `selectable: true` enables mouse/keyboard text selection and copy with Ctrl+C; Ctrl+A selects all. Default false; ordinary text sizing and rendering are unchanged. Its internal selectable overlay is `selectableText`. Constrained no-wrap labels copy the displayed elided string. |
| `Caption` | Label | — | 10px, muted. |
| `Overline` | Label | `title`, `wide` | uppercase 10px semibold tracked; the section-header signature. |
| `Mono` | Label | — | mono family, 11px. |
| `Heading` | Label | `level` (1 display, 2 title, 3 large) | semibold. |

### Code editing

| Type | Base | Properties and commands | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `CodeDocument` | QObject (C++) | `text`, `revision`, `lineCount`, `modified`, `filePath`, `errorString`, `syntaxTokens`, `diagnostics`, `completions`, `folds`, `visualLineCount`, `tokenColors`; `replace`, `editCursors`, `undo`, `redo`, `findNext`, `lineColumn`, `positionForLineColumn`, `foldLines`, `unfoldLines`, `lineForVisualRow`, `load`, `save`, `publishSyntaxTokens`, `publishDiagnostics`, `publishCompletions`, `applyCompletion` | Native `QTextDocument` buffer. Provider results carry a revision and stale results are rejected. UTF-16 offsets; atomic local UTF-8 save. See [code-editor.md](code-editor.md). |
| `CodeLanguageService` | QObject (C++) | `document`, `program`, `arguments`, `languageId`, read-only `status`, `errorString`, `documentUri`; `start`, `stop`, `requestCompletion(cursorPosition,prefixStart)` | Optional explicit local LSP stdio process. Versioned UTF-16 diagnostics and revision-safe plain completions; bounded shutdown and process cleanup. See [code-editor.md](code-editor.md). |
| `CodeEditor` | T.Control | `document`, `languageService`, `tooltip`, `readOnly`, `showLineNumbers`, `showFindBar`, `additionalCursors`; `editor`, `cursorPosition`, `selectionStart`, `selectionEnd`, `findNext`, `goToLine`, `addCursorBelow`, `clearAdditionalCursors`, `foldSelection`, `unfoldAtCursor`, `acceptCompletion`, `requestLanguageCompletion` | Source text view with syntax colors, diagnostics gutter, visual folding, secondary carets, revision-tagged suggestions, Ctrl+Space language completion, search and cursor status. Internal objectNames: `codeFindField`, `codeFindNext`, `codeViewport`, `codeLineGutter`, `codeLineNumber`, `codeScroll`, `codeSourceEdit`, `codeCompletionPanel`, `codeCompletionItem`, `codeStatus`. |

## Passive structure

| Type | Properties | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AppWindow` | `T.ApplicationWindow` | `menuBar` (a `Tk.MenuBar`), `toolBars` (list of items stacked under it, usually `Tk.ToolBar`), default: content, `findBar` (slot above the status bar), `statusBar` (slot at the bottom), `toolBarsVisible`, `statusBarVisible`, `contentHostItem` | — | The window of a document app: four bands, each as tall as its content, the content taking the rest; theme background and a full palette bridge so native popups inherit the theme; set `width`/`height` once and never resize a shown window from the app. objectNames `appHeader`, `appToolBars`, `appContent`, `appFooter`, `appFindBar`, `appStatusBar`. |
| `Viewport` | Item | `zoom`, `minZoom`, `maxZoom`, `fitMode` (`"none"`, `"width"`, `"page"`), `contentPadding`, `wheelZoomStep`, `interactive`, `accessibleName` (default “Document viewport”); read-only `contentWidth/Height`, `viewportWidth/Height`, `contentItem`, `scrollItem`; default: the document surface | `zoomModified(zoom)` | Zoomable, scrollable document viewport on the `canvas` role: the single child keeps its implicit size in document units and is scaled by `zoom`, centred when narrower than the view. Ctrl+wheel zooms about the cursor, Shift+wheel scrolls sideways, middle-drag pans; two fingers on a touchscreen pinch-zoom about their centroid and pan (a touchpad pinch zooms), and `touchPan` (default off) lets one finger pan — read-only `gestureActive` while either runs (D-304); ordinary wheel input reaches an ancestor Scroll at a pan edge (D-021). When focused, arrows/PageUp/PageDown/Home/End pan to hard bounds and unconsumed edge keys may reach an ancestor Scroll; Plus/Equal and Minus zoom. The Pane role reports its name, focus and zoom; accessible increase/decrease use the same guarded zoom operation. Disabled or `interactive: false` views leave Tab order and ignore these user actions, while owner `zoom` assignment remains silent. The focus ring is themed and does not change geometry. `setZoom(z, anchor)`, `zoomAbout(anchor, factor)`, `zoomIn/Out()`, `fitWidth()`, `fitPage()`, `toContent(point)`, `fromContent(point)`, `ensureVisible(rect)`. objectNames `viewportScroll`, `viewportSurface`, `viewportContent`, `viewportFocusRing`, `viewportPinch`, `viewportTouchPan`. |
| `Box` | `color`, `borderColor`, `borderWidth`, `borderLeft/Top/Right/Bottom`, `radius`, `padding(+sides)`, `fill`, `clipContent`, `interactive`, `tooltip`, `hovered`, `pressed`, `contentItem` | `clicked(point)`, `rightClicked(point)`, `doubleClicked(point)` | The CSS box; see layout.md. `interactive` enables a named Button with a theme focus ring; pointer, Space, Return/Enter and `Accessible.pressAction` share `activate(point)`. Disabled/passive boxes cannot focus or activate. `tooltip` supplies the accessible name and hover tooltip. Stable objectName `boxFocusRing`. |
| `Scroll` | `overflowX/overflowY` (`Tk.Scroll.Auto/Hidden/Always`), `padding`, `contentRightInset`, `contentX/Y`, `contentItem`, `flickable`, `accessibleName` | — | `ensureVisible(item)`, `scrollToTop/Bottom()`; `contentRightInset` keeps right-edge controls clear of the overlay scrollbar; keyboard edge handoff and nested focus reveal (D-021). |
| `ScrollBar` | T.ScrollBar | — | thin overlay; used by Scroll, attachable to any Flickable. |
| `Divider` | `vertical`, `thickness`, `inset` | — | 1px rule. |
| `Spacer` | `size` | — | flexible space in a Flex. |
| `Island` | `spacing`, `paddingH`, `paddingV` | — | rounded glass pill over the canvas; children in a centred row. |
| `SectionHeader` | `title`, `count`, `collapsible`, `collapsed`, `canToggle`, `countNeedsOwnLine` (read); default: trailing actions | `toggle()` | At least `size.row` high; when the title and note exceed the available width, the title stays on the first line and the note wraps below it, growing the row. When collapsible it is a disclosure button: Tab then Space/Return/Enter toggles, and `Accessible.pressAction` uses the same toggle operation as a tap. The accessible name is `title` (or “Section” when empty); its description reports expanded/collapsed state. Disabled disclosures retain the button role but cannot focus or toggle. Noncollapsible headers are static text. objectNames `sectionTitle` on the Overline and `sectionCount_<title>` on a wrapped note. |
| `Panel` | `title`, `iconName`, `grip`, `headerVisible`, `collapsible`, `collapsed`, `closable`, `floatable`, `floating`, `active`, `padding`, `color`; `actions` (header slot); `headerItem`, `bodyItem` | `collapseToggled(bool)`, `closeRequested()`, `floatRequested()` | objectNames `panelHeader`, `panelTitle`, `panelBody`, `panelCollapse`, `panelFloat`, `panelClose`. |
| `Icon` | `name`, `source`, `size`, `color`, `strokeWidth`, `tint`, `available` | — | C++; Lucide set + `Tk.Icons.registerIcon(name, [paths])`. |
| `ToolTip` | T.ToolTip | — | caption text on `tooltipBg`; default delay/timeout use `motion.tooltipDelay`/`motion.tooltipTimeout`. Declare it as a child: `Tk.ToolTip { text: control.tooltip; visible: control.hovered }`. Never the attached form (`ToolTip.text:`): that instantiates the QtQuick.Controls style's tooltip, not this one. |
| `IconButton` | `iconName`, `iconSize`, `small`, `danger`, `ghost`, `tooltip`, `iconColor`, `checkable/checked` (T.AbstractButton) | `clicked` | 24/20px square. `tooltip` supplies its accessible name; Tab, Space/Return and `Accessible.pressAction` invoke the same enabled template click. Checkable state is exposed to accessibility; disabled controls are not accessible focus stops or actions. |

## Buttons and chips

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals | Size / look |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Button` | T.Button | `text`, `iconName`, `trailingIconName`, `variant` (`"default"`, `"accent"`, `"ghost"`, `"outline"`, `"danger"`), `small`, `large`, `busy`, `available` (capability; `enabled` is owned by the busy binding), `tooltip`, `checkable/checked` | `clicked` | 24px, padding-x `space.md`, radius sm, body font, icon 14 with `space.xs+2` gap. `busy` shows a `Spinner` in place of the icon and disables. `accent` = accent fill + `accentContrast` text; `danger` = danger fill; `ghost` = no fill/border at rest; `outline` = border only. Text is the accessible name and tooltip is its description; Tab, Space/Return and `Accessible.pressAction` share the enabled click path. Checkable state is exposed to accessibility. For a shared semantic action use `CommandButton`; see [commands.md](commands.md). |
| `CommandButton` | Item containing `Button` | `registry`, `shortcutMap`, `commandId`, `iconOnly`, `small`, `tooltip`; read-only `button`, `disabledReason`; `enabled` follows the binding | `triggered` | Toolbar-friendly presentation of one `CommandBinding`. Label, icon, enabled/checkable/checked state and reason follow the registry. The contained Button is the only activation surface; accepted click, keyboard or accessible press invokes the binding once, and `triggered` is emitted only when the registry accepts it. Icon-only use should supply `tooltip`; the label remains the accessible name. Internal button objectName `commandButtonAction`. |
| `Chip` | T.AbstractButton | `text`, `iconName`, `chevron`, `active` (alias of checked), `checkable`, `emphasis` (`"plain"`, `"tinted"`, `"solid"`), `rounded`, `small`, `tooltip` | `clicked` | The Sloom pill: 32px (small 28), radius 6, `chipBg/chipBorder`, hover `chipHover*`, label and icon in accent colour, 6px gap, padding-x 8. `rounded` is the 36px fully round island action (10px padding, transparent at rest, `panel` on hover, text colour until lit). `emphasis`: "tinted" = chipBg + controlActiveBorder, "solid" = controlActiveBg + controlActiveBorder; `active` lights the accent border. Text (or tooltip when text is empty) is the accessible name; tooltip is also the description. Tab, Space/Return and `Accessible.pressAction` share the enabled click path; checked state is exposed to accessibility. |
| `Segmented` | Item | `model` (strings or `{text, iconName, value, tooltip, enabled}`), `currentIndex`, `currentValue` (read), `small`, `stretch`, `tooltip`; `enabled_` (compatibility alias of `enabled`); `activate(index)` | `activated(index)` (user activation only) | one 24px (small 20) row of joined segments in a `controlBg` frame; selected segment `controlActiveBg`, seams between unselected neighbours. Accessible PageTabList with a name and selected PageTab entries. ←/→ moves selection and focus, skipping disabled entries. A model entry with `enabled: false` cannot receive focus or activate; disabling the control disables all entries. objectNames `segment_<index>` and `segmentFocus_<index>` per segment; the focus ring is visible on active focus. Each segment keeps at least `size.controlSm` width. `stretch: true` fills the width it is given; inside a Flex row also set `Tk.Flex.grow: 1` so it is given one. |
| `PillSwitcher` | Item | `model` (strings or `{text, iconName, value, tooltip, accent, objectName, enabled}`), `currentIndex`, `currentValue` (read), `small`, `showLabels`, `showActiveMarker`, `tooltip`; `itemAt(index)`, `activate(index)` | `activated(index, value)` (user selection changes only) | fully rounded top-bar page switcher; 28px high (24 small), optional icon-only presentation and active colour marker. ←/→ wraps; Home/End moves to the first/last enabled item. Accessible PageTabList with selected PageTab state. Disabled entries cannot focus or activate. Programmatic `currentIndex` assignment is silent. Item objectNames default to `pillSwitcherItem_<index>` and can be overridden per entry; focus rings use `pillSwitcherFocus_<index>` and follow active focus. |
| `ToolBar` | Box | `compact` (28 instead of 36), `gap`, `edge` (`"bottom"` border by default, `"top"`, `"none"`), `wrap`, `rowItem` (read-only: the row the items live in, for bars that create items at run time); default property `items` | — | horizontal Flex, `align: Center`, `space.sm` side padding, background `surface`. `wrap: true` flows the items onto further rows and grows the bar by whole rows, so a command is never hidden for want of width. |
| `ColorButton` | T.AbstractButton | `color`, `colors` (for the popover's swatches), `columns`, `small`, `ghost`, `tooltip` | `colorSelected(color)`, `customRequested()` | control height; a 16px swatch of `color` with a chevron. Click, Space and accessible press open a `Tk.Popover` holding `Tk.ColorSwatches`; disabled controls cannot open it. Picking sets `color` and emits `colorSelected`, while owner colour assignment is silent. "Custom…" emits `customRequested` for the host's picker. The tooltip supplies the accessible name; objectNames `colorButtonSwatch`, `colorButtonPopover`. |
| `ColorPicker` | Item | required `color` (host-provided sRGB preview), `tooltip`, `alphaEnabled`; read-only `previewColor`, normalized `hue` (0–1), `saturation`, `brightness`, `alpha`; `begin(part)`, `preview(color)`, `finish()`, `cancel()`, `nudgeHue(delta)` | `previewRequested(color)`, `editFinished(color)`, `editCanceled(restoredColor)` | saturation/brightness square, hue rail and optional alpha rail. Pointer drags preview continuously and finish once on release; Escape cancels the active pointer gesture. Arrow keys and accessible increase/decrease actions nudge hue cyclically, alpha or saturation/brightness. Hiding alpha removes its rail and its implicit height. The host owns canonical colour, profile conversion, edit transactions, undo and persistence: user actions never assign the host `color` property. This control presents sRGB values and does not perform managed display/profile conversion. objectNames `colorPickerSaturationValue`, `colorPickerSvMarker`, `colorPickerHue`, `colorPickerHueMarker`, `colorPickerAlpha`. |
| `ToolSeparator` | Item | `inset`, `margin` | — | 1px vertical rule inset 6px from the bar edges; an Item that stretches on the cross axis (not a Divider) so the inset is measured from the bar, not the row's content height. |
| `Badge` | Rectangle | `text`, `variant` (`"default"`, `"accent"`, `"danger"`, `"success"`, `"warning"`, `"info"`), `dot`, `tooltip` | — | 16px tall (`size.controlSm - space.sm`, so it scales), radius full, micro font, semibold; `dot` is an 8px status dot. |
| `ProgressBar` | T.ProgressBar | `value`, `from`, `to`, `indeterminate`, `thin`, `tooltip` | — | 3px track (`thin` 2px), accent bar, radius full; the indeterminate slide stays off when reduced motion zeroes the motion ladder. |
| `Spinner` | T.BusyIndicator | `size` (14), `running`, `color`, `tooltip` | — | rotating `loader-circle` arc in accent; stops (stays visible) under reduced motion. |

## Inputs

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `CheckBox` | T.CheckBox | `text`, `checked`, `checkState`, `tristate`, `small`, `tooltip` | `toggled` | 14px box (12 small), radius xs, accent fill when checked with `check` icon (`minus` when partially checked) in `accentContrast`; row height `size.controlSm` (small `size.toggleTargetSm`: 16px Compact, 48px Touch). Keyboard Space and accessible press use template click semantics. Accessible checked/focus state follows the control; partially checked state is described. Programmatic `checked`/`checkState` assignment does not emit `toggled`. |
| `Switch` | T.Switch | `text`, `checked`, `small`, `tooltip` | `toggled` | 26x14 track (22x12 small; derived from the icon sizes so it scales), radius full, accent when on; 10px (8px) knob; row height `size.controlSm` (small `size.toggleTargetSm`: 16px Compact, 48px Touch). Keyboard Space and accessible press use template click semantics. Accessible checked/focus state follows the control; programmatic `checked` assignment does not emit `toggled`. |
| `RadioButton` | T.RadioButton | `text`, `checked`, `small`, `tooltip` | `toggled` | 14px circle (12 small) with a half-size accent dot; row height `size.controlSm` (small `size.toggleTargetSm`: 16px Compact, 48px Touch). Keyboard Space and accessible press use template click semantics. Accessible checked/focus state follows the control; programmatic `checked` assignment does not emit `toggled`. |
| `TextField` | T.TextField | `placeholderText`, `text`, `iconName` (leading), `clearable`, `small`, `error`, `mono`, `tooltip`, `trailing`; inherited `readOnly` | `accepted`, `editingFinished`, `cleared` | 24px, `inputBg/inputBorder`, focus `inputFocusBorder` + ring, padding-x `space.sm+2`; placeholder muted at the *small* size (caption when `small`) so it lines up with typed text. `clearText()` clears and emits `cleared` only while enabled and writable; Escape and the clear button obey the same gate. Read-only state is included in the accessible description, combined with `Error` when both apply. The control *is* the text input; the clear button is objectName `fieldClear`. Interactive parts are direct children, never inside `background` (T.TextField keeps its background at z -1). |
| `SearchField` | TextField | + `debounce` (ms, default 150) | `searched(text)` | search icon leading, clearable, Escape clears; `searched` fires after the debounce following user edits, immediately on Enter, and with "" on clear. Programmatic `text` synchronization does not search. |
| `TextArea` | T.TextArea | `placeholderText`, `text`, `rows` (min visible rows, 3), `mono`, `error`, `tooltip`; inherited `readOnly`; read-only `minimumHeight` | `editingFinished` (focus loss) | wraps and grows with its text from `rows` lines. Editing updates public `text` immediately; there is no separate draft, commit, or Escape-cancel operation. `editingFinished` means the focused edit session ended; it is not a user-change notification and can follow owner assignment made while focused. The toolkit does not expose a cross-version user-only edit signal: Qt's inherited `textEdited` is available only since Qt 6.9, while QindaTK supports Qt 6.8. Read-only and disabled block user edits but still allow owner assignment. Read-only state is included in the accessible description, combined with `Error` when both apply. Place inside `Tk.Scroll` for long text. Default width 240. |
| `NumberField` | T.Control | `value`, `defaultValue` (reset target), `mixed`, `from`, `to`, `stepSize`, `decimals`, `prefix`, `suffix`, `label` (short inline label, e.g. "W"), `editable`, `scrub` (drag horizontally on the label/field to change), `scrubSensitivity` (units per px, default `stepSize`), `stepButtons` (show ▲▼ on hover/focus, always in Touch), `small`, `error`, `tooltip`, `live`; read-only `clampedValue` (shown number: `value` clamped to `from`..`to` and rounded to `decimals`), `editing`, `inputItem`, `inputInvalid` | `valueModified(value)` (user edits and explicit `reset()` only, with the clamped result), `editingFinished` | 24px; the host owns `value` and `mixed` and the field never assigns either (D-198): a user edit only emits `valueModified`, the display follows `value` when the host applies it and snaps back when it refuses, so `value: model.x` bindings survive clamping and edits; a field without a model echoes (`onValueModified: (v) => value = v`); mono digits; Ctrl+wheel steps; arrow keys and accessible SpinBox increase/decrease step, Shift ×10, Alt ×0.1; Escape reverts the typed draft. In Touch, two side-by-side 48×48 step targets stay visible; each has an accessible Button action. `reset()` reports the clamped `defaultValue`; assigning `value` remains silent. `mixed: true` shows a “Mixed” placeholder and is preserved by focus alone; a valid user commit on a mixed field always reports, and the host clears `mixed`. Invalid typed drafts remain visible, set `inputInvalid`, and can be reverted with Escape. `editable: false` and `enabled: false` block keyboard, scrub, wheel, and step edits. Scrub is disabled while the field is being edited so text selection works. objectNames `numberInput`, `numberMixedPlaceholder`, `numberLabel`, `numberStepUp`, `numberStepDown`. |
| `Slider` | T.Slider | `value`, `from`, `to`, `stepSize`, `showValue`, `decimals`, `suffix`, `small`, `tooltip` | `moved`, `valueModified(value)` | 2px track, 10px round handle (8 small), accent fill; `showValue` adds a mono readout right (it takes `rightPadding`). Its full control height follows `size.sliderTarget` (16px base) or `size.sliderTargetSm` (14px small); both scale with density and reach at least 48px in Touch, while the track and handle stay compact. Horizontal only. The accessible name uses `tooltip`, falling back to “Value”; its description follows the formatted value. Keyboard and assistive value changes emit `valueModified`, programmatic assignment does not. |
| `ComboBox` | T.ComboBox | `model`, `currentIndex`, `currentText`, `textRole`, `iconRole`, `editable`, `editText`, `acceptableInput`, `validator`, `small`, `placeholderText`, `tooltip` | `activated(index)`, `accepted()` | 24px on `inputBg`, chevron-down trailing; popup = Menu-styled list (`popoverBg`, `popoverBorder`, radius md), rows at least `size.menuItem` (22) and tall enough for scaled text; at most 12 rows, further limited by host space, then scroll; opens above when that gives it more room and remeasures if the control or a scrolling ancestor moves. The selected row exposes `Accessible.selected` and shows a check. Programmatic `currentIndex`/`editText` changes emit no user operation. Keyboard/pointer selection emits `activated`; an editable field emits `accepted` on Return/Enter only when its validator accepts the draft. The app owns adding a novel string to its model. A JS array of strings needs no roles. A non-editable combo draws its label elided at the right (`comboDisplayText`) over the input, which keeps the full text with hidden glyphs so `implicitWidth` always measures the full label and Grid/Flex tracks do not oscillate; editable mode is the plain text input. objectNames `comboPopup` on the popup, `comboDisplayText` on the elided label. |
| `ColorSwatches` | Item | `colors` (list of colour strings or `{name, color}`), `current`, `columns` (8), `customButton`, `swatchSize`; `selectIndex(index)` | `selected(color)`, `customRequested()` | a `Tk.Grid` of authored-size swatches (16px in Compact); each cell is at least `size.controlSm`, which resolves to 48px in Touch, while the swatch artwork remains centred at its authored size. The one equal to `current` is ringed in accent; tap, Space, Enter or accessible press on a focused swatch selects through one guarded action. Disabled swatches reject user actions; programmatic `current` changes are silent. Tooltips and accessible names carry the swatch names. objectNames `swatchGrid`, `swatch_<index>`, `swatchCustom`. |
| `ColorField` | T.Control | `color`, `showHex`, `small`, `error`, `readOnly`, `tooltip`; read-only `inputInvalid` | `colorEdited(color)`, `pickRequested()` | 16px swatch (checkerboard behind alpha, objectName `colorSwatch`) + a bare mono hex input inside the field's own frame (objectName `colorHex`; `#rgb`, `#rrggbb`, `#aarrggbb`, bare hex accepted). Invalid hex stays visible after Return or focus loss, sets `inputInvalid` and the danger border; Escape restores the committed colour. `applyHex(text)` is a silent programmatic update; only a changed valid user commit emits `colorEdited`. The swatch is a keyboard and accessible Button action; `readOnly` and `enabled: false` block edits and picker requests while preserving programmatic `color`/`applyHex` updates. The accessible text editor reports the current value and invalid recovery hint. |

## Command composition and typed inspectors

Dialogs and command palettes inside content that moves between native windows
can set `hostItem` to an item in that content. Their overlay then follows the
host item; popovers follow `anchorItem` automatically.

All command rows read enabled, checked, label and disabled-reason state from
`CommandRegistry` when a registry and command ID are supplied. Menu choices
use the same command invocation path. Editor controls receive an existing
`PropertyBinding`; they do not create or own document content. A rejected
edit cancels its preview, and an accepted user edit is one undo transaction.

| Type | Base | Main properties and actions | Internal objectNames and behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `SplitButton` | Item with two template buttons | `registry`, `shortcutMap`, `commandId`, `menuModel`, `small`, `tooltip`; `triggered()`, `openMenu()` | `splitPrimary`, `splitMenuButton`. Main segment invokes its command; arrow opens choices without invoking the main command. Escape returns focus to the arrow. |
| `MenuButton` | T.Button | `registry`, `shortcutMap`, `menuModel`, `selectedCommandId`, `showSelectedCommand`, `tooltip`; `choiceTriggered(commandId)`, `openMenu()` | Menu-only activation. Selection may show the bound label/icon; disabled choice state follows the registry. |
| `ToolShelf` | Item | `tools`, `activeToolId`, `temporaryToolId`, `orientation`, `tooltip`; `toolRequested`, `temporaryToolRequested`, `temporaryToolReleased`; `beginTemporaryTool`, `endTemporaryTool` | `tool_<id>`. Arrow keys walk enabled tools; temporary-tool requests are explicit and the host owns lasting tool state. |
| `CommandBar` | Item | `registry`, `shortcutMap`, `model`, `defaultOrder`, `customizedOrder`, `compact`, `tooltip`; `setOrder`, `resetOrder`, `orderChanged` | `commandSlot_<id>`, `propertyCommand_<id>`. Stable IDs drive user order. Property slots use `PropertyBinding` transactions. |
| `OverflowToolBar` | Item | `registry`, `shortcutMap`, `model`, `gap`, `compact`, `tooltip`; read-only `visibleIndices`, `overflowIndices`; `overflowChanged(commandIds)` | `measure_<id>`, `toolbarCommand_<id>`, `toolbarOverflowButton`, `overflowCommand_<id>`. Measures rendered labels, spills complete groups by priority and moves focus to the overflow menu when its focused command spills. |
| `Inspector` | Item | `properties` descriptors (`id`, `label`, `binding`, `editorType`, units/help), `filterText`, `tooltip`, `labelWidth` | `inspectorRow_<id>`. Numeric, vector and text editors share mixed/reset/error presentation and edit through supplied bindings. `FormGroup` supplies expandable grouping where needed. |
| `MixedValueField` | T.Control | `binding`, `label`, `helpText`, `mixedText`, `showReset`, `tooltip`; `beginEdit`, `updateEdit`, `commitEdit`, `cancelEdit`, `reset` | `mixedValueEditor`, `mixedValueState`, `mixedValueReset`. Mixed, unset, invalid and read-only states stay distinct; focus alone does not replace a mixed value. |
| `UnitField` | Item around NumberField | `binding`, `displayUnit`, `units`, `unitScale`, `unitOffset`, `precision`, `from`, `to`, `stepSize`, `tooltip`; `setUnit`, `edit`, `valueModified` | Canonical value is `display * unitScale + unitOffset`. Switching units changes presentation only. Invalid conversion metadata disables editing; commit uses the binding transaction. |
| `VectorField` | Item | `binding`, `componentLabels`, `componentUnits`, `precision`, `stepSize`, `tooltip`; `editComponent(index,value)` | `vectorComponent_<index>`. `PropertyBinding.updateComponent` patches each selected target independently, preserving untouched axes even when the whole selection is mixed, then commits one undo step. |
| `TransformEditor` | Item | `translationBinding`, `rotationBinding`, `scaleBinding`, `pivotBinding`, `boundsBinding`, `tooltip` | Composes typed rows through `UnitField` and `VectorField`. A host geometry adapter owns matrix decomposition and coordinate conventions. |
| `FormGroup` | Item | `title`, `collapsed`, `collapsible`, `validationItems`, `spacing`; `focusFirstInvalid()` | `formGroupHeader`, `formGroupBody`. Groups rows, reports invalid and pending counts separately, expands and focuses the first invalid editor. |
| `ExpressionField` | Item | `binding`, `localeService`, `symbols`, `units`, `draft`, `errorMessage`, `errorOffset`, `label`, `tooltip`; `previewDraft`, `commitDraft`; `previewed`, `expressionCommitted` | `expressionInput`. `ExpressionEvaluator` parses a bounded numeric/unit grammar with allowlisted functions and exact scoped symbol keys; commits a canonical value through one binding transaction. Invalid drafts retain a location and never mutate the document. |
| `FilePathField` | Item | `binding`, `dialogAdapter`, `uriValidator`, `mode`, `mimeTypes`, `draft`, `recentPaths`, `validationStatus`, `label`, `tooltip`; `setDraft`, `inspectDraft`, `commitDraft`, `browse`, `cancelBrowse`; `pathCommitted`, `openRequested`, `browseRequested` | `pathInput`, `pathBrowse`, `pathOpen`. Local file URIs are validated; remote availability is reported as unknown. Browse and validation completions are matched against request/draft/document revisions before a binding commit. Accepted browse URIs use `FileDialogAdapter.encodedUrls`, preserving percent escapes across Qt versions. |
| `TokenField` | Item | `binding`, `standalone`, `validator`, `placeholderText`, `completionProvider`, `duplicatePolicy` (`value`, `label`, `allow`), `tokens`, `draft`, `suggestions`, `label`, `tooltip`; `commitDraft`, `appendCandidates`, `removeAt`, `move`, `queryCompletion`; `tokenAdded`, `tokenRemoved`, `tokenReordered`, `tokensEdited(tokens)` | `tokenItem`, `tokenChip`, `tokenRemove`, `tokenInput`, `tokenSuggestion`. Tokens keep stable UUIDs across reorder, split pasted text into bounded candidates and commit the typed list through one binding transaction. Completion results are rejected after draft/edit/document revision changes; IME composition does not prematurely commit. A `standalone` field owns `tokens` itself and reports each edit as `tokensEdited(tokens)` instead of a binding transaction; `validator(label)` may return `{label, value, valid, error}` to mark invalid tokens (RichMailComposer recipients). Committing keeps focus in `tokenInput`. |

`PreferencesDialog` presents staged `SettingsModel` descriptors with
`preferencesSearch`, `preferencesCategories`, `preferencesSettings`,
`preferenceRow_<scopedKey>`, `preference_<scopedKey>`, and
`preferencesApply`/`preferencesCancel` objectNames. Escape, Cancel and popup
closure discard the stage; Return/Enter validates the focused editor before
applying. A rejected setting hidden by category or search filters is revealed
and focused.

## Application and document navigation

These controls present host-owned routes, commands and document sessions. Stable
IDs carry identity; displayed labels can change without moving the underlying
selection or saved tab order. `DocumentTabs` reads and mutates the authoritative
`DocumentManager` view order. A dirty last view needs a host close decision;
the control emits `closeRequested` and waits for `completeClose`.

| Type | Base | Main properties and actions | Internal objectNames and behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Ribbon` | Item | `tabs`, `contextIds`, `currentTabId`, `commandRegistry`, `shortcutMap`, `collapsed`, `keyTipsVisible`, `tooltip`, `mode` (`expanded`/`compact`), `stackRows` (2), `autoSqueeze`, `itemDelegate`, `trailing` (an Item placed at the right of the tab row); read-only `visibleTabs`, `currentTab`, `bandHeight`, `squeezeLevel` (0–3); `activateTab`, `activateItem`, `openItemMenu`, `keyTipFor`; `tabChanged`, `itemRequested`, `collapseToggled` | `ribbonTabRow`, `ribbonTab_<id>`, `ribbonTrailing`, `ribbonCollapseButton`, `ribbonGroupViewport`, `ribbonGroups`, `ribbonGroup_<id>`, `ribbonItem_<id>`, `ribbonAction_<id>`, `ribbonItemMenu`, `ribbonMenuItem_<id>`. Tabs declare groups by stable ID; a group lists `items` (columns: a `large` item is its own column, `small`/`icon`/component items stack `stackRows` high) or explicit `rows` (`[[…],[…]]`). Items are data: `{id, label, iconName, tooltip, shortcut, keyTip, size, checkable, checked, enabled, visible}` plus one of `action` (an Action-shaped QObject — `text`/`plainText`, `iconName`, `enabled`, `visible`, `checkable`, `checked`, optional `shortcutText`, `trigger()` — bound live), `commandId` (a `CommandButton` over `commandRegistry`) or `component` (a host Component); `menu: [item… | "|"]` adds a dropdown and `split: true` keeps the body a command. Contextual tabs appear only for matching context IDs and draw their label in the accent. Pointer and key-tip activation pass through the same authority (registry, then action). The ribbon never hides a command: groups squeeze one at a time from the right, just enough to fit — 0 (as declared), 1 (large items small), 2 (icon-only where an icon exists), every group reaching 1 before any reaches 2 — and 3 wraps the groups onto more lines; read-only `squeezePlan` (`{level, levels}`) holds the per-group levels and `squeezeLevel` the deepest one, planned from `RibbonGroup.widthAt()` (D-305). `compact` draws one row per group without captions. Clicking a tab while collapsed shows the band; double-clicking the current tab toggles collapse. Arrow keys switch tabs, Alt shows key tips. See D-297. |
| `RibbonButton` | T.AbstractButton | `size` (`large`/`small`/`icon`), `text`, `iconName`, `tooltip`, `shortcutText`, `hasMenu`, `split`, `bandHeight`, checkable/checked; read-only `largeWidth`, `smallWidth`, `iconWidth`, `plainLabel` | `menuRequested` | Large: 20px icon over a one-line caption (+ chevron), `bandHeight` tall; small: icon + label at `size.control`; icon: square. Widths per weight come from label metrics only (the squeeze plans with them). `&` mnemonics and a trailing "…"/"..." are stripped for display and the accessible name. With `split`, `ribbonButtonMenuArea` (the chevron half) emits `menuRequested` and never `clicked`. Checked uses `accentSubtle` with the active border. |
| `RibbonGroup` | Item | `groupData`, `ribbon`, `level`, `compact`; read-only `entries`, `arrangement`; `widthAt(level)`, `arrange(level)` | `ribbonGroupCard`, `ribbonGroupCaption`. A soft card (`panelAlt`, `radius.md`) with an Overline caption; items placed by one arrange() that both measures and positions, so planned and drawn widths agree and a level change never re-creates an item. |
| `RibbonSlot` | Item | `ribbon`, `groupId`, `itemData`, `weight`; read-only `action`, `label`, `iconName` (empty when the icon set lacks the name), `itemEnabled`, `content`; `widthFor(weight)` | `ribbonItem_<id>`. Chooses the presentation (component, `itemDelegate`, `CommandButton`, `RibbonButton`) and tells a host delegate what it draws through its `ribbonItem`, `ribbonWeight` and `ribbonGroupId` properties when it declares them. |
| `DocumentTabs` | Item | `documentManager`, `currentViewId`, read-only `activeDocumentId`, `entries`, `pendingDocuments`, `errorText`, `tooltip`; `refresh`, `requestClose`, `completeClose`, `closeOthers`, `requestReorder`; `viewActivated`, `closeRequested`, `closeFailed`, `reorderRequested` | `documentTabsViewport`, `documentTab_<viewId>`, `documentTabSelect_<viewId>`, `documentTabError`. Each session view gets a tab; activation calls `DocumentManager.activate`, and dragging calls its `moveView`. Closing one of several views removes only that view. Dirty last-view close remains pending for host confirmation; a blocked close clears pending state and reports its reason. Left/Right/Home/End focus and activate tabs. |
| `NavigationRail` | Item | `groups`, `currentId`, `expanded`, `expandedWidth`, `compactWidth`, `tooltip`; read-only `entries`; `activate(entry)`, `confirmRoute(id)`; `destinationRequested(id,destination)`, `routeReconciled(previousId,currentId)` | `navigationGroup_<id>`, `navigationItem_<id>`, `navigationButton_<id>`, `navigationIcon_<id>`. Groups contain route entries with stable IDs and optional icon, badge, `visible`, `allowed` and `enabled` states. Hidden or disallowed routes are omitted. Activation requests a host route change without selecting it; the host calls `confirmRoute` only after accepting navigation. Invalid restored, hidden or removed IDs fall back to the first enabled visible route (or empty), and `routeReconciled` lets the host update persisted state. Compact mode centers the icon and keeps accessible labels. Arrow/Home/End navigation skips disabled entries. |
| `BreadcrumbBar` | Item | `path`, `itemMaximumWidth`, `siblingParentId`, `siblingItems`, `siblingPending`, `siblingRequestId`, `tooltip`; `activate`, `showSiblings`, `supplySiblings(id,requestId,items)`; `navigateRequested(id)`, `siblingsRequested(id,requestId)` | `breadcrumbViewport`, `breadcrumbSegment_<id>`, `breadcrumbSelect_<id>`, `breadcrumbSiblings_<id>`, `breadcrumbSiblingMenu`. Path labels elide within a horizontal scroll viewport. Ancestors navigate by ID. A segment with inline `siblings` opens the menu immediately; a segment with `siblingProvider: true` shows the button and requests data. The host supplies a matching result with `supplySiblings`; stale requests and removed segments are ignored. |

## History, notifications and task feedback

`UndoService`, `NotificationModel` and `JobModel` own these views' state. See
[history-feedback.md](history-feedback.md) for identity, lifetime and retry
rules. Notification action IDs are requests to the host; it must recheck the
current command context. A task retry likewise asks the host to validate the
source and destination before it starts new work.

| Type | Base | Main properties and actions | Internal objectNames and behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `HistoryView` | Box | `undoService`, `listHeight`, read-only `historyRows`; `navigate(entryId)`, `goToHistoryIndex(index)` | `historyView`, `historyUndo`, `historyRedo`, `historyPosition`, `historyCleanState`, `historyEntries`, `historyRow_<entryId>`, `historyEntry_<entryId>`, `historyEmpty`, `historyLastError`. Shows the active document's transient undo history, applied steps and clean marker. Navigation delegates to guarded service operations; a retired branch ID cannot replay. |
| `NotificationCenter` | Box | `notificationModel`, `showRead`, `listHeight`; `count`, `markRead`, `dismissNotification`, `activate`; `actionRequested(notificationId,actionId)` | `notificationCenter`, `notificationCount`, `notificationList`, `notification_<id>`, `notificationNotice_<id>`, `notificationAction_<id>_<actionId>`, `notificationMarkRead_<id>`, `notificationEmpty`. Renders bounded model records with severity, source/time, read state, repeat count, dismiss and action controls. Repeated failures share one ID. Actions emit IDs and mark the record read. |
| `Toast` | FocusScope | `notificationModel`, `notificationId`, `title`, `message`, `variant`, `actionText`, `actionId`, `duration`, `remaining`, `paused`; `show`, `announce`, `presentNext`, `dismiss`, `activateAction`; `dismissed`, `actionRequested(actionId)` | `toast`, `toastNotice`, `toastAction`. `announce` posts through the bounded notification queue; expiration leaves the persistent notice intact. Hover or keyboard focus pauses the themed countdown; action and dismissal are keyboard accessible. The host resolves action IDs. |
| `TaskCenter` | Box | `jobModel`, `showFinished`, `expandedJobId`, `listHeight`; `cancelTask`, `requestRetry`, `openResult`; `cancelRequested(jobId)`, `retryRequested(jobId)`, `resultRequested(jobId,result)` | `taskCenter`, `taskCenterCounts`, `taskCenterClearFinished`, `taskCenterList`, `taskRow_<jobId>`, `taskProgress_<jobId>`, `taskDetail_<jobId>`, `taskCancel_<jobId>`, `taskRetry_<jobId>`, `taskOpenResult_<jobId>`, `taskCenterEmpty`. Enumerates job states and progress, allows cancellation, reports retry requests for failed work, and offers committed results. Closing this view never cancels a job. |
| `RenderQueueView` | Box | `jobs` (host-owned immutable snapshots), `listHeight`; `validateJob(jobId)`, `submitJob(jobId)`, `cancelJob(jobId)`, `retryJob(jobId)`, `openOutput(jobId,manifest)`; `validateRequested(jobId)`, `submitRequested(jobId)`, `cancelRequested(jobId)`, `retryRequested(jobId)`, `openOutputRequested(jobId,manifest)` | `renderQueueView`, `renderQueueList`, `renderJob_<jobId>`, `renderJobState_<jobId>`, `renderJobSource_<jobId>`, `renderJobOutput_<jobId>`, `renderProgress_<jobId>`, `renderJobDiagnostic_<jobId>`, `renderJobManifest_<jobId>`, action names prefixed `renderValidate_`, `renderSubmit_`, `renderCancel_`, `renderRetry_`, `renderOpenOutput_`, `renderQueueEmpty`. Rows carry `jobId`, `sourceSnapshot`, `sourceVersion`, `sourceRange`, `outputFormat`, `outputPath`, `state`, `progress`, `warning`, `error`, and `outputManifest`. State and progress come from the host snapshot; requests only emit stable IDs. Open output is available only for a succeeded job with a validated manifest, so queued work is never reported as written output. |

## Typography, calendar and format dialogs

`FontPicker` and `FontFeaturesEditor` use provider metadata rather than
guessing installed coverage or OpenType support. `FontCatalog` enumerates
installed families/styles and a fallback. It reads the resolved face's
OpenType `fvar`, `GSUB` and `GPOS` tables for variable-axis bounds and feature
tags. `checkGlyphCoverage(family, style, sampleText)` checks Unicode code points
against the selected face's cmap without font fallback; it reports missing
code points, not shaping, ligature, script or feature eligibility. Samples
over 4096 UTF-16 units and malformed Unicode report unavailable coverage.
Malformed tables report unavailable axis/feature metadata. All-day dates remain ISO date
strings; instants remain UTC ISO strings. `LocaleService` controls localized
presentation and classifies DST gaps and repeated local times before a
`DateTimeField` commits.

| Type | Base | Main properties and actions | Internal objectNames and behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `FontPicker` | Item | `binding`, `styleBinding`, `fontProvider`, `family`, `style`, `searchText`, `specimenText`, `includeMissingSelection`, `tooltip`; read-only `selectedFaceMetadata`; `requestSelection(family,style)`, `requestStyle(style)`; `fontSelectionRequested` | `fontSearch`, `fontFamilyPicker`, `fontStylePicker`, `fontSpecimen`, `fontSpecimenText`, `fontAvailabilityState`. Search filters installed families; the specimen renders the selected face or explicit fallback. A missing family retains its name. Family/style edits use bindings and a shared undo macro when both change. |
| `FontFeaturesEditor` | T.Control | `fontProvider` or explicit `fontMetadata`, `fontFamily`, `fontStyle`, `sampleText`, `axisBindings`, `featureBindings`, `tooltip`; read-only `effectiveFontMetadata`, `glyphCoverage`; `updateBinding` | `fontMetadataUnavailable`, `fontGlyphCoverage`, `fontAxis_<tag>`, `fontFeature_<tag>`, `fontFeatureReset_<tag>`. Actual face metadata supplies axes and feature tags. A host sample with `FontCatalog` shows face-level unsupported code points visibly and in the accessible description. Unsupported or unbound settings remain visible and disabled. A mixed feature uses the partial checkbox state; editing or resetting to the schema default is one binding transaction and one undo step. |
| `DateTimeField` | T.Control | `binding`, `localeService`, `dateOnly`, `dateFormat`, `dateTimeFormat`, inclusive canonical `minValue`/`maxValue`, `draft`, `resolutionState`, `validationError`, `resolutionCandidates`, `tooltip`; `commitDraft()`, `chooseCandidate(index)`, `clearValue()`, `cancelDraft()`; `valueModified` | `dateTimeInput`, `dateTimeCommit`, `dateTimeClear`, `dateTimeValidation`. Date-only mode commits an ISO date without time or zone; instant bounds are UTC ISO strings. Out-of-range entries and chosen DST candidates are rejected before the binding transaction. Clearing commits an empty string through the binding, so a host schema can reject it. For an ambiguous local time, the field shows both actual UTC candidates and requires a choice; a nonexistent local time reports an error and cannot commit. Escape restores the committed value. |
| `CalendarPicker` | Item | `localeService`, `selectedDate`, `minDate`, `maxDate`, `markedDates`, `rangeMode`, `rangeStart`, `rangeEnd`, `shownYear`, `shownMonth`, `tooltip`; `showDate`, `shiftMonth`, `activateCell`; `dateSelected`, `dateRangeSelected` | `calendarMonthLabel`, `calendarGrid`. `LocaleService.monthGrid` supplies calendar-native headings and stable ISO date IDs. Arrow keys move by day/week, PageUp/PageDown change month, Return selects; disabled dates cannot be selected. The calendar includes named weekday headers and day actions. |
| `ImportDialog` | T.Dialog | `formatRegistry`, `fileDialogAdapter`, `documentManager`, `sourceUrl`, `formatId`, `plan`, `jobId`, `allowLossy`; `refreshPlan()`, `startOrRetry()`, `cancelJob()`; `importAdopted(session)`, `operationFailed(message)` | `importDialog`, `importSourcePath`, `importChooseSource`, `importFormat`, `importLossAcknowledgement`, `importPreflightStatus`, `importWarning`, `importLoss`, `importPreview`, `importProgress`, `importFooter`, `importStart`. Shows only providers with an import operation, reviews the preflight report, passes approved options to the job and adopts only after success. Closing abandons the job identity and requests cancellation, so a late result cannot open a document. |
| `ExportDialog` | T.Dialog | `formatRegistry`, `fileDialogAdapter`, `documentSession` or `document`, `destinationUrl`, `formatId`, `conflictPolicy`, `allowLossy`, `allowExtensionMismatch`, UTF-8 options, `plan`, `jobId`; `refreshPlan()`, `startOrRetry()`, `cancelJob()`; `exportFinished(result)`, `operationFailed(message)` | `exportDialog`, `exportDestinationPath`, `exportChooseDestination`, `exportFormat`, `exportEntityId`, `exportLineEnding`, `exportBom`, `exportConflictPolicy`, `exportExtensionMismatch`, `exportLossAcknowledgement`, `exportPreflightStatus`, `exportWarning`, `exportLoss`, `exportPreview`, `exportProgress`, `exportFooter`, `exportStart`. Shows only export-capable providers; replacement requires a matching destination hash and losses require explicit acknowledgement. Export does not mark a session clean. |

See [format-registry.md](format-registry.md) for provider scope, preflight
receipts, job states and the difference between export and Save.

## Pen input

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `StylusHandler` | QQuickPointerDeviceHandler (C++) | `acceptedDevices` (default stylus + mouse; add `TouchScreen` — and `Finger` to `acceptedPointerTypes` — to ink with one finger), `acceptedPointerTypes` (pen, eraser, generic), `mousePressure` (0.6); read-only `active`, `position`, `pressure`, `tiltX`, `tiltY`, `rotation`, `pointerType` (`"pen"`, `"eraser"`, `"mouse"`, `"touch"`), `buttons` | `began(sample)`, `moved(sample)`, `ended(sample)`, `canceled` | The only way QtQuick hands a pen's pressure and tilt to an item: a pointer handler receiving the raw tablet event. `sample` is a `stylusSample` value (`position` in the parent item's coordinates, `scenePosition`, `pressure`, `tiltX/Y`, `rotation`, `pointerType`, `buttons`, `modifiers`, `synthesizedFromMouse`, `timestamp`); C++ consumers connect to the same signals with `QindaTK::StylusSample`. A mouse drag is the fallback (one sample stream, `mousePressure`); the mouse events Qt synthesises from tablet input are dropped so a stroke is never sampled twice; touch is ignored (a resting palm) unless opted in; then one finger inks with `mousePressure`, a second finger cancels the stroke (`canceled`) and hands the fingers on, and a finger's grab always approves takeover by a pinch or pan (D-304). Grabs the point exclusively for the gesture. |

## Structure and navigation

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TabStrip` | Item | `model` (strings or `{text, iconName, closable, tooltip, enabled}`, or a C++ `QStringList`/`QVariantList` property — sequence objects count by `length`, never `Array.isArray`), `currentIndex`, `count` (read), `style` (`"underline"`, `"pill"`), `closable`, `small`, `stretch` | `currentIndexChanged`, `tabClosed(index)`, `tabActivated(index)`; `tabItem(index)` returns a tab's delegate item | 22px tabs (`size.tab`), caption font, active tab text colour `text` + 2px accent underline (`underline`) or `controlActiveBg` (`pill`); hover `hover`; accessible PageTabList and selected PageTab states. Tabs take keyboard focus; ←/→ move selection and focus across enabled tabs, Home/End go to the first/last enabled tab. Disabled tabs and their close actions are inert. Programmatic `currentIndex` assignment is silent. objectNames `tab_<index>`, `tabClose_<index>`, `tabFocus_<index>`; the focus ring follows active focus. |
| `StatusBar` | Box | `gap`; default: fields | — | `size.statusBar` high (22px Compact; 48px control plus the top rule in Touch), `surface`, top border, horizontal Flex; fields stretch to the content box and centre themselves; use `Spacer` to push right. |
| `StatusField` | Item | `text`, `iconName`, `muted`, `tooltip`, `actionable` (default `true`), `hovered` (read) | `clicked` | one `size.statusBar` entry (22px Compact; actionable fields use `size.control` in Touch). Actionable fields use a template Button, accept pointer, Tab/Space/Return/Enter and accessible press through one guarded click signal, and show a themed focus ring. The accessible name is `text` (or `tooltip` for an icon-only field); `tooltip` is also its description and hover tooltip. Set `actionable: false` for a static StaticText with no button or Tab stop. objectName `statusAction` on the template button and `statusFocusRing` on its focus outline. |
| `PropertyRow` | Item | `label`, `labelWidth` (`size.labelWidth`), `hint`, `alignTop`, `dense`, `editorItem` (read); default: editor | — | inspector row: caption label left (muted), editor fills (a `Box`, so one editor is stretched); min height `size.row`; `hint` in caption below. objectNames `propertyLabel`, `propertyEditor`. |
| `PropertyGroup` | Item | `title`, `collapsible`, `collapsed`, `count`, `divider`, `spacing`; default: rows | — | `SectionHeader` + column Flex (gap xs) + optional bottom `Divider`. The header disclosure and group's externally assigned `collapsed` value stay synchronized; the group description reports expanded/collapsed state. objectNames `groupHeader`, `groupBody`. |
| `KeyValue` | Item | `key`, `value`, `mono`, `accent` | — | 22px readout row: caption key left, value right-aligned (mono, small size by default) — Sloom's "Sequence Info". objectNames `keyLabel`, `valueLabel`. |
| `ListRow` | T.ItemDelegate | `text`, `secondaryText`, `iconName`, `selected`, `active`, `small`, `indent`, `tooltip`; slots `leading`, `trailing` (+ `leadingItem`/`trailingItem` read) | `clicked`, `rightClicked(point)`, `doubleClicked` | 22px (28 with secondary text, 20 small), `hover`, selected `selection`, active text `accent` semibold; full-width. Tab focuses the row, Space and accessible press use the template click path, and owner `selected` assignment is silent. Accessible ListItem name uses tooltip or text; secondary text is its description. The focus ring is visible on active focus; disabled rows reject actions. objectNames `rowText`, `rowSecondary`, `rowFocusRing`. |
| `TreeRow` | ListRow | + `depth`, `expandable`, `expanded`, `editable`; read-only `editing`; `toggle()`, `activate()`, `startEditing()`, `cancelEditing()` | `clicked` (inherited from ListRow), `expansionToggled(expanded)`, `renamed(text)` | Accessible TreeItem named by `text`; enabled rows, including nonexpandable and noneditable leaves, join Tab order. Space/Return/Enter expand or collapse expandable rows; leaf activation uses the inherited `clicked` action, shared by pointer, keyboard and accessible press. F2 begins rename; an editable leaf's accessible activation starts editing. Focused rows paint `treeFocusRing`; disabled rows cannot focus or activate. Assigning `expanded` is silent; `toggle()` and activation emit `expansionToggled` for user-facing changes. Chevron (objectName `treeChevron`) at `depth * space.lg`; double-click toggles, or when editable starts inline rename: small `Tk.TextField` (objectName `treeEditor`) over the label, Enter emits `renamed(text)` only for non-empty changed text, Escape or focus loss cancels. The row never changes its own `text`. The signal is not `toggled(bool)`: AbstractButton already owns `toggled()` and QML refuses the override. |
| `Menu` | T.Menu | `title`, `minWidth` (160) | — | `popoverBg`, `popoverBorder`, radius md, padding `space.xs`, items 22px, submenu arrow, cascading, `popup()`; keyboard focus enters when opened and returns to the opener on Escape. Implicit width follows the widest item's natural label, icon, shortcut and arrow width, capped to the host window and anchor screen's available width; overlong labels elide but retain their full accessible name. Implicit height is capped to the anchor screen’s available work area through `menuWorkArea` (`Tk.ScreenWorkArea`), and selecting an offscreen row by keyboard scrolls it into view in the menu viewport. Wheel scrolling and the `Tk.ScrollBar` use that same viewport. |
| `MenuItem` | T.MenuItem | `text`, `shortcut` (display string), `iconName`, `checkable`, `checked`, `radio`, `danger`; optional command adapter: `commandRegistry`, `shortcutMap`, `commandId`, `commandBinding`, read-only `commandBound`; `tooltip` | `triggered` | 22px, small font, a `check` icon replaces the icon slot when checkable (a `circle-dot` when `radio`), shortcut in caption muted right-aligned at `space.xl` gap, chevron for a submenu. Accessible MenuItem name strips single `&` mnemonic markers and turns `&&` into a literal `&`; checked and enabled focusability track template state. Space/Return and `Accessible.pressAction` activate the same guarded template click path. Owner `checked` assignments do not emit `triggered`; only user activation does. With a nonempty `commandId` and registry, label/icon/shortcut/enabled/checkable/checked mirror the binding and `triggered` calls its guarded `trigger()`. `tooltip` supplies the hover tooltip; when bound, the disabled reason is the accessible description. Exclusivity of radio items is the owner's (an action model). objectName `menuItemText`. |
| `MenuSeparator` | T.MenuSeparator | — | — | 1px divider with `space.xs` margins. |
| `MenuBar` | T.MenuBar | — | — | 22px in Compact, `surface` with a bottom divider; accessible MenuBar role/name and MenuItem roles/names on entries; menu titles small font; open title `controlActiveBg`, hover `hover`. Children are `Tk.Menu { title }`. A `&` in a title or item text marks the keyboard mnemonic and is not displayed (`&&` shows one `&`); Alt+title mnemonic opens a menu and Alt+item mnemonic activates an item. |
| `Popover` | T.Popup | `anchorItem`, `placement` (`"bottom"`, `"top"`, `"left"`, `"right"`), `padding`, `arrow` (bool), `gap` | `opened`, `closed` | positioned next to `anchorItem` inside that item's current window Overlay, including after a native panel transfer; clamped to the window, `popoverBg`; its dimensions are capped to the overlay with `space.xs` margins, and oversized content scrolls on both axes through `contentItem` (`Tk.Scroll`, objectName `popoverBody`). The default child content remains declarative. While open, follows anchor position/size, scrolling on its ancestors, placement/gap/arrow changes, and overlay resizing. Also exposes `open()`/`close()`/`reposition()`. Non-modal; takes keyboard focus when opened, returns it to the opener when closed, and closes on Escape or a press outside. |
| `Dialog` | T.Dialog | `title`, `subtitle`, `dialogWidth` (`size.dialogWidth`, 420 compact; `width` is derived and clamped to the window), `primaryText`, `secondaryText`, `tertiaryText` (a third, left-aligned button), `primaryEnabled`, `destructive`; default: content; `header`/`footer` replaceable | `accepted`, `rejected`, `tertiary` | modal, dim scrim `overlay`, `popoverBg`, radius lg, header `Overline` title + caption subtitle; height is clamped to the host with a scrolling body and fixed header/footer. The body reserves width beside its overlay scrollbar so form controls remain visible; keyboard focus reveals body controls. Escape rejects, Enter accepts (when `primaryEnabled`), the primary button takes focus on open. The dialog body exposes a Dialog role named from `title`, or “Dialog” when empty. Disabled primary buttons reject keyboard and accessible press. `standardButtons` is deliberately unsupported (the footer is ours). objectNames `dialogHeader`, `dialogTitle`, `dialogBody`, `dialogFooter`, `dialogPrimary`, `dialogSecondary`, `dialogTertiary`. |
| `MessageDialog` | Dialog | + `text`, `variant` (`"info"`, `"question"`, `"warning"`, `"danger"`), `iconName` | — | a status icon beside wrapping text; Save / Cancel / Discard style questions use `primaryText`/`secondaryText`/`tertiaryText`. objectNames `messageIcon`, `messageText`. |
| `PromptDialog` | Dialog | + `label`, `text`, `placeholderText`, `acceptEmpty` (false); read-only `acceptable` | — | one line of text (a name); the field takes focus and selects on open; Enter accepts; a blank value greys OK and blocks Enter unless `acceptEmpty`. objectNames `promptLabel`, `promptField`. |
| `CommandPalette` | T.Popup | `commands` (`[{id, label, shortcut, section, iconName, keywords, enabled}]`; `enabled` defaults true), optional `registry` and `shortcutMap`; `placeholderText`, `maxVisible` (10), `filterText`, `rows`/`resultCount`/`currentRow` (read) | `activated(id)`, `closed` | modal, top-centred at 12% height, width min(560, window-32), height clamped to the host with a scrolling result list; case-insensitive substring filter over label+keywords; ↑↓ Enter Esc, click; sections as Overline rows in first-appearance order; an EmptyState when nothing matches. The commands list and command rows expose accessible roles, names and selected state. In direct mode rows come from `commands`; when `registry` is set, its visible command descriptors and live state replace that list. A supplied `shortcutMap` provides native-formatted shortcuts. Disabled commands stay visible but are skipped by navigation and reject click, keyboard and accessible activation. In registry mode activation first calls `registry.trigger(id)`; rejection keeps the palette open, accepted activation emits `activated(id)` and closes. In direct mode activation only emits `activated(id)`. Command model changes are silent. objectNames `paletteInput`, `paletteList`. See [commands.md](commands.md). |
| `Notice` | Box | `text`, `title`, `variant` (`"info"`, `"success"`, `"warning"`, `"danger"`), `iconName` (defaults per variant), `dismissible`; slot `actions` | `dismissed` | inline message bar with tinted subtle background (`accentSubtle`/`dangerSubtle`/15% status) and a 2px left edge + icon in the status colour; 22px for one line of text. objectNames `noticeTitle`, `noticeText`, `noticeDismiss`. |
| `Splitter` | T.SplitView | `orientation`, `handleSize` (`size.seam`), `tooltip` (optional accessible name for each handle) | — | seam `seam`/`seamHover` with a `size.seamTarget` cross-axis hit area (at least 48px in Touch), preserving pane geometry and native resize constraints/state. Each seam is a focusable `Accessible.Splitter`, named by `tooltip` or its preceding visible pane number; Left/Right or Up/Down and accessible increase/decrease resize the adjacent panes in `space.lg` steps within both panes' minimum/maximum constraints. The theme focus outline surrounds only the painted seam. objectNames `splitterHandle`, `splitterHitTarget`, `splitterFocusRing`; children use `SplitView.preferredWidth/minimumWidth/fillWidth` (import `QtQuick.Templates as T` → `T.SplitView.preferredWidth`, or `QtQuick.Controls as QQC`). |
| `Ruler` | Item | `orientation`, `origin` (px offset of 0), `pixelsPerUnit` (px per unit; not `scale`, which is Item's transform), `unit` (label), `majorEvery`, `minorEvery`, `labels` (bool), `cursorPosition` (px, -1 none), `minimumMinorSpacing` (`space.unit`, 4 compact), read-only `markerSize` (`size.rulerMarker`, 9 compact), `markers` (`[{id, position (px), kind: "indent-first"\|"indent-left"\|"indent-right"\|"tab", draggable}]`), read-only `tabInsertStep` (`space.unit`), `band` / `activeBand` (`[start, end]` px: the measured surface in `panelAlt`, its live part in `inputBg`; with a band set the ticks cover the band only) | `markerMoved(id, position)`, `tabAdded(position)`, `tabRemoved(id)` | Canvas-drawn ticks (`textMuted`), micro mono labels (rotated on a vertical ruler), cursor line in accent; minor ticks thin out below the minimum; repaints on theme and pixelsPerUnit changes; thickness `size.row` (22), default length `size.rulerLength` (200 compact). Markers are accent triangles (first-line hangs from the top edge, indents and tabs stand on the bottom edge); their painted `markerSize` is unchanged by hit expansion. In Touch, marker drag hit radius is `size.controlSm / 2` (24px), and overlapping targets resolve to the nearest marker (ties keep the first model entry). Draggable markers have stable per-id object/accessibility ids, a visible focus outline, Slider role, localized name, and a position description. Left/Right and accessible increase/decrease move by `space.unit` pixels through the same clamped `markerMoved` operation; the host writes the new position back without losing marker focus. Disabled rulers and `draggable: false` markers reject edits while owner marker assignment remains silent. Delete removes a focused draggable tab through `tabRemoved`; other marker kinds are not deleted. A focusable “Tab stop insertion position” Slider starts at the ruler midpoint; Left/Right and accessible increase/decrease adjust by `tabInsertStep`, while Enter and accessible press each emit one `tabAdded(position)`. Its themed position/focus indicator appears while focused. The owner still applies that request to `markers`; assignment does not emit a command. A double-click on the empty bottom half remains an additional insertion gesture; dragging a tab below the ruler removes it. objectNames `rulerBand`, `rulerActiveBand`, `rulerMarker_<id>`, `rulerMarkerArea`, `rulerTabInsertCommand`, `rulerTabInsertIndicator`. |
| `ZoomControl` | Item | `value` (1.0), `from`, `to`, `step`, `presets`; `zoomIn()`, `zoomOut()`, `reset()` | `valueModified(value)`, `resetRequested` | `−` IconButton, mono percent button (click, Space/Return or accessible press requests reset to 100%), `+` IconButton; steps walk `presets` when given, else add `step`; 24px; fits in an `Island`. Owner assignment to `value` is silent. Disabled controls ignore user methods and actions while still accepting owner value updates. `valueModified` reports only changed zoom; `resetRequested` reports an enabled reset command even when already at 100%. objectNames `zoomOut`, `zoomReadout`, `zoomIn`. |
| `EmptyState` | Item | `text`, `title`, `iconName`, `maxTextWidth` (320); default: actions | — | centred muted icon + overline title + wrapping caption, lg padding; for empty lists/panels. objectNames `emptyTitle`, `emptyText`. |
| `Card` | Box | `padding` (`space.md`), `selected`, `interactive`, `tooltip` (from Box) | `clicked` (from Box) | `panelAlt` background, 1px border, radius md; `selected` → accent border; interactive hover lifts the border to `controlHoverBorder` and tints the fill. Interactive cards inherit Box activation, naming and focus-ring behavior; passive cards have no role or tab stop. |

## Productivity workflows

These controls present host-owned snapshots and emit explicit request signals.
They do not store mail, interpret recurrence rules, mutate event/task snapshots,
or contact a provider. A host should correlate each request ID, apply or reject
the proposal, then republish accepted state. Error/offline status is shown next
to cached data so stale snapshots remain usable.

| Type | Host snapshot | Request signals and acknowledgment | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `MailView` | `messages` (`messageId`, `threadId`, `sender`, `subject`, `snippet`, `bodyPlain`, `bodyTrustMode`, `unread`, `flagged`, UTC `date`, `attachments` [{`id`, `name`, `mimeType`, `size`, `state` `remote`/`local`}]), `selectedMessageId`, `threaded`, `expandedThreadIds`, `syncState`, `offline`, `errorText`; read-only `selectedMessage`, `threads`, `rows`, `attachmentJobs` | `messageSelected(messageId)`, `refreshRequested(requestId)`, `actionRequested(requestId, messageId, action)` (`read`, `unread`, `flag`, `unflag`, `archive`, `delete`), `replyRequested(messageId)`, `attachmentRequested(requestId, messageId, attachmentId)`, `attachmentOpenRequested(messageId, attachmentId)`; `selectMessage`, `toggleThread`, `requestAction`, `requestAttachment`, `acknowledge(requestId, state, message)`, `acknowledgeAttachment(requestId, state, message)` | Conversations group by `threadId` (newest activity first, messages oldest first); a single-message conversation is one row, the conversation holding the selection is expanded. The reader lists attachments with their state (on server, loading, saved, failed); Download asks the host for that one attachment and each job is tracked by request ID, so a late answer only changes its own attachment, never the reader of another message. Saved attachments open through `attachmentOpenRequested`. Reader shows host-provided plain text and trust metadata; it does not render untrusted HTML. Cached messages remain readable offline; actions and downloads are rejected offline. `conflict` and `offline` acknowledgments show in `mailError`. objectNames `mailMessageList`, `mailThread_<threadId>`, `mailMessage_<id>`, `mailReader`, `mailReaderSender`, `mailReaderBody`, `mailAttachment_<id>`, `mailAttachmentState`, `mailAttachmentLoad`, `mailRefresh`, `mailReply`, `mailToggleRead`, `mailToggleFlag`, `mailArchive`, `mailDelete`, `mailError`, `mailRequestState`. |
| `RichMailComposer` | Host snapshot `draft` (`id` retained by host, `to`, `cc`, `bcc`, `subject`, plain `body`, `richDocumentJson`, `attachments`), editor buffer `workingDraft`, read-only `richDocument`, `toField`, `ccField`, `bccField`, presentation `title`, `headerVisible`, `actionsVisible`, `bccVisible` (shown automatically when a draft carries Bcc), `bodyRows`, `sendText` ("Send"), `recipientError`, `validDraft`, `attachmentJobs`, `online`, `status`, `statusMessage` | `draftChangeRequested(field, value)`, `saveDraftRequested(requestId, draft)`, `sendRequested(requestId, draft)`, `attachmentAddRequested(requestId)`, `attachmentRemoveRequested(requestId, attachmentId)`; `snapshot()`, `editDraft(field, value)`, `acknowledge(requestId, state, message)`, `requestAttachmentAdd()`, `requestAttachmentRemove(id)`, `acknowledgeAttachment(requestId, state, message, attachment)` | To and Cc are standalone `TokenField`s: each address is committed with comma or Enter and checked by `MailAddressValidator` (the RFC 5322 rules SmtpMailProvider sends with); invalid tokens are marked, named in `composerRecipientError` and block send, and the cursor stays in the recipient field. The draft keeps `to`/`cc` as strings (`Name <a@b>, c@d`), so existing records load. Text left in a recipient field is committed before save or send. Attachments change only through jobs: an accepted add appends the host's metadata, an accepted remove drops it, and a failed job leaves the draft untouched and shows its reason. The body uses `RichTextEditor` plus `FormatBar`; `snapshot()` includes plain text and the `qindatk.richtext/1` JSON. Send requires a valid recipient, a subject and an online host; failed sends keep the draft and its attachments. objectNames `composerTo`, `composerCc`, `composerRecipientError`, `composerSubject`, `composerFormatBar`, `composerBody`, `composerAttachments`, `composerAttach`, `composerAttachment_<id>`, `composerAttachmentRemove`, `composerAttachmentJob`, `composerStatus`, `composerSave`, `composerSend`. See [productivity-store.md](productivity-store.md#rich-draft-bodies). |
| `CalendarView` | `events` (`eventId`, `occurrenceId`, `title`, `startLocal`, `endLocal`, `zone`, `allDay`, `calendarId`, `recurrenceRule`, `overridden`), `rangeLabel`, `selectedEventId`, `selectedOccurrenceId`, `moveScope` (`occurrence`, `following`, `series`), `zone`, `offline`, `errorText`, `requestState`, `requestMessage` | `navigateRequested(requestId, direction)`, `eventSelected(eventId)`, `occurrenceSelected(eventId, occurrenceId)`, `eventChangeRequested(requestId, eventId, proposedChange)`; `proposeMove(row, direction)`, `proposeChange`, `select(row)`, `acknowledge(requestId, state, message)` | Agenda of host-projected rows with local wall time and zone as supplied. −/+ buttons propose `{kind: "shift-local", minutes, zone, scope, occurrenceId}`: an hour for timed rows, a day for all-day rows. A repeating row uses the `calendarMoveScope` choice (This event / This and following / All events); a single event always moves as `series`. Moved occurrences show a Moved badge. The view does not calculate DST; the store applies the move (see [productivity-store.md](productivity-store.md#calendar-edits)). objectNames `calendarAgenda`, `calendarEvent_<eventId>` (one per occurrence row), `calendarMoveScope`, `calendarMoveEarlier`, `calendarMoveLater`, `calendarPrevious`, `calendarNext`, `calendarRequestState`. |
| `CalendarGridView` | `events` (projection rows as `CalendarView` takes them, plus `location`, `readOnly`), `calendars` ([{`id`, `name`, `color`, `visible`}]; `color` is user data used as given, else the theme's `accent`, `success`, `warning`, `danger`, `info` in calendar order), `mode` (`day`, `workweek`, `week`, `month`), `anchorDate` (ISO date; falls back to the date of `now`), `weekStart` (1 Mon … 7 Sun, default `locale.firstDayOfWeek`), `workDays` (default [1..5]), `dayStartHour`/`dayEndHour` (working band, 8/18; the grid still scrolls 0–24 h and opens at `dayStartHour`), `hourHeight` (default `2 × size.row`), `now` (host local `yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm[:ss]`; the view never reads the clock), `selectedOccurrenceId`, `zone` (label only), `offline`, `readOnly`, `errorText`, `moveScope` (`occurrence`/`following`/`series`), `snapMinutes` (15), `createMinutes` (30), `use24Hour` (from `locale.timeFormat`), `locale` (Control), `tooltip`, `requestState`, `requestMessage`, `lastRequestId`; read-only `days`, `rangeStart`, `rangeEnd` (exclusive: the day after the last visible day; month = the 6-week grid), `title` (“September 2026”, “21 – 27 Sep 2026”), `visibleRange`, `todayDate`, `entries`, `orderedKeys`, `dragState`, `dragDistance` | `rangeChanged(rangeStart, rangeEnd)` (also once on completion; the host re-projects), `occurrenceSelected(eventId, occurrenceId)`, `openRequested(eventId, occurrenceId)` (double-click, Enter, accessible press), `createRequested(startLocal, endLocal, allDay)` (drag or double-click on empty time, snapped; a whole day in the all-day lane and month), `eventChangeRequested(requestId, eventId, proposedChange)` with `{kind: "shift-local" \| "resize-local", minutes, zone, scope, occurrenceId}`, `deleteRequested(eventId, occurrenceId)` (Delete), `moreRequested(dateIso)`; `navigate(direction)` (−1/+1 day, week or month; updates `anchorDate`), `goToday(todayIso)`, `setMode(mode)`, `select(row)`, `acknowledge(requestId, state, message)`, `proposeMove(row, minutes)`, `proposeResize(row, minutes)` (return the request ID or ""), `scrollToHour(hour)` | Outlook/Google-class time grid over host-projected rows (D-301). Day/week modes: a time gutter (locale 12/24 h), day headers (today in an accent circle), an all-day lane (all-day rows and timed rows of 24 h or more as bars, first-fit into lanes; beyond three lanes a “+k more” row), and a scrolling 0–24 h grid with hour rules, half-hour hairlines, the non-working hours and days in `panelAlt`, a wash on today and a `danger` now line. Overlapping timed events pack into side-by-side columns (interval-graph first-fit per overlap cluster, ordered by start, then longer first, then eventId, then occurrenceId; a piece widens into free columns on its right); a piece crossing midnight is cut per day. Blocks are tinted from the calendar colour (`mix(panel, colour, opacity.tint)`, `tintStrong` hovered/selected) with a solid left edge; they show title, time range and location as height allows, `repeat` for recurring rows, a Moved badge for `overridden`, `lock` for read-only rows; narrow pieces drop decoration and a sliver shows only its colour. Month: 6 × 7 cells (other-month days muted, today circled, “1 Oct” on month starts), all-day chips then timed chips (dot, time, title), as many as fit and a “+k more” link. Drag moves (timed: snapped minutes across days; all-day and month: whole days), drag of a timed block's bottom edge resizes (at least `snapMinutes`), drag on empty time creates; a ghost shows the proposal (and stays, muted, until `acknowledge` or the next `events`), Escape cancels, read-only rows, `readOnly` and `offline` refuse. Keyboard: one event is the Tab stop; Tab/Shift+Tab and arrows walk events in that order, Enter opens, Space selects, Delete asks to delete, Alt/Ctrl+Up/Down propose ∓`snapMinutes` (∓1 day for all-day rows), Alt/Ctrl+Left/Right ∓1 day, Alt/Ctrl+Shift+Up/Down resize; focus follows the occurrence when the host re-projects. Accessible: grid Table, day columns Column, headers ColumnHeader, month cells Cell, events Button named “Standup, Tuesday 22 September, 09:00 to 09:15” with calendar, location, repeats, moved and read-only in the description; tooltips via `Tk.ToolTip`. Internal parts (not for direct use): `CalendarGridTime`, `CalendarGridAllDay`, `CalendarGridMonth`, `CalendarGridEvent`, `CalendarGridMore`, `CalendarGridLayout.js`. objectNames `calendarGrid`, `calendarGridNotice`, `calendarGridHeader`, `calendarGridGutter`, `calendarGridAllDay`, `calendarGridScroll`, `calendarGridDay_<yyyy-MM-dd>`, `calendarGridEvent_<occurrenceId>` (each visible piece), `calendarGridMoved`, `calendarGridFocusRing`, `calendarGridMonthCell_<yyyy-MM-dd>`, `calendarGridMore_<yyyy-MM-dd>`, `calendarGridNowLine`, `calendarGridGhost`. Example: `examples/calendar-grid`; store mapping in [productivity-store.md](productivity-store.md#calendar-grid-requests). |
| `RecurrenceEditor` | `rule` (`frequency`, `interval`, `weekdays` 1–7, `endMode`, `count`, `until`, `exceptions`), `eventId`, `zone`, `startLocal`, read-only `skipNote`, `preview`, `previewState`, `requestState`, `requestMessage` | `ruleChangeRequested(requestId, eventId, proposedRule)`, `previewRequested(requestId, eventId, rule)`, `occurrenceExceptionRequested(requestId, eventId, occurrenceId, excluded)`; `acknowledge(requestId, state, message)` | `toggleWeekday(index)` proposes a copied rule; the count/until fields propose an explicit end condition; `requestPreview()` asks the host recurrence service. “Skip once” requests exclusion for a host-supplied occurrence ID; each entry of `rule.exceptions` is listed with Restore, which requests `excluded: false`. With `startLocal` set, `skipNote` explains skipped dates (monthly rules on the 29th–31st, yearly on 29 February). A host persists the requests with `LocalProductivityStore.setEventRecurrence`/`setOccurrenceExcluded` and republishes `rule`. Only host-supplied preview rows are rendered; the view never expands rules. objectNames `recurrenceFrequency`, `recurrenceInterval`, `recurrenceWeekday_<index>`, `recurrenceCount`, `recurrenceUntil`, `recurrencePreview`, `recurrenceExceptionCount`, `recurrenceSkipNote`, `recurrenceException`, `recurrenceRestore`, `recurrenceSkip`. |
| `RecurrenceService` | `error`; `expand(eventId, startLocal, durationSeconds, zoneId, rule, rangeStartUtc, rangeEndUtc, limit)`, `occurrenceAt(eventId, startLocal, durationSeconds, zoneId, rule, occurrenceLocal)`, `durationSeconds(startLocal, endLocal, zoneId)` | Returns bounded occurrence snapshots; `errorChanged` | Local wall-time recurrence with daily/weekly/monthly/yearly rules, exclusions, date-only all-day series (whole local days) and an explicit DST policy; month-end and 29 February rules skip dates that do not exist. It does not store or synchronize events. See [productivity.md](productivity.md). |
| `MailAddressValidator` | none | `parseOne(text)` → `{valid, name, address, display, error}`; `parseList(text)` → `{ok, error, recipients}` | Recipient parsing for `RichMailComposer` with the RFC 5322 rules `SmtpMailProvider` uses (`Mime::parseAddressList`); list splitting respects quoted display names. |
