| `TaskBoard` | `columns` (`status`, `label`, `tasks`: `taskId`, `title`, `assignee`, `dueDate`, `priority`, `syncState`), `conflicts` (provider entries {`collection`, `id`, `remoteFields`, `remoteDeleted`}), `offline`, `errorText`, `filterText`; read-only `taskConflicts`, `dragTaskId`, `dropStatus`, `dropIndex` | `moveRequested(requestId, taskId, fromStatus, toStatus, targetIndex)` (`-1` = end), `editRequested(requestId, taskId)`, `conflictResolutionRequested(requestId, taskId, choice)` (`local`/`remote`); `move`, `shift`, `resolveConflict`, `acknowledge(requestId, state, message)` | Host owns ordering and commits. Cards move within a column (up/down buttons, Ctrl+Up/Down on a focused card, or dragging onto a position) and between columns (labeled buttons, Ctrl+Left/Right, or dragging onto another column; the target column highlights). A card with `syncState: "conflict"` shows a Conflict badge; `conflicts` for tasks are listed with Keep mine / Use server. `conflict`, `offline` and `rejected` acknowledgments show in `taskRequestNotice`. `filterText` filters title/assignee. Offline proposals are rejected locally. objectNames `taskFilter`, `taskRequestNotice`, `taskConflicts`, `taskConflict_<id>`, `taskKeepLocal`, `taskUseRemote`, `taskColumn_<status>`, `taskCard_<id>`, `taskSyncState`, `taskMoveUp`, `taskMoveDown`, `taskRequestState`. |
| `LocalProductivityStore` | `filePath`, read-only `messages`, `drafts`, `events`, `tasks`, `revision`, `lastError`, `status` (`conflict` when the on-disk revision/content changed; `busy` when its lock times out), `recoveryAvailable`, `canUndoTaskMove`, `canUndoEventChange` | `upsert(collection, record)`, `remove(collection, id)`, `moveTask(taskId, toStatus, targetOrder)`, `undoTaskMove()`, `shiftEventLocal(eventId, minutes, scope, occurrenceId)`, `setEventRecurrence(eventId, rule)`, `setOccurrenceExcluded(eventId, occurrenceId, excluded)`, `setOccurrenceOverride(eventId, occurrenceId, fields)`, `undoEventChange()`, `load()`/`save()` or `loadFrom(path)`/`saveTo(path)` | Offline-first JSON snapshot with stable IDs, attachment metadata, recurrence rules retained verbatim, atomic replacement, previous-snapshot recovery and per-file save locking. A stale writer is rejected without changing primary or recovery files; reload, reapply/merge and save to retry. Task moves and calendar edits have bounded undo; moves take an explicit scope (one occurrence as an override, this and following as a split series, or the whole series), all-day events move by whole days, and moves reject DST gaps and folds. Delivery and remote sync are the providers' job; recurrence evaluation is `RecurrenceService`. See `docs/productivity-store.md`. |
| `LocalAttachmentStore` | `rootPath`, `defaultPath`, `status`, `lastError`, `maximumBytes` (8 MiB) | `importFile(id, sourcePath)`, `importBytes(id, name, mimeType, bytes)`, `read(id)`, `remove(id)` | Stores attachment payload bytes separately from `LocalProductivityStore` draft metadata. IDs map to SHA-256 directory names; staged directory replacement keeps payload and metadata together. Results include `ok` and `error`; `read` returns bytes and base64. Local only; no upload. |
| `LocalCalendarProjectionModel` | `store`, `recurrenceService`, read-only `occurrences`, `rangeStartUtc`, `rangeEndUtc`, `error` | `project(rangeStartUtc, rangeEndUtc, limit)` | Projects stored event snapshots and weekly/daily/monthly/yearly recurrence rules through `RecurrenceService` into a UTC range. Output rows preserve event metadata and include stable `eventId`/`occurrenceId`, exact local wall-time fields, zone and UTC bounds. Reprojects when the store emits `dataChanged`. Occurrences with an `overrides` entry take its fields and times and carry `overridden`/`recurrenceId` under their original `occurrenceId`; all-day rows span whole local days. This local projection does not sync or edit events. |

The `examples/local-productivity` fixture puts the five views over one local
store and routes every request through `ProductivityHost.qml` to the store,
`LocalAttachmentStore` and the SMTP, IMAP and CalDAV providers. With no
account configured, refresh and send reach the providers and are rejected by
them; `tst_productivity_seam` runs the same file against local test servers
(see [productivity-providers.md](productivity-providers.md#wiring-the-views)).
`examples/productivity-suite` continues to show provider-independent request
snapshots.

`examples/local-attachment-store` demonstrates a stable draft attachment ID
whose payload bytes are stored and read back by `LocalAttachmentStore`.

## Local database, forms and reports

`Tk.LocalDatabaseProvider` is a local SQLite adapter used by host applications.
`open(path)` opens/creates a local file; `openMemory()` creates a process-local
fixture. `tables` and `schema(tableName)` expose table, field, primary-key and
foreign-key metadata. `query(sql, bindings)` prepares exactly one read-only SQL
statement and binds each `?` value, returning `{ok, columns, rows, truncated}`
with a 5000-row cap. `execute(sql, bindings)` permits a single prepared
non-schema write. Single schema changes go through `previewMigration(sql)` and
an explicit `executeMigration(sql)`. Ordered sets of single-statement steps
use `previewMigrationPlan(statements)` and `executeMigrationPlan(statements)`;
the preview executes inside a SQLite transaction and rolls it all back,
while the explicit commit applies all steps atomically. Each step rejects
multiple SQL operations. These APIs do not provide a remote connection,
general-purpose transaction manager, permission policy, or query planner.

Designs and report runs are C++ models ([forms and reports](forms-reports.md)):
`Tk.LocalDataDesignDocument` is the form/report design as a session document
(schema `qindatk.local-data-design.v1`) whose edits are undoable commands, and
`Tk.LocalReportDocumentModel.build(provider, report, parameters)` evaluates a
report definition (bands, up to three group levels, WP-029 totals, filters and
typed parameters) into one stable paginated `document`, keeping the last good
document and reporting `errorText`/`errorStage` when a run fails.

| Type | Host snapshot | Requests / methods | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `FormDesigner` | `provider`, `designDocument` (`Tk.LocalDataDesignDocument`), `tableName`, `recordId`, `record`, `design` (`title`, `fields` with `fieldName`, `label`, `hidden`), `mode` (`design`, `preview`, `edit`), `displayFields` (read-only) | `designChangeRequested(fieldName, value)` (`{label}`, `{index}`, `{hidden}` or `{title}` when no document is bound), `designSaveRequested(requestId, design)`, `recordSaveRequested(requestId, tableName, recordId, changes)`; `acknowledge(requestId, state, message)`, `setFieldValue`, `validateRecord`, `requestRecordSave`, `updateLabel`, `moveField`, `setShown`, `updateTitle` | Fields come from provider schema; required values are checked from SQLite nullability. Edits are held as a working record and sent to the host as one change map; the control does not write the database. Record values change and `recordSaveRequested` fires only in edit mode: in design or preview mode `setFieldValue` and `requestRecordSave` return false with `requestState` `refused`. A blank entry is null, a number column refuses text (and an INTEGER column a fraction) with `requestState` `invalid`, and a blank required field fails the save. With `designDocument`, the form is the document's form in form order (hidden fields only in design mode), and every label, order, shown and title edit is one undoable document command, with undo/redo buttons in the header ([forms and reports](forms-reports.md#designers)). Labels form one column (the `size.labelWidth` basis) so values line up; the column type (`formType_<name>`, "TEXT · REQUIRED") shows only in design mode, and the status line (`formStatus`) is empty while idle. objectNames `formField_<name>`, `formLabel_<name>`, `formType_<name>`, `formValue_<name>`, `formBool_<name>`, `formDesignLabel_<name>`, `formShown_<name>`, `formMoveUp_<name>`, `formMoveDown_<name>`, `formTitle`, `formUndo`, `formRedo`, `formSaveDesign`, `formSaveRecord`, `formStatus`. |
| `DatabaseSchemaView` | `provider`, `selectedTable`, `migrationSql`, optional ordered `migrationStatements`, `schemaSnapshot`, `migrationPlan`, `requestState` | `migrationPreviewRequested(requestId, plan)`, `migrationCommitRequested(requestId, plan)`; `requestPreview`, `requestCommit`, `acknowledge` | Shows columns, types, nullability, primary keys, defaults and foreign-key relationships from SQLite introspection. Preview validates a single CREATE/ALTER/DROP step or an ordered plan without leaving schema changes behind. “Request apply” only emits a host request; the host explicitly calls `executeMigration` or `executeMigrationPlan`. The header badge (`schemaDatabase`) names the database file, with the full path as its tooltip. objectNames `schemaDatabase`, `schemaTablePicker`, `schemaField_<name>`, `schemaRelation_<id>_<sequence>`, `schemaMigrationSql`, `schemaPreviewMigration`, `schemaRequestMigration`, `schemaPlanRelation_<added|removed>_<index>` (the foreign keys a previewed plan adds or removes, from `planRelationships`) and `schemaMigrationFailed` (a failed or rejected commit; the view re-reads the provider on every acknowledgement and never patches `schemaSnapshot` from a plan). |
| `ReportDesigner` | `provider`, `designDocument`, `tableName`, `filterField`, `filterValue`, `groupField`, `sumField`, `totalFunction` (`sum`, `average`, `minimum`, `maximum`, `countValues`), `pageBreakPerGroup`, `showDetail`, `pageSize`, `headerText`, `footerText`, `pageHeaderText`, `pageFooterText` (running page bands; `{page}` and `{pages}` expand, default "Page {page} of {pages}"), `previewRows`, `previewColumns`, `reportState` | `previewRequested(requestId, querySpec)`, `exportRequested(requestId, reportDesign)`; `runPreview`, `requestExport`, `applyText`, `applyGroup`, `applyDetail`, `applyFilter` | The quick preview builds identifier-checked, parameterized reads from the selected schema, including grouped counts and the chosen aggregate over a declared numeric field, limited to 1–500 rows. With `designDocument`, the panel edits the document's report (header, footer, page header and footer, first group level with its total and page break, detail rows, an equality filter as a typed WP-029 parameter) as undoable commands and follows undo/redo; the export request carries `designDocument.report`. Hosts pass the export definition to `LocalReportDocumentModel`, which evaluates it through the query engine into a paginated `RichTextDocument` shared by preview, PDF and printers ([forms and reports](forms-reports.md)). objectNames `reportRunPreview`, `reportRequestExport`, `reportUndo`, `reportRedo`, `reportTable`, `reportFilterField`, `reportFilterValue`, `reportGroupField`, `reportSumField`, `reportTotalFunction`, `reportPageBreak`, `reportDetail`, `reportPageSize`, `reportHeader`, `reportFooter`, `reportPageHeader`, `reportPageFooter`. |
| `LocalDataDesignStore` | `defaultPath` | `saveToFile(path, design, provider)`, `loadFromFile(path, provider)`, `validate(design, provider)` | Atomic JSON codec `qindatk.local-data-design/1`. Stable bindings store SQLite provider/schema/table and field names; save/reopen validate bindings against live provider introspection when supplied. Read/write bound is 1 MiB. |
| `LocalReportDocumentModel` | `document`, `rowCount`, `status`, `defaultPdfPath` | `build(provider, reportDesign)`, `exportPdf(path)` | Resolves report table/fields against the live schema, performs a prepared query capped at 2000 output rows, constructs one paragraph per row in `RichTextDocument`, then exports paginated PDF through `RichTextDocument.exportPdf`. This local path does not send jobs to a physical printer. |

[`examples/forms-schema-report`](../examples/forms-schema-report/Main.qml)
opens an in-memory SQLite database, edits an actual row through bound values,
introspects its relationships, atomically saves/reopens a form/report design
and checks its stable schema binding, previews report rows, and builds a
paginated `RichTextDocument` PDF. Sample records remain in memory; the design
file and exported PDF are written locally.

## Asset-backed media adapters

These controls own one `Tk.AssetProvider` subscription each. They request only
when `active` is true and the provider/resource ID are set. Changing the
provider, resource, options, document, view ID, or active state releases the
old subscription before starting another. Destruction releases the current
subscription; virtualized delegates that are pooled should set `active: false`
or update their identity while they are not displaying a row. `refresh()`
explicitly retries. A backend error remains visible in the control's state and
accessible description; adapters never draw placeholder data as a successful
asset.

| Type | Properties | Read-only properties / actions | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AssetThumbnail` | `provider`, `resourceId`, `requestKind` (`"thumbnail"`), `requestOptions`, `document`, `viewId`, `active`, `caption`, `crop`, `selected`, `tooltip` | `subscriptionId`, `assetState` (`idle`, `loading`, `ready`, `failed`, `missing`, `stale`, or `cancelled`), `assetData`, `assetError`; `refresh()` | Requests through `AssetProvider.requestAsset`. By default the request size uses the thumbnail's nominal theme size; `requestOptions.size` overrides it. On success, `assetData.previewUrl` becomes the `Tk.Thumbnail.source`; while pending, missing, or failed the normal placeholder/error glyph is shown. `source` and `placeholderIcon` are provider-owned. `active: false` releases the subscription, which is useful when a recycled delegate leaves the visible set. The subscription retains the provider that created it so replacing `provider` releases it from the original owner. objectNames `assetThumbnail`, `thumbnailImage`, `thumbnailPlaceholder`. |
| `AssetWaveform` | `provider`, `resourceId`, `requestOptions`, `columns` (0 uses the displayed width), `document`, `viewId`, `active`, `color`, `showCentreLine`, `tooltip` | `subscriptionId`, `assetState`, `assetData`, `assetError`, `peaks`; `refresh()` | Requests kind `"waveform"` and passes returned `assetData.peaks` to `Tk.Waveform`. The backend reduces peaks to the requested number of columns; width changes coalesce through `Theme.motion.fast`. Empty, missing, cancelled, or failed results remain visibly empty and report their state; no sample peaks are invented. The subscription retains the provider that created it so replacing `provider` releases it from the original owner. objectNames `assetWaveform`, `assetWaveformDisplay`, `assetWaveformStatus`. |
| `AssetFilmstrip` | `provider`, `resourceId`, `headTimeTicks` (`"0"`), `tailTimeTicks` (empty means unknown and inactive), `rateNumerator`/`rateDenominator` (decimal strings), `document`, `viewId`, `active`, `crop`, `frameSize`, `tooltip` | `assetState`, `assetError` | Requests `"frame"` assets for the supplied head and tail times. The tail request stays inactive until `tailTimeTicks` is supplied. The default file backend uses installed FFmpeg tools for local video endpoint frames; a host can inject a streaming decoder. It never substitutes fabricated frames. objectNames `assetFilmstrip`, `assetFilmstripFrames`, `assetFilmstripHead`, `assetFilmstripTail`. |

For PDF pages, `Tk.AssetThumbnail` can request `"pdf-page"` with zero-based
`requestOptions.pageIndex` and `rotation` (0/90/180/270). The default file
backend returns a real PNG through Poppler's installed command-line tools.
`"pdf-text"` for the same page returns extracted words with point-space
rectangles for a host search/selection adapter.

All three controls use `Tk.AssetProvider` and `Tk.ResourceResolver`; register a
logical resource ID before requesting it. `document` and `viewId` scope
subscriptions to a live editing context. `invalidateView(viewId)` makes those
subscriptions stale, so the owner can refresh them when the view context is
rebuilt.

## Audio editing surfaces

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `PitchEditor` | T.Control | `sourceId`, `channelId`, `analysisSnapshot` (host-supplied segments, notes, contour, confidence and exact sample boundaries), `snapshotRevision`, `selectedSegmentId`, `selectedNoteId`, `operation` (`pitch`, `time`, `formant`), `editAcknowledgement`, `editingEnabled`, `statusText`, `tooltip` | `selectionRequested`, `editPreviewRequested`, `editCommitRequested`, `gestureCanceled`, `zoomRequested`, `scrollRequested` | Renders supplied pitch cents, voiced contour and note regions. Drag a note to send a preview and release to request commit; Escape cancels. Time targets come from host-supplied sample boundary strings. Pitch and formant target units/ranges come from snapshot metadata. The host owns analysis, correction constraints, DSP, persistent parameters, rendered audio, cancellation and undo; the toolkit host is `AudioPitchSource` (YIN analysis, TD-PSOLA `pitch` source edits with previews and undo, docs/audio-pitch.md): bind `analysisSnapshot: pitch.snapshot`, `editingEnabled: pitch.current` and forward the three request signals to `previewEdit`/`commitEdit`/`cancelEdit`. The view itself detects nothing and applies nothing. objectNames `pitchEditor`, `pitchContourCanvas`, `pitchEditorStatus`. |

These controls present host-provided audio analysis and report exact sample
selection/edit requests. The host remains the authority for source samples,
index conversion, DSP, cancellation, and accepted state. Waveform columns carry
exact decimal-string boundaries alongside reduced extrema; spectrogram tiles
carry normalized time extents and frequency bounds with the provider's declared
FFT metadata. The reference providers are `AudioWaveformSource` and
`AudioSpectrogramSource`, and edits go to `AudioSession.edits`
([audio-editing.md](audio-editing.md)).

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `WaveformEditor` | T.Control | `sourceId`, `channelId`, `columns` (`minimum`, `maximum`, `startSample`, `endSample`), `channelLayout` (`[{id, label}]`, one band each), `channelColumns` (one columns list per band), `selectionChannelId` (host: the committed selection's channel, `"all"`/empty for every channel), `regions` (stable `id`, exact sample range, label), `rulerMarks`, `selectionStartSample`, `selectionEndSample`, `gainDb` (−96…+48), `fadeShape` (`linear`/`equalPower`), `gainDbPerPixel`, `editingEnabled`, `statusText`, `tooltip` | `selectionPreviewRequested(gestureId, sourceId, channelId, startSample, endSample)`, `selectionCommitted(...)`, `gestureCanceled(gestureId)`, `editRequested(gestureId, sourceId, channelId, operation, startSample, endSample, parameters)`, `zoomRequested(anchorSample, factor)`, `scrollRequested(fractionDelta)` | Pointer drag previews exact decimal sample bounds from the host-provided column boundaries; release commits a selection request, Escape cancels. With a `channelLayout` each channel gets a band and a drag inside one band selects that channel (across bands `"all"`). The committed host selection is shaded (exact boundary strings, else linear between the first and last boundary) and carries handles: the gain handle (drag up/down, `gainDbPerPixel`) shows and sets `gainDb` and requests `gain` with `{gainDb}` on release; the fade-in/fade-out handles drag inwards, preview the curve and request `fadeIn`/`fadeOut` from the selection edge to the nearest exact boundary with `{shape}`. Keys: F / Shift+F fade the whole selection, E switches the fade curve, Up/Down (Shift: 0.1 dB) set the gain, G applies it (never at 0 dB), S / T / Delete (or Backspace) request `silence` / `crop` / `delete` (`requestEdit(operation, parameters)`); none alter the selection. Columns fewer than half the pixel width are joined as a line (sample zoom). Ctrl+wheel requests zoom and wheel/arrows request scrolling. objectNames `waveformEditor`, `waveformSelectionCanvas`, `waveformSelectionDrag`, `waveformGainHandle`, `waveformGainText`, `waveformGainDrag`, `waveformFadeInHandle`, `waveformFadeOutHandle`. |
| `SpectrogramView` | T.Control | `sourceId`, `channelId`, `tiles` (`x0`, `x1`, `lowHz`, `highHz`, `intensity`), `sampleBoundaries` (exact decimal strings), `minimumHz`, `maximumHz`, `frequencyScale` (`linear` or `log`), `fftSize`, `hopSize`, `fftWindow`, `fftNormalization`, `selectionMode` (`rectangle`, `lasso`, `paint`), `brushRadius`, committed `selectionStartSample`/`selectionEndSample`/`selectionLowHz`/`selectionHighHz` (`hasSelection`), committed `maskPolygons` (`[[{sample, hz}]]`, `hasMask`), `statusText`, `tooltip` | `selectionPreviewRequested(gestureId, sourceId, channelId, startSample, endSample, lowHz, highHz)`, `selectionCommitted(...)`, `maskCommitted(gestureId, sourceId, channelId, polygons, startSample, endSample, lowHz, highHz)`, `gestureCanceled(gestureId)`, `processRequested(gestureId, sourceId, channelId, operation, startSample, endSample, lowHz, highHz, parameters)`, `zoomRequested(anchorSample, anchorHz, factor)`, `scrollRequested(timeFraction, frequencyFraction)` | Draws supplied bounded time/frequency tiles and exact sample boundaries; frequency coordinates follow the declared linear/log axis. R/L/B pick rectangle, lasso or paint: a lasso commits one polygon, a paint stroke adds one octagon per dab to the committed mask (`maskCommitted`, exact sample strings past 2^53, at most 4096 points). The committed region (`spectrogramSelection`) and mask are placed from sample/Hz coordinates at any zoom; Delete/Backspace (`requestProcess("spectralGain")`) requests processing of the mask (`parameters.mask`) or else the region. Selection, processing and navigation are requests to the host. A draw cap limits each paint to 12,000 supplied tiles; FFT, normalization, processing, tile generation, and accepted region state belong to the host. objectNames `spectrogramView`, `spectrogramTileCanvas`, `spectrogramSelection`. |
| `AudioWaveformSource` | QObject (C++) | `session`, `resourceId`, `channel` (−1 all), `startSample`/`endSample` (view range; empty end = source end), `columnCount`; `columns`, `state` (`empty`/`loading`/`ready`/`failed`/`canceled`), `error`, `revision`, `frames`, `sampleRate`, `channels`; `zoom(anchorSample, factor)`, `scroll(fraction)`, `showAll()`, `cancel()`, `refresh()`, `extrema(start, end)` | — | Waveform provider over `session.edits`: an exact min/max pyramid built once per source revision on a worker thread (D-135); columns at exact sample boundaries down to one sample per column; a source edit sets `loading` at once and recomputes. |
| `AudioSpectrogramSource` | QObject (C++) | the `AudioWaveformSource` range properties plus `fftSize`, `hopSize`, `rows`, `minimumHz`, `maximumHz`, `frequencyScale`, `floorDb`, `maxFrames`; `tiles`, `sampleBoundaries`, `resolvedMaximumHz`, `effectiveHop`, `stride`, `window` (`Hann`), `normalization`, `cachedTiles`, `cacheBytes`; `cell(column, row)`, `rowForFrequency(hz)` | — | Spectrogram provider (D-136): centred periodic-Hann frames, amplitude dBFS, ≤ 12 000 cells per request, strided hop for wide ranges, 32 MiB tile cache, worker-thread FFTs; a setting change or `cancel()` abandons the running request and a superseded result is never published. |
| `AudioPitchSource` | QObject (C++) | the `AudioWaveformSource` range properties plus `minimumHz`, `maximumHz`, `voicingThreshold`, `correctionAmount`, `pitchSnapCents`; `snapshot` (PitchEditor's `analysisSnapshot`), `current`, `holding`, `noteCount`, `analysisRuns`, `detector` (`YIN`), `corrector` (`TD-PSOLA`); `frameAt(sample)`, `noteAt(sample)`, `notes()`, `editFor(…)`, `previewEdit`/`commitEdit`/`cancelEdit` (PitchEditor's request arguments), `writeTestSignal(file, notes, rate)` | — | Pitch provider (D-194…D-197, docs/audio-pitch.md): YIN once per committed buffer on a worker; holds its analysis while a preview is ready; stale at once on any committed change and refuses edits until re-analysed; edits are `pitch` source edits (TD-PSOLA) with one undo step each. |
| `AudioSourceEditModel` | QObject (C++, `session.edits`) | `canUndo`, `canRedo`, `undoText`, `redoText`, `historyCount`, `previewRequestId`, `previewState`, `previewResourceId`, `lastError`; `apply(resourceId, edit)`, `preview(requestId, resourceId, edit)`, `commitPreview`, `cancelPreview`, `undo`, `redo`, `edits`, `capabilities`, `problems`, `revision`, `frames`, `channels`, `sampleRate` | `sourceChanged(resourceId, revision)`, `previewReady/previewFailed/previewCanceled(requestId, …)` | Sample edits of every session source (D-134, D-137): `gain`, `fadeIn`, `fadeOut`, `silence`, `delete`, `crop`, `spectralGain`; one undo step per edit, saved with the session, heard in playback and renders. Previews run on a worker thread and are provisional until committed. |
| `TakeCompView` | T.Control | `takes` (`takeId`, `sourceId`, signed decimal-string `sourceOffsetSample`, bounded `peaks`, availability), `compSegments` (stable `segmentId`, `takeId`, exact `startSample`/`endSample`, optional exact fade boundaries), ordered decimal-string `sampleBoundaries`, host selection, `referenceTakeId`, `auditionTakeId` (the take the host auditions now), host `proposal`, `editingEnabled`, `statusText`, `tooltip`, `sampleRate` (lane offsets in seconds; 0 shows samples), `keyboardTakeIndex` (read `keyboardTakeId`); `beginSwipe`/`updateSwipe`/`finishSwipe`/`cancelSwipe`, `moveCursor(delta, extend)`, `focusTake(delta)`, `stopAudition()` | `compProposalPreviewRequested(proposalId, takeId, startSample, endSample, sourceOffsetSample)`, `compProposalRequested(proposalId, segmentId, takeId, startSample, endSample, sourceId, sourceOffsetSample)`, `proposalDecisionRequested(proposalId, accept)`, `auditionRequested(takeId, sourceId, startSample, endSample, sourceOffsetSample)`, `auditionStopRequested(takeId)`, `compareRequested(takeId, referenceTakeId, startSample, endSample, takeOffsetSample, referenceOffsetSample)`, `compProposalPreviewCanceled(proposalId)`, `selectionRequested(startSample, endSample)` | Swipe boundaries are selected from the supplied exact decimal-string table; values are never converted through floating point. Host-provided comp segments render with overlap/fade regions. Selection, proposal accept/reject, audition and compare are requests only; the component does not alter comp state, render audio, persist a session or claim acceptance. Invalid boundary/take/segment snapshots reject editing. Keys: Left/Right select a boundary cell (Shift extends, Home/End jump) through `selectionRequested`, Up/Down choose the take, Enter proposes it for the host selection, Space auditions (the auditioned take's Space or lane button, then labelled Stop, requests `auditionStopRequested`), C compares, Escape cancels a swipe or else ends the audition. `AudioTakeCompModel.viewState()` (`session.comps`) supplies a real comp and its preview/commit/cancel calls answer the requests ([audio-comping.md](audio-comping.md)). Stable names `takeCompView`, `activeCompLane`, `compSegmentCanvas`, `takeLane_<takeId>`, `takeLabel_<takeId>`, `takeOffset_<takeId>`, `takeSwipeSurface_<takeId>`, `takeAudition_<takeId>`, `proposalAccept`, `proposalReject`. |

## MIDI editing and performance surfaces

The note and event data, exact tick conversion, supported MIDI ranges, pattern
playback, probability seed policy, launch quantization and active clip state
belong to the host. These controls draw immutable snapshots and emit requests
using stable IDs; a request or acknowledgment never invents scheduled output.

| Type | Base | Host contract and requests | Stable objectNames |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `PianoRoll` | T.Control | `patternId`, `notes` (`id`, `pitch`, `velocity`, normalized `x0`/`x1`, exact decimal `startTicks`/`endTicks`), `tickBoundaries` (exact decimal strings), pitch range, selected note, `auditionEnabled`; `notePreviewRequested(requestId, patternId, noteId, operation, targetTicks, targetPitch)`, `noteEditFinished`, `noteEditCanceled`, `noteInsertRequested`, `noteDeleteRequested`, `auditionRequested(pitch, velocity)` / `auditionReleased(pitch)` (always paired: finish, cancel, pitch change, disable, destruction), `zoomRequested`, `scrollRequested` | `pianoRoll`, `pianoRollCanvas`. Drag an existing note to request move or resize; double-click requests a note over adjacent exact tick boundaries; Escape cancels. The host retains duration, expression, overlap policy, validation and undo; `session.midi` (docs/audio-midi.md) implements every request and `noteRows`/`tickBoundaries` feed it. |
| `MidiControllerLane` | T.Control | `patternId`, `controllerId`, `events` (stable event ID, exact ticks, normalized x, value, optional note ID), `tickBoundaries`, value range and resolution; `valuePreviewRequested`, `editFinished`, `editCanceled`, `eventValueRequested`, `eventDeleteRequested`, `zoomRequested`, `scrollRequested` | `midiControllerLane`, `midiControllerCanvas`. Drag and double-click send event/value requests; host owns controller encoding, quantization and note association (`session.midi.previewEventEdit`/`setEvent`, `controllerEvents`, `controllerDescriptor`). |
| `StepSequencer` | T.Control | `patternId`, `rows` and steps with stable IDs, enabled/gate/velocity/probability; `stepEditRequested(requestId, patternId, rowId, stepId, field, value)`, `probabilityEditRequested`, `stepSelected`, `zoomRequested`, `scrollRequested` | `stepSequencer`, `stepSequencerCanvas`. Cells display host values and request discrete changes; the UI does not toggle its own source snapshot or run probability/playback logic (`session.midi.setStep`, `stepRows`; seeded probability D-151). |
| `ClipLauncher` | T.Control | `slots` (`id`, `sceneId`, label, host state/availability), `quantization`, `activeSlotId`; `launchRequested(requestId, slotId, quantization)`, `recordRequested(requestId, slotId, quantization)` (from `requestRecord(slotId)`), `stopRequested`, `slotSelected`, `launchAcknowledged`; `acknowledgeLaunch(requestId, accepted/rejected/error/canceled, message)` answers either request | `clipLauncher`, `clipLauncherStatus`, `clipLauncherStopActive`, `clipLauncherSlot_<slotId>`. Queued, playing and recording labels come from host snapshots. Accepted acknowledgment reports only the request result; the active slot changes only when the host publishes it. `session.launcher` supplies `slotList` and engine-quantized acknowledgments (D-153); `session.launcher.record`/`stopRecording` take the record requests. |

## Terminal

`Tk.TerminalSession` owns the local PTY and parsed screen; `Tk.TerminalView`
renders it and forwards keyboard input. See [terminal-session.md](terminal-session.md)
for process lifecycle and platform limits.

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TerminalView` | T.Control | `session` (`Tk.TerminalSession`), `tooltip`; derives terminal rows and columns from its viewport and monospace cell metrics | — | Renders styled libvterm screen spans, cursor and bounded scrollback; supports mouse text selection/copy, keyboard input and system clipboard paste, terminal key sequences, and scroll-follow at the live edge. It never starts a process; the host calls `session.start(program, arguments, workingDirectory)` after an explicit user action. objectNames `terminalView`, `terminalStatusBar`, `terminalViewport`, `terminalRows`, `terminalLine_<index>`, `terminalCursor`, `terminalInput`. |

## Telemetry

Readings over time and against a limit. `Tk.Graph` draws on the scene graph;
`Tk.Meter` is a `QQuickPaintedItem`. Both take a `Tk.Theme.ramp.*` scale (see
[theming.md](theming.md)) so a value is coloured by its magnitude.

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Graph` | Item (C++) | `series` (default property, list of `GraphSeries`), `capacity` 120, `minValue` 0, `maxValue` 100, `autoScale`, `autoScaleFloor` 1, `headroom` 1.15, read-only `effectiveMax`, `mirrored`, `gridRows`, `gridColumns`, `gridColor`, `baselineColor` | — | Time series; samples enter at the right. `append(v)` (first series), `appendTo(i, v)`, `appendRow([...])` (one sample per series, keeping them aligned; a short row repeats each series' last value rather than injecting a zero), `clear()`, `seriesAt(i)`, `seriesCount()`. Implicit size 160×48. `mirrored` hangs the trace from the top edge, for a send series under a receive series. |
| `GraphSeries` | QObject (C++) | `color`, `ramp`, `fill`, `fillOpacity` 0.45, `lineWidth` 1.5, `visible`, `label`, `values`, read-only `count`/`last`/`peak`/`capacity` | — | A fixed-capacity ring buffer: `append(v)` is O(1) and never reallocates. `at(0)` is the **oldest** retained sample. Non-finite samples are stored as 0. The owning Graph sets `capacity`, so every series on one graph shares an x axis. |
| `ChartAxis` | QObject (C++) | Axis kind/domain/units/time zone/reversal, validated `viewportStart`/`viewportEnd`, `setViewport(start,end)` | `changed()`, `viewportChanged()` | Share one instance between `ChartView`s to link its domain and viewport; unshared axes stay independent. Exact UTC domains remain decimal strings. See [chart-data.md](chart-data.md). |
| `ChartSeries` | QObject (C++) | `id`, `name`, `kind`, `stackKey`, `xUnit`, `yUnit`, `sizeUnit`, `baseline`, `visible`, `capacity`, read-only `count` | `changed()`, `pointsChanged()` | Typed point collection for line, step, area, stack, bar, waterfall, scatter, bubble and vector marks. See [chart-data.md](chart-data.md). |
| `ChartDataset` | QAbstractListModel (C++) | `seriesId`, `name`, `kind`, `stackKey`, `xUnit`, `yUnit`, `sizeUnit`, `baseline`, `visible`, `capacity` (2–100,000), optional `color`/`fillColor`, `hasColor`, read-only `count`/`lastError`; roles `pointId`, `x`, `utcMs`, `y`, `category`, `size`, `dx`, `dy`, `gap`, `total` | `changed()`, `rowsChanged()`, `tableMetadataChanged()`, `errorChanged()` | QML ingestion adapter. Unset colors inherit `ChartView` theme roles. `appendPoint(map)`, `replacePoints(list)`, `removePoint(pointId)`, `get(row)`. Require stable point IDs; date-axis `utcMs` must be a signed 64-bit decimal **string**. A full adapter evicts the oldest row on append. |
| `ChartAnnotationModel` | QAbstractListModel (C++) | `count`, `lastError`, `replaceAnnotations(rows)`, `get(row)`; stable `id`, label, kind, exact X/UTC/category, Y and units | `annotationsChanged()`, `errorChanged()` | Atomic, bounded (1024) point and axis-line annotations. Coordinates and units must match view axes. |
| `ChartHistogramSelection` | QObject (C++, renderer-owned) | read-only `valid`, bin indices, exact `lower`/`upper`, inclusivity, unit, channel, normalization | `changed()` | Typed contiguous source-bin domain range; the final source upper edge alone is inclusive. |
| `ChartRenderer` | QQuickPaintedItem (C++) | `xAxis`, `yAxis`, `datasets`, `annotationModel`, themed styles, bounded `paintPointLimit`, selected point/annotation IDs, `histogramSelection`, table and validation state | `pointSelected`, `annotationSelected`, `histogramRangeSelected`, `viewportChanged`, `exportImageReady` | Projects source data through linked axes; paints stable-ID annotations and histogram selection. `annotationAt`, `selectAnnotationAt`, `annotationsCsv`, `selectHistogramBins`, chart CSV, bounded image/PNG/SVG export. Renderer cap: 64 datasets, 200,000 points. |
| `ChartView` | Item (QML) | Assignable `xAxis`/`yAxis`, default `datasets`, `annotationModel`, `showDataTable`, `showAnnotationTable`, `histogramSelection`, view fractions, selection, chart/annotation CSV and image/viewport methods | `pointSelected`, `annotationSelected`, `histogramRangeSelected`, `exportRequested` | Themed chart and accessible full-source point/annotation tables. Sharing an axis instance links zoom/pan. objectNames `chartView`, `chartPlot`, `chartDataTable`, `chartAnnotationTable`, `chartFocusRing`. |

| `Sparkline` | Graph (QML) | `color`, `ramp`, `fill`, `fillOpacity` 0.35, `lineWidth` 1, `values`, read-only `last`/`peak`, `tooltip` | — | One-series Graph sized for a list row or table cell (`size.sparkline` × `size.sparklineHeight`). No grid, no baseline. `append(v)` passes through. |
| `Meter` | QQuickPaintedItem (C++) | `value`, `from` 0, `to` 100, read-only `position`, `color`, `trackColor`, `ramp`, `rampMode` (`Meter.ByPosition` default, `Meter.ByValue`), `segments` 0, `segmentGap` 1, `radius` 1, `vertical`, read-only `valueColor` | — | How hard something is working now — not how far a task has run, which is `ProgressBar`. `segments` > 0 draws btop's blocks, each at its own ramp position; `ByValue` colours the whole fill by the current reading. Bind a label's colour to `valueColor` so the number agrees with the bar. Implicit size 80×8. |

## Virtual collections

`Tk.VirtualCollectionAdapter` indexes materialized model rows by stable `id`
role and keeps selected IDs separate from view row positions. With a separate
`identityModel`, filtering a proxy does not erase selected source items;
removing a source item does. Tree expansion projects only visible descendants
and loads lazy children through the source model. A 4,096-ID ceiling on range
requests prevents an accidental whole-model copy into QML.

| Type | Base | Main properties and actions | Stable objectNames and behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `VirtualList` | T.Control | `model`, `identityModel`, `selectionModel`, `idRole`, `labelRole`, `secondaryRole`, `iconRole`, `emptyText`, `tooltip`; `selectRow(index, modifiers)`; `activated(id)`, `contextRequested(id, position)` | `virtualList`, `virtualListView`, `virtualListDelegate`, `virtualListEmpty`. A recycling ListView keeps delegates bounded; rows select by ID, keyboard Up/Down changes selection and Enter activates. Accessible row press selects and activates its stable ID. The host applies sort/filter to its model. |
| `VirtualTree` | T.Control | `model`, `identityModel`, `selectionModel`, `idRole`, role names, `flatProjection`, `tooltip`; `requestExpansion`, `requestRename`, `requestMove`; `activated`, `expansionRequested`, `renameRequested`, `moveRequested` | `virtualTree`, `virtualTreeView`, `virtualTreeDelegate`, `treeDisclosure_<id>`, `treeRow_<id>`. A hierarchical source gets a C++ visible-row projection. Expansion can fetch lazy children; collapse hides them while retaining a selected child's stable ID. `flatProjection` accepts a host-provided visible-row model. Focused Up/Down selects the current stable ID, Left/Right requests expansion, Enter and accessible row press activate. Rename/move signals are requests to the document model. |
| `SceneOutliner` | T.Control | `scene`, `keepWorldOnDrop`, `listHeight`, `tooltip`; `reparentRequested` | `sceneOutliner`, `sceneOutlinerRows`, `sceneOutlinerRow_<id>`, `sceneOutlinerSelect_<id>`, `sceneOutlinerExpand_<id>`, `sceneOutlinerVisibility_<id>`, `sceneOutlinerDrop_<id>`. Projects `SceneModel3D.entities` into an ID-stable hierarchy, selects through the scene model, supports Up/Down traversal and Left/Right expansion, toggles local visibility, and requests reparenting by dropping onto a parent. The host routes `reparentRequested` to `SceneModel3D.reparent`; `keepWorldOnDrop` makes world/local transform preservation explicit, and model errors stay visible through `lastError`. It does not expose rename because the model has no rename API. |
| `AssetGrid` | T.Control | `model`, `identityModel`, `selectionModel`, `provider`, `document`, `viewId`, `resourceRole`, `labelRole`, `captionRole`, `tileWidth`, `tileHeight`, `tileSpacing`, `tooltip`; `activated(id)`, `contextRequested(id, position)` | `assetGrid`, `assetGridView`, `assetGridDelegate`, `assetGridThumbnail_<id>`, `assetGridCaption`, `assetGridEmpty`. GridView recycles tiles; pooled thumbnails stop subscriptions and a reused tile refreshes its resource ID. Pointer modifiers and keyboard arrows/Home/End/Space select stable IDs; Enter and accessible row press activate. External current-ID changes restore the visible row. |
| `AssetDependencyIndex` | C++ `QObject` | `projectRoot`, `assets`, `diagnostics`, `assetCount`, `edgeCount`, `lastError`, `scanning`; `upsertAsset`, `setAvailable`, `beginScan`, `commitScan`, `commitScanEdges`, `scanQmlFile`, `scanProjectAsync`, `cancelProjectScan`, `asset`, `dependencies`, `references`, `dependents`, `affectedBy`, `relinkCandidates`, `relinkPlan`; `scanRejected`, `projectScanFinished`, `projectScanDiscarded` | Bounded stable-ID directed graph. QML sources are scanned one at a time or as a whole project on a worker; edges record kind, line and reference text. Generation tokens reject stale results. Missing/cycle diagnostics name the line, transitive reverse impact is inspectable, and relink plans list the edits a host applies. See [asset-dependencies.md](asset-dependencies.md). |

Rebuild is available only when nothing in the selected asset's dependency closure is missing or cyclic; Rebuild affected sends the valid outputs in dependency order. Scan state with Cancel. The host handles all requested actions. Imports its own export and Chrome Trace Event JSON off-thread. See [trace-exploration.md](trace-exploration.md). |

Shared time ruler, zoom to a 50 ns window (buttons, Ctrl+wheel), pan (slider, Shift+wheel), filters, click selection, exact-time inspector with duration, depth and ancestor path, a caption with records in view and merged marks, worker export, and Cancel while loading. Wraps virtualized `AssetGrid`. Without `catalog` every action is a host request and metadata comes from `selectedMetadata`. With `catalog` the grid shows the catalog rows (`assetId` identity), search sets `searchText`, the bin filter and "move to" boxes use its virtual bins, Create proxy and Use proxy/Use original drive `createProxy`/`setProxyActive`, and the metadata panel reads `catalog.asset()` (duration, codec, resolution, audio, online, proxy status, tags). Host mode requests a frame through the injected AssetProvider with exact frame identity and timestamp; the host owns decoding, resource lifetime, transport and seek acceptance, and stepping uses host-supplied identities. Player mode shows a `MediaFrameView`, seeks through the player (the engine acknowledges each request) and always shows the identity of the frame on screen (timecode · frame · ticks), marking a pending seek instead of relabelling the old frame; missing or damaged media shows its error, never a stale picture. Keys in player mode: Space play/stop, Left/Right step, Home/End first/last frame. Double-click toggles overlays. |

## Event stream

| Type | Base | Properties and actions | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `EventLogModel` | QAbstractListModel (C++) | `maxEntries` (default 5,000; hard limit 100,000), `retainedCount`, filtered `count`, `lastRejectedCount`, `severityFilter`, `sourceFilter`, `textFilter`, inclusive `fromUtc`/`toUtc`, `selectedEventId`; `appendEvent(map)`, `appendEvents(list)`, `eventAt(row)`, `eventById(id)`, `indexOfEventId(id)`, `selectEvent(id)`, `clearSelection()`, `clear()`, `exportCsv(ids)` | `eventsAboutToAppend()`, `eventsAppended(retainedFromBatch)`, `eventsRejected(count)`, `selectionChanged()` | Real bounded model with stable event IDs and UTC-normalized timestamps. Batch ingestion emits one model insertion/reset rather than one notification per event. Missing timestamps default to now; malformed timestamps, non-string details and oversized rows are rejected whole and counted in `lastRejectedCount`. Each event payload is capped at 16 KiB, each field has a smaller ceiling, retained payload at 64 MiB and batch staging at 16 MiB. A batch returns/signals the number of its newest valid rows that fit the staging/retention window; earlier valid rows may be discarded by that bound. Filters synchronously scan only retained rows. CSV uses RFC 4180 quoting; an empty ID list exports the filtered rows, while a nonempty list exports those retained IDs. |
| `EventLogView` | T.Control | `model` (`Tk.EventLogModel`), `followTail` (default true), `tooltip`, read-only `selectedEventId`, `listView` | `exportRequested(csv)`, `detailRequested(eventId, details)` | Host owns file writing, details navigation and any asynchronous export job. Pausing only preserves the visible stable event anchor; ingestion and bounded eviction continue. The control uses a recycling Qt Quick ListView because the generic `VirtualList` row does not expose event-specific timestamp, severity and source columns. Delegates remain viewport-bounded. objectNames `eventLogView`, `eventLogFilters`, `eventLogSeverityFilter`, `eventLogSourceFilter`, `eventLogTextFilter`, `eventLogFromUtc`, `eventLogToUtc`, `eventLogFollow`, `eventLogExport`, `eventLogCount`, `eventLogMode`, `eventLogRows`, `eventLogRow_<eventId>`, `eventLogDetails`. |

## Tables

`DiffView` is a virtual line comparison surface backed by `DiffModel`. It
supports aligned side-by-side and inline presentation, stable hunk navigation,
and explicit accept/reject requests through a host transaction adapter. See
[diff-view.md](diff-view.md) for roles, bounds, revision guards, cancellation,
delegate contract and objectNames.

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `DataTable` | Item | `columns` (`list<TableColumn>`), `model`, `sortKey`, `sortOrder`, `currentIndex`, `showHeader`, `alternatingRows`, `rowHeight` (`size.row`), `emptyText`, `columnWidths`, `tooltip` (accessible table name), read-only `count`/`visibleColumns`/`gridWidth` | `sortRequested(key, order)`, `activated(index)` (user action only), `rowRightClicked(index, point)`, `columnResized(key, width)` | Accessible Table named by `tooltip` or “Data table”; each virtual row is an accessible Row named from its visible column titles and formatted values, and exposes selectable/selected state. Tab focuses the table; ↑/↓ changes selection and Return/Enter activates a valid current row. Pointer activation and the row’s accessible press action select and emit `activated`; keyboard navigation and programmatic `currentIndex` assignment select without emitting it. Focusable ColumnHeader items use Space/Return/Enter or accessible press to request sort, and Left/Right or accessible increase/decrease to resize fixed columns; active focus has a themed ring. Virtualised: only visible rows exist. **The table never sorts or filters** — a header action emits `sortRequested` and nothing moves until the owner applies it to its model, because only the model knows whether "3.9" outranks "12" as a number or as text. `model` is a JS array or anything exposing `get(index)`; `recordAt(index)` is the single path a cell takes to its data. Header seams also drag fixed columns wider. When the pane is too narrow, header and rows scroll horizontally together at column minimum widths; cell text elides within its column. objectNames `tableHorizontal`, `tableHeader`, `tableBody`, `headerCell_<key>`, `headerFocusRing_<key>`, `tableRow`. |
| `TableColumn` | QtObject | `key`, `title`, `width` (`size.tableColumn`, 80 compact), `minWidth` (`size.control`, 24 compact), `flex` 0, `align`, `mono`, `muted`, `visible`, `sortable`, `resizable`, `descendingFirst`, `tooltip`, `formatter`, `delegate`, `ramp`, `rampFrom`, `rampTo` | — | Fixed (`width`) or elastic (`flex` > 0, sharing what the fixed columns leave, never below `minWidth`). `formatter(value, row)` returns the cell text; `delegate` replaces the label entirely and reads its data from its **parent**: `parent.value`, `parent.row`, `parent.column` — this is how a sparkline or meter becomes a cell. It is `parent` and not a required property because a Loader does not initialise a loaded item's required properties from its own same-named ones, and a delegate declared in another file cannot see the table's internal ids. `ramp` colours the cell's text by magnitude. `descendingFirst` is right for "who is using the most". |

## Planning timeline

| Type | Base | Properties and actions | Signals and stable objectNames |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `GanttCalendar` | QObject (C++) | `timeZoneId`, `workingDays` (Monday bit 0), `workStart`, `workEnd`, `holidays` (local ISO dates); `snapForward(utc)`, `addWorkingMinutes(utc, minutes)`, `workingMinutesBetween(a,b)`, `formatLocal(utc)`, `localDate(utc)` (ISO local date), `dayStartUtc(isoDate)` (UTC ms of local midnight, first valid instant on a DST-skipped midnight), `isWorkingDate(isoDate)` | `changed`, `errorChanged`; exact UTC millisecond **strings** cross QML. Working minutes are elapsed time in local working intervals, including DST transitions. |
| `GanttTaskModel` | QAbstractListModel (C++) | `calendar`, `transactionAdapter`, `count`, `previewActive`, `lastError`; `replaceTasks(rows)`, `get(index)`, `displayGet(index)`, `indexOfId(taskId)`, `previewMove(id,startUtc)`, `commitPreview()`, `cancelPreview()`, `undo()` | `rowsChanged`, `previewChanged`, `committed`; roles `taskId`, `label`, `startUtc`, `durationMinutes`, `progress`, `baselineStartUtc`, `baselineDurationMinutes`, `dependencies`, and the optional validated host fields `finishUtc`, `baselineFinishUtc`, `kind` (`task`/`summary`/`milestone`), `critical`, `inactive`, `outlineLevel` (0–64), `barText`, `linkTypes` (parallel to `dependencies`: FS/SS/FF/SF) — see [gantt.md](gantt.md#host-scheduled-rows-d-302). IDs are UUID strings compatible with `SelectionModel`; replacement is atomic. |
| `GanttSimpleScheduleAdapter` | `GanttTransactionAdapter` (C++) | Reference finish-to-start dependency propagation (every link treated as FS; drops `finishUtc` from rows it moves) and one undo stack; host can subclass `GanttTransactionAdapter` for constraints and document transactions | `previewMove` returns the whole schedule without mutating source, `commitMove` accepts atomically, `cancelMove` discards, `undoMove` returns a prior schedule. |
| `GanttView` | T.Control | `taskModel`, `calendar`, `selectionModel`, `allocationProvider`, `visibleStartUtc`, `timeScale` (`working` default, `calendar`), `pixelsPerWorkingMinute`, `pixelsPerDay` (`size.control`), `labelWidth` (0 hides the label column), `rowHeight` (`size.row`), `headerHeight` (`size.timeRuler`; ≥ 1.5 rows gives the calendar scale a two-tier ruler), `markers` ([{`utc`, `label`, `role`}]), `contentY` (read/write), read-only `contentHeight`, `visibleEndUtc`, `tooltip`; `previewMove`, `commitPreview`, `cancelPreview`, `undo`, `scrollBy(minutes)` | `taskActivated(taskId)`, `previewRequested(taskId,startUtc)`; objectNames `ganttView`, `ganttHeader`, `ganttRulerMajor`, `ganttRulerMinor`, `ganttMarkerLabel_<i>`, `ganttRows`, `ganttStripe`, `ganttNonWorking`, `ganttLabelDivider`, `ganttRow`, `ganttRowSelection`, `ganttTaskLabel`, `ganttTrack`, `ganttTaskBar`, `ganttProgress`, `ganttSummaryBar`, `ganttSummaryProgress`, `ganttMilestone`, `ganttBaseline`, `ganttAllocation`, `ganttBarText`, `ganttDependencies`, `ganttMarker_<i>`. Drag or Left/Right previews; Enter commits, Escape cancels, Ctrl+Z undoes; a click anywhere in a row selects, double-click activates; horizontal wheel or Shift+wheel scrolls time. Task bars are a subtle fill with a solid progress (danger when `critical`, outline when `inactive`), summaries a bracket, milestones a diamond, baselines a lane under the bar; links follow `linkTypes` with arrowheads (D-303). Internal parts (not for direct use): `GanttBackdrop`, `GanttRuler`, `GanttRow`, `GanttLinks`, `GanttMarkers`, `GanttScale.js`. |

See [gantt.md](gantt.md) for the task schema, calendar and transaction contracts, allocation provider, grid synchronization and limitations.

## Editable two-dimensional grids

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `EditableGrid` | T.Control | `model` (`Tk.EditableGridModel`), `tooltip`, `rowHeaderWidth`, `headerHeight`, `columnLabel(id, index)`, `rowLabel(id, index)`, `activeRowId`, `activeColumnId`, `scrollX`, `scrollY`, `showHeaders`, `alternatingRows`, `fillHandleEnabled`; read-only `bodyWidth`, `bodyHeight`, `activeRow`, `activeColumn`, `visibleCellRecords`, `editorRect`; `setCurrent`, `beginCellEdit`, `commitCellEdit`, `cancelCellEdit`, `focusCellEditor`, `areaRect(rowId, columnId)`, `pasteMatrix`, `fillSelection` | `currentCellChanged(rowId, columnId)`, `editCommitted(rowId, columnId, value)`, `editCancelled()` | Two-axis virtualized view over bounded model regions (`layoutCells`). Frozen rows/columns use model geometry. Arrows and Page Up/Down navigate; Shift extends a stable-ID range and Escape restores the prior cell; Ctrl+Home/End jump to grid ends. Enter/F2 edits from the model's `editValue`, and typing a character starts an edit with it; a refused commit keeps the editor open. Ctrl+Z/Y uses model-local history when enabled. Merged areas are one cell for navigation, selection and editing. `pasteMatrix(row, column, rows)` and `fillSelection(targetRow, targetColumn)` call atomic model transactions. The accessible table reports current value and range extent; visible cells expose selected/focused state and an assistive select action. Cells stack by pane (body, frozen strips, frozen corner); the selection and then the editor draw above them, each clipped to its pane. objectNames `gridColumnHeaders`, `gridRowHeaders`, `gridCornerHeader`, `gridCellViewport`, `gridRangeOverlay`, `gridCell_<rowId>_<columnId>`, `gridEditorPane`, `gridCellEditor`. |
| `GridCellDelegate` | Item | `modelData` (a `layoutCells` record), `index`, `grid`, `editorActive`; read-only `cellStyle`, `displayText`, `merged`, `hasIcon`, `fill`, `hashed`, `indentWidth` | objectNames `gridCell_<rowId>_<columnId>`, `gridCellBackground`, `gridCellDataBar`, `gridCellIcon`, `gridCellText`, `gridCellHashes`. Draws one cell or merged area from the record's `style`: alignment, bold/italic/underline/strike-out, document text/fill colors (text over a document fill uses a contrasting theme color), a data bar, a trend or status icon (`iconTone` positive/negative/muted/accent, else warning), an outline `indent` (levels × `space.lg`, icon and text move together) and the danger color for error values. A `numeric` value that does not fit shows `###` (read-only `hashed`), never a cut number; other text elides. Keeps no state. Accessible Cell named with the value. |
| `GridFillHandle` | Item | `grid`, `active`; read-only `corner`, `dragging`, `target`; `previewTo(x, y)`, `commit()` | objectNames `gridFillHandle`, `gridFillPreview`. The drawn fill handle on the last range's corner: dragging previews an extension along one axis and releasing calls `EditableGridModel.fillRange` (see [editable-grid.md](editable-grid.md#fill-handle)). Hidden for read-only models and when its corner is scrolled out or under a frozen pane. Accessible Grip with a tooltip. |
| `HeaderView` | T.Control | `gridModel`, `orientation`, `scrollX`, `scrollY`, `rowHeaderWidth`, `sectionLabel(id, index)`, `tooltip` | — | Visible section IDs and geometry come from the model. Drag a seam to resize or a section body to reorder by stable ID. Header order and sizes are never copied into QML state. objectNames `gridColumnHeader_<id>`, `gridRowHeader_<id>`, `gridColumnResize_<id>`, `gridRowResize_<id>`. |
| `RangeSelectionOverlay` | Item | `gridModel`, `scrollX`, `scrollY`, `rowHeaderWidth`, `headerHeight` | — | Resolves model-owned ID ranges into up to four frozen/body bands, each drawn in a clip of its pane so a band scrolled under a frozen pane is cut there. It stores no endpoints and is ignored by accessibility. objectNames `gridRangeSelection_<index>` (the whole band, overlay coordinates), its pane clip `gridRangePane_<index>` and the drawn outline `gridRangeBorder_<index>`. |

`EditableGridModel` supplies sparse edits, stable section IDs, frozen geometry,
range selection and transaction guards, including insert/remove notifications
and safe history invalidation. The [100,000 × 100 example](../examples/editable-grid.qml)
instantiates only viewport cells. See [editable-grid.md](editable-grid.md) for
source, domain-source and external undo contracts.

## Query and pivot

These controls edit typed version-1 query JSON and delegate validation and
execution to `Tk.QueryController`. They do not build SQL or other executable
text. Local execution is bounded by the controller's row, group, result-cell
and provenance limits.

| Type | Base | Properties and actions | Stable objectNames and behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `QueryController` | QObject (C++) | `source` (`EditableGridModel`), `schema`, `query`, `status`, `diagnostic`, `requestId`, `resultModel` (read-only `EditableGridModel`), `rowLabels`, `columnLabels`, `maxSnapshotValues`; `refresh()`, `cancel()`, `cellValue(rowId,columnId)`, `sourceRows(rowId,columnId)`; `completed(requestId)`, `rejected(requestId,reason)` | QML adapter for bounded asynchronous local query execution. `schema` is `{id,revision,fields:[{id,type,nullable}]}` and `query` is the typed version-1 JSON object. Only the current request can publish. Source and result models remain separate; result snapshots reject edits and retain aggregate provenance. Status is `idle`, `running`, `complete`, `rejected` or `cancelled`. |
| `FilterBar` | Item | `fields` (`{id,label,type}`), `clauses` (`{field,type,op,value}`), `conjunction` (`and`/`or`), `valid`, `validationError`, `toPredicate()`, `addClause()`, `updateClause(index,key,value)`, `removeClause(index)`; `predicateChanged(predicate)`, `clausesEdited(clauses)`, `conjunctionEdited(conjunction)`, `clauseRemoved(index)` | `filterConjunction`, `filterAdd`, `filterClause_<index>`, `filterField_<index>`, `filterOperator_<index>`, `filterValue_<index>`, `filterBoolean_<index>`, `filterRemove_<index>`, `filterValidation`. Operators are type-filtered; integer literals stay decimal strings to preserve signed 64-bit values. Incomplete or invalid drafts remain editable and block query submission. Null predicates use explicit `isNull`/`isNotNull`; matching multiple clauses emits an AND/OR tree. |
| `QueryBuilder` | Item | `controller`, `schema`, `sourceRevision`, `joinSchema`, `joinSourceRevision`, `joinEnabled`, `joinLeftField`, `joinRightField`, `joinLeftOuter`, `detailMode`, `projection`, `sortField`, `sortAscending`, `sortNullsLast`, `clauses`, `conjunction`, `rowFields`, `columnFields`, `measures`, `querySpec`, `status`, `diagnostic`, `loadDiagnostic`, `loadSpec(spec)`, `requestPreview()`; `queryBuilt(spec)`, `queryRequested(spec)`, `previewRequested(requestId)` | Existing filter/axis/measure/preview names plus `queryTransformPanel`, `queryDetailMode`, `queryJoinEnabled`, `queryJoinLeftField`, `queryJoinRightField`, `queryJoinLeftOuter`, `queryProject_<fieldId>`, `querySortField`, `querySortAscending`, `querySortNullsLast`. The transform panel edits one typed join and detail projection/sort; aggregate axes and measures remain available. `requestPreview()` sends the versioned AST to the bounded controller. `loadSpec()` roundtrips supported visual drafts and reports unsupported predicates/fields. |
| `PivotView` | Item | `controller`, `maxRows` (50; capped at 50), `maxColumns` (24; capped at 24), `rowPage`, `columnPage`, `collapsedRows`, `rowHeaderWidth`, `columnWidth`, read-only `totalRows`/`totalColumns`/`visibleRows`/`visibleColumns`/`truncated`; `drillThrough(rowId,columnId,sourceRows)` | Existing paging/header/cell names plus `pivotExpand_<rowId>`. Reads the controller's read-only result snapshot. Prefix subtotal rows can collapse descendants without changing result values or provenance. It instantiates at most `50 × 24` cells; paging keeps all visible bounded result IDs reachable. Activation emits stable IDs and contributing source rows; joined records resolve through controller `sourceReferences`. No edit path. |

## Rich text and paginated documents

One `Tk.RichTextDocument` can be shown by any number of views at once. Each
view keeps its own layout and selection; edits made in one view reflow the
others and keep their logical selections. Every formatting action is a
public command ID and one undo step. The model, persistence schema, layout
and command table are in [rich-text.md](rich-text.md).

| Type | Base | Properties and actions | Signals and stable objectNames |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `PdfView` | Item | `documentId`, `resourceId`, `resourceRegistered`, `provider`, `document`, `viewId`, `pageCount`, zero-based `currentPage`, `zoom`, host-bounded `visiblePages` (`[{pageIndex,pageId,pageWidth,pageHeight,rotation,state,error}]`), `maxVisiblePages` (capped at 9), `capabilities`, `query`, `searchStatus`, `searchError`, `searchResults` (`[{id,pageIndex,snippet,bounds}]`), `links` (`[{id,label,target,pageIndex}]`), `selectedSearchResultId`, `tooltip`; `requestPage`, `requestZoom`, `search`, `showResult`, `selectResultText` | `pageRequested(index)`, `zoomChangeRequested(zoom)`, `rotationRequested(pageIndex,deltaDegrees)`, `searchRequested(query,requestId)`, `searchResultRequested(resultId,pageIndex)`, `textSelectionRequested(resultId,pageIndex,bounds)`, `linkActivatedRequested(linkId,target)`. Each visible delegate requests `AssetThumbnail` kind `pdf-page` using the resource ID and options `{pageIndex,rotation}`; only the bounded page window subscribes. The host owns PDF decoding, metadata, text geometry, selection and navigation. Per-page loading/errors and unavailable search/render capabilities stay visible. Annotation identity, permissions and persistence are host-owned. Creation and accept/reject are requests; visible status changes only with host snapshots. Deletion accepts `accepted` or `in-document` records when `capabilities.canDelete` permits. Redaction is proposal-only: the control has no final-redaction action. | `RichTextDocument` | QObject (C++) | `documentId`, `revision`, `paragraphCount`, `characterCount`, `readOnly`, `externalUndo`, `canUndo`, `canRedo`, `undoText`, `redoText`, `lastError`, `pageSetup` (points), `pageSetupMm` (publishing dialog map), `defaultFontFamily`, `defaultFontSize`; `applyPageSetupMm(settings)`, `exportPdf(path)`, `undo()`, `redo()`, `toJson()`, `loadJson(json)`, `saveToFile(path)`, `loadFromFile(path or URL)`, `text()`, `paragraphIds()`, `paragraphText(id)`, `paragraphFormatMap(id)`, `missingFontFamilies()`, `applyHistoryChanges(transactionId, reverse, expectedRevision)`, `releaseHistoryTransaction(id)` | `changed`, `historyChanged`, `committed(transactionId, label)`, `rejected(message)`. `applyPageSetupMm` commits document-wide size, margins, columns, gutter and (WP-053) bleed as one undo step; section and page scopes return `Unsupported`. `exportPdf` writes every paginated page to a local PDF path at the document's page dimensions, through the same sheet painter as `PrintAdapter` ([printing.md](printing.md)). A continuous, auto-growing editor with input-field chrome: it grows from `rows` lines up to `maximumHeight`, then scrolls. objectNames `richTextEditorArea`, `richTextEditorTile`, `richTextEditorPlaceholder`, `richTextEditorScrollBar`, `richTextEditorFocusRing`. |
| `RichDocumentView` | T.Control | `document`, `readOnly`, `zoom`, `tooltip`; read-only `area`, `formatState`, `currentPage`, `pageCount`, `paginationComplete`; `goToPage(page)`, `invokeCommand(id, args)` | `linkActivated(href)`. Pages are stacked vertically with a gap. `goToPage` scrolls and keeps the selection. `currentPage` is the page navigated to, the caret's page when the caret is in view, or the page at the viewport's centre. Only visible pages are materialised, each as `richDocumentPage_<pageKey>` holding `richDocumentTile_<pageKey>`. Pages beyond the viewport are laid out in 8 ms slices while `richDocumentPagination` shows progress. Other objectNames: `richDocumentArea`, `richDocumentVerticalScrollBar`, `richDocumentHorizontalScrollBar`. |

| `RichTextPageModel`, `RichTextTile` | C++ | Page rows (`pageIndex`, `pageKey`, `revision`, `label`, `section`, `ordinal`, `heading`, `current`), `outline(limit)`, `sectionIndex(page)`, `sectionCount()`, `generation`; a tile paints one page region (`area`, `pageIndex`, `sourceRect`, `decorations`, `paper`) | Building blocks of `PageNavigator` and `RichDocumentView`, used for custom page UIs. |

`PdfAnnotationDocument` is the optional local PDF annotation backend (`Poppler Qt6` when installed). `open(path)` accepts a file path or file URL; `addAnnotation(pageIndex, kind, boundsPoints, text, author)` supports `note` and `highlight` and returns a persistent ID; `removeAnnotation(id)` removes a record; `saveAs(path)` writes a PDF copy and reopens it. `annotations`, `pageCount`, `dirty`, `capabilities` and `errorString` are observable. `boundsPoints` uses unrotated page points from the top left, matching `pdf-text` word rectangles. The backend reports note/highlight availability through `capabilities.annotationKinds`; form editing, OCR, signatures and final redaction remain unavailable.

`RichTextSessionAdapter` (`session`, `document`, `extensionKey`, `loaded`,
`lastError`, `warnings`, `reload()`, `flush()`) keeps the document in a
`DocumentSession`, which then handles save, recovery and stale-save checks.

`RichTextEditor`, `RichDocumentView` and their inner `RichTextArea` expose
`assetProvider`. When set, `asset:<resourceId>` image objects request a
thumbnail through that provider and repaint after it arrives or is relinked.
The host registers each resource ID in the provider's `ResourceResolver`.
[examples/pdf-review](../examples/pdf-review/Main.qml) uses a two-page PDF resource and an in-memory `PdfAnnotationDocument` whose changes can be saved with `saveAs`. [examples/print-preflight](../examples/print-preflight/Main.qml) binds PrintPreview, PrintSheetView and PreflightPanel to one PrintAdapter: real output sheets, jobs and preflight issues with repairs. [examples/document-review](../examples/document-review/Main.qml) illustrates reference/review request surfaces. [examples/rich-document](../examples/rich-document/Main.qml) shows a paged
view, a draft editor over the same document, a `FormatBar` and a
`PageNavigator` on a mixed-script fixture.

## Slide authoring and presentation

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals and notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `SlideCanvas` | Item | `deck` (`Tk.SlideDeck`), `slideId`, `capabilities` (`canEdit`, `canInsertText`, `canInsertShape`, `canApplyMaster`), `snapping`, `fillColors` (default `deck.documentColors()`), `tooltip`; read-only `selectedObjectIds`, `selectedObject`, `textEditing`, `currentSlide`, `masterChoices`, `canvasDocument`, `toolController`, `spatialTransform`, `view`, `textEditor`; `select(ids)`, `insert(kind)`, `deleteSelection()`, `setFill(color)`, `beginTextEdit()`, `commitTextEdit()`, `cancelTextEdit()` | The editable slide on the WP-042 canvas stack ([slides.md](slides.md#canvas)): a `Tk.SlideCanvasDocument` in a `Tk.CanvasView`, `Tk.ToolController` select/marquee/Shift multi-select, `Tk.SpatialTransform` resize, rotate and keyboard nudge through `Tk.SelectionOverlay`, drag-inside-selection moves that snap (`Tk.SnapModel`: other objects, slide edges, the centre guides drawn by `Tk.GuideLayer`). Every change is one deck undo step. Delete/Backspace delete; F2/Enter or double-click edit text through `deck.beginTextEdit` in a `Tk.RichTextEditor` (formatting survives; Done commits one step, Escape discards). Pixels come from the output painter, so master background, font sizes and rich text show as in the show; empty placeholders show their prompt here only. The audience slot is a separate accessible surface and never displays speaker notes. Names `presenterAudienceStatus`, `presenterDisplayPicker`, `presenterReconnect`, `presenterAudienceOutput`, `presenterPrivatePanel`, `presenterPrevious`, `presenterNext`, `presenterNextSlide`, `presenterNotes`, `presenterTimer`, `presenterTimerAction`, `presenterBlank`, `presenterAnnotationTool`, `presenterClearInk`. |
| `PresenterConsole` | Item | `show` (`Tk.SlideShow`), `windows` (`Tk.PresentationWindows`, optional), `tooltip`; read-only `live` | A `PresenterView` bound to the show (notes, titles, clock, blanking, display choice and reconnect through `windows`) whose audience preview is a `SlideFrameView` of the live frame. Previous/Next step builds exactly like the keys (`SlideShow.previous`/`next`). Annotation: the tool picker sets `show.annotationTool`; dragging on the preview (`presenterInkInput`) adds pen or highlighter ink, pressing with the pointer shows the pointer dot, the eraser clears the slide's ink; all of it reaches the audience output. Keys: Right/Down/Space/Page Down next, Left/Up/Page Up/Backspace previous, B or period blank, A/P/H pointer/pen/highlighter, E clear ink, Home/End, Escape ends the show. It is `PresentationWindows.presenterDelegate`'s usual content; keep the `show`/`windows` names. | `SlideFrameView` | QQuickPaintedItem (C++) | `show` (live frame) or `deck` + `slideId` + `step` (−1 complete) + `ticks`, `keyNavigation`; read-only `displayedSlideId`, `paintCount`; `grabFrame(w,h)`, `mapToSlide(point)`, `mapFromSlide(point)` | Paints with the shared slide painter, letterboxed in black; it has no notes input. With `keyNavigation` and a `show` it steps and blanks from the keyboard (the audience window uses it). Wrappers set `Accessible` (it is `Accessible.Graphic` inside `PresenterConsole`). |
| `SlideCanvasDocument` | CanvasDocumentAdapter (C++) | `deck`, `slideId`, `showPrompts`, `renderScale`; read-only `revision`, `objectCount`, `pageRect`, `pageColor`, `guides`, `imageRenders`; `slideObjectId`, `canvasObjectId`, `slideObjectIds`, `canvasObjectIds` | One slide as a canvas document for `CanvasView` and the canvas tools; commits moves, resizes, rotations and removals as one deck step and refuses inserts ([slides.md](slides.md#canvas)). |
| `SlidePreview` | QObject (C++) | `deck`, `liveClock`; read-only `state`, `effectId`, `slideId`, `step`, `ticks`, `stepEndTicks`, `lastError`; `start(effectId)`, `cancel()`, `reset()`, `advance(ticks)` | The AnimationPane player: plays the effect's build step through the show's evaluator; `cancel()` shows the build complete ([slides.md](slides.md#preview)). Bind a `SlideFrameView` to its `slideId`/`step`/`ticks`. |

[examples/slide-authoring](../examples/slide-authoring/Main.qml) is an authoring workspace on a seeded `Tk.SlideDeck` (two masters, rotated shape, effect preview, rehearsal console). [examples/slide-show](../examples/slide-show/Main.qml) wires them to the persistent `Tk.SlideDeck` (undo, master inheritance, effects, notes), rehearses with `Tk.SlideShow` and `Tk.PresenterConsole`, and opens native audience/presenter windows with `Tk.PresentationWindows`. The model, timing, painter, playback and windows are documented in [slides.md](slides.md).

## Notebook and structured blocks

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals and notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `NotebookView` | Item | `notebook` (`Tk.NotebookDocument`, optional), `notebookId`, `notebookTitle`, host `sections` (`[{id,title,collapsed}]`) and `pages` (`[{id,sectionId,title,tags,excerpt,status,updatedText}]`), `currentPageId`, `focusedSectionId`, `query`, `searchResultPageIds`/`searchState`, `errorText`, `historyBackPageIds`, `historyForwardPageIds`, `capabilities`, `tooltip`; read-only `presentedSections`, `presentedPages`, `currentPage`, `currentIndex`, `visiblePages`; `findPage`, `pageIndex`, `findSection`, `pagesInSection`, `filteredPages`, `navigateTo`, `goBack`, `goForward`, `submitSearch`, `insertSection`, `insertPage`, `setCollapsed`, `movePageToSection`, `deletePage`, `moveCurrent`, `addTag`, `removeTag`; default `pageContent` slot | `pageSelectedRequested(pageId)`, `pageOpened(pageId)` (notebook mode), `sectionSelectionRequested(sectionId)`, `sectionCollapseRequested(sectionId,collapsed)`, `sectionInsertRequested(title,requestId)`, `pageInsertRequested(sectionId,afterPageId,requestId)`, `pageMoveRequested(pageId,targetSectionId,targetIndex,requestId)`, `pageDeleteRequested(pageId)`, `tagEditRequested(pageId,tags,requestId)`, `searchRequested(query,requestId)`, `historyNavigationRequested(pageId)`, `pageOrderChangeRequested(pageId,targetIndex,requestId)` | Two modes ([notebooks](notebooks.md#views)). With `notebook`, sections and pages come from the model, structural edits and tags apply to it (undoable), the view owns `currentPageId` and back/forward history, and `submitSearch()` fills `searchResultPageIds` from indexed block text. Without it, navigation, search results and structure stay host snapshots and every change is a request signal. Before a search completes, local filtering narrows title/tag/excerpt metadata; a complete result is authoritative even when empty or when a body hit has no metadata match. | `BlockEditor` | Item | `notebook` (`Tk.NotebookDocument`, optional), `clipboard`, `scriptRuntime`, `resolver`, `notebookId`, `pageId`, host `blocks` (`[{id,type,data,locked,error,depth}]`), `selectedBlockId`, host `focusedBlockId`, `caretOffset`, `selection` (`{anchorBlockId,anchorOffset,focusBlockId,focusOffset}`), `drafts`, `autoCommitDrafts` (default true), `blockTypes`, `capabilities` (adds `canNest`), `provider`, `document`, `viewId`, `errorText`, `tooltip`; read-only `presentedBlocks`, `presentedIds`, `selectedBlock`, `listView`, `activeError`; `findBlock`, `indexOf`, `makeDefaultData`, `insertBlock`, `moveBlock`, `nestBlock`, `deleteBlock`, `submitText`, `submitData`, `splitBlock`, `mergeWithPrevious`, `navigate`, `focusBlock`, `extendSelection`, `copy`, `cut`, `paste`, `deleteSelection`, `formatSelection`, `evaluate`, `tableEdit`, `storeDraft`, `commitDraft`, `flushDrafts` | `blockSelectionRequested(blockId)`, `blockFocusRequested(blockId)`, `blockNavigateRequested(blockId,direction)`, `blockInsertRequested(afterBlockId,type,data,requestId)`, `blockMoveRequested(blockId,targetIndex,requestId)`, `blockNestRequested(blockId,direction,requestId)`, `blockContentEditRequested(blockId,data,requestId)`, `blockSplitRequested(blockId,cursorPosition,requestId)`, `blockMergeRequested(firstBlockId,secondBlockId,requestId)`, `blockDeleteRequested(blockId)`, `blockActionRequested(blockId,action,requestId)`, `blockOpenRequested(blockId,targetId)` | Two modes ([notebooks](notebooks.md#views)). With `notebook`, every edit (text, formats, split, merge, nesting, moves, tables, charts, code evaluation, copy/cut/paste, undo/redo buttons) applies to the persistent model; without it the host owns IDs, order and data and receives requests. The list is a virtualized `ListView` (`reuseItems`) over block IDs; unsaved text lives in `drafts` by block ID, survives recycling and, in notebook mode, commits after a pause in typing and before a page change ([keyboard](notebooks.md#keyboard)). Text, table, chart (`Tk.ChartView` over `chartSeries`), media (an image drawn through `provider`, or from the resolver's URI without one; resolver availability, relink request), embed (link validity) and preserved unsupported blocks have separate parts.
