Use `BlockEditor` rather than instantiating them directly. |

[examples/notebook-authoring](../examples/notebook-authoring/Main.qml) wires page and block requests to a host-owned in-memory notebook, including split/merge, reorder, offline links and missing media. Persistent storage/sync, indexed search, rich cross-block cursor semantics, clipboard preservation and ink/table editors remain model/backend work.

## Workbook and spreadsheet

The spreadsheet stack (WP-055) is described in [workbook.md](workbook.md):
grammar, functions, references, names, formats, conditional formats,
validation, merged cells, sheet properties, clipboard ranges, interchange,
sessions and the native `.qtkwb` format. The `Workbook` owns every input,
formula, value, format, rule and the history; the views keep only the active
cell, selection endpoints and open prompts.

| Type | Base | Properties and actions | Signals, objectNames and behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Workbook` | QObject (C++) | `documentId`, `revision`, `resetGeneration` (decimal strings), `sheets` (`{id,name,index,rows,columns,cells,formulas,tabColor,hidden,protected}`), `sheetCount`, `canUndo`, `canRedo`, `undoText`, `redoText`, `modified`, `undoService`, `transactionActive`, `lastError`, `lastErrorDetail`, `statistics`, `functionNames`, `fileUrl`, `names`, `lastPasteReport`; sheets (`addSheet`, `removeSheet`, `renameSheet`, `moveSheet`, `sheetProperties`, `setSheetProperties`, `setColumnWidth`, `setRowHeight`), cells (`setCellInput`, `setCellInputs`, `cellInput`, `cellDisplay`, `cellValue`, `cellType`, `cellInfo`, `usedRange`, `visibleCells`, `checkInput`, `validateInput`), ranges (`pasteInputs`, `rangeTransfer`, `pasteTransfer`, `copyCells`, `fill`, `clearCells`, row/column insert/remove), presentation (`applyStyle`, `clearFormats`, `cellFormat`, `cellPresentation`, `numberFormatProblem`, `mergeCells`, `unmergeCells`, `mergedAreas`, `mergeAt`), rules (`conditionalFormats`, `checkConditionalFormat`, `setConditionalFormat`, `removeConditionalFormat`, `moveConditionalFormat`, `previewConditionalFormat`, `validations`, `checkValidation`, `setValidation`, `removeValidation`, `validationAt`, `validationItems`), names (`defineName`, `removeName`, `nameProblem`), history (`beginTransaction`, `commitTransaction`, `cancelTransaction`, `undo`, `redo`, `markSaved`, `markUnsaved`), files (`serializedText`, `restoreText`, `save`, `open`, `reset`, `registerFormat`, `adoptImport`) and address helpers | `revisionChanged`, `historyChanged`, `sheetsChanged`, `sheetPropertiesChanged(sheetId)`, `namesChanged`, `pasteReported`, `transactionChanged`, `errorChanged`, `resetOccurred`, `fileChanged`, `rejected(reason)`, `cellsChanged(sheetId,firstRow,firstColumn,lastRow,lastColumn)`, `rowsInserted`/`rowsRemoved`/`columnsInserted`/`columnsRemoved(sheetId,at,count)`. Every operation validates first and is one atomic undo step; failures change nothing and report a span or field. A `DocumentModel` (schema `qindatk.workbook.v1`, kind `workbook`): its steps go to the `UndoService` holding its history, private while standalone and shared once `DocumentManager.createDocument`/`openDocument` wrap it in a `DocumentSession` ([workbook.md](workbook.md#sessions)). A `SnapshotSource` for exporting a standalone workbook. |
| `WorkbookSheetAdapter` | QObject (C++) | `workbook`, `sheetId`, read-only `sheetName`, `gridModel` (`EditableGridModel`), `rowCount`, `columnCount`, `hasCopiedRange`, `copiedRange`, `lastError`, `lastErrorDetail`; `inputAt`, `displayAt`, `cellInfo`, `checkInput`, `commitInput`, `validateInput`, `validationAt`, `validationItems`, `fillDown`, `fillRight`, `fillRange`, `copyRange`, `pasteCopied`, `pasteInputs(row,column,rows,policy)`, `clearRange`, `parseTabularText`, `reportResult(ok)`, `columnLabel`, `address`, `sourceReadCount()`, `resetSourceReads()` | `cellsChanged(firstRow,firstColumn,lastRow,lastColumn)`, `validationPrompt(row,column,input,check)`, `sheetIdChanged`, `sheetNameChanged`, `dimensionsChanged`, `errorChanged`. A domain source for the grid (`sourceCommits`): display, editor text, presentation and merged areas for requested cells only; commits, pastes and fill-handle drags become one Workbook transaction. Follows frozen panes, sizes and protection; records finished header resizes. Falls back to the first sheet when its sheet disappears. |
| <a id="sheetview"></a>`SheetView` | T.Control | `workbook`, `sheetId` (requested), `tooltip`, `pastePolicy` (`allOrNothing`/`skipInvalid`), `clipboard` (`Tk.ClipboardService`); read-only `currentSheetId`, `sheetName`, `adapter`, `grid`, `activeRow`, `activeColumn`, `hasActiveCell`, `activeAddress`, `activeInput` (source), `activeDisplay` (formatted), `activeFormat`, `activeValidation`, `activeMerge`, `sheetProperties`, `readOnly`, `validationPending`, `selection`, `selectionAddress`, `errorText`, `errorDetail`; `setCurrent(row,column,extend)`, `selectRange`, `focusGrid`, `beginEdit`, `commitInput(text)`, `commitInputAt(row,column,text,confirmed)`, `fillDown`, `fillRight`, `copySelection`, `pasteCopied`, `pasteText(tsv)`, `clearSelection`, `applyStyle(patch)`, `toggleStyle(key)`, `clearFormats`, `mergeSelection`, `unmergeSelection`, `freezePanes`, `unfreezePanes`, `openValidationList`, `undo`, `redo` | `currentCellChanged(row,column,address)`, `selectionEdited()`, `sheetShown(sheetId)`, `inputCommitted(row,column,input)`, `inputRejected(message,detail)`, `validationResolved(row,column,action)`. A `Tk.EditableGrid` (`sheetGrid`) with A–Z column labels, formatted and conditionally styled cells, merged areas, frozen panes and the fill handle. Enter/F2 or typing edits the source input, never the displayed value. With the table focused: Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Shift+Z or Ctrl+Y undo/redo in the Workbook, Ctrl+D / Ctrl+R fill down/right, Ctrl+C / Ctrl+V copy/paste through the system clipboard (formulas move; text from other applications pastes as values), Delete clears, Ctrl+B/I/U toggle bold/italic/underline, Alt+Down opens a list rule's values. `Tk.SheetCellAssist` (`sheetCellAssist`) shows the active cell's input message (`sheetInputMessage`, `sheetInputMessageTitle`, `sheetInputMessageText`), a list button (`sheetListButton`, menu `sheetListMenu`) and the prompt for a warning/information entry (`sheetValidationPrompt`: Yes/No/Cancel or OK/Cancel). Remembers the active cell per sheet. Accessible Pane named “<tooltip>, sheet <name>” describing the active cell's formula and result. |
| `FormulaBar` | T.Control | `sheetView`, `tooltip`, `editing`, `targetSheetId`, `targetRow`, `targetColumn`, `errorText`, `errorStart`, `errorEnd`; read-only `workbook`, `text`, `address`, `targetAddress`, `result`, `pointMode`, `inputCheck`, `referenceSpans`, `functionHint`; `beginEdit()`, `commit(restoreSelection)`, `cancel()`, `insertReference(text)`, `jumpTo(address)` | `committed(address,input)`, `cancelled()`. objectNames `formulaNameBox`, `formulaFunctionLabel`, `formulaEditor`, `formulaCancel`, `formulaCommit`, `formulaResult`, `formulaError`, `formulaHint`. The editor shows the active cell's source input; `formulaResult` shows its formatted value. An edit keeps its target cell until Enter commits (a rejection selects the offending span and names it in `formulaError`) or Escape restores the prior input. An entry that breaks a warning/information rule waits for the sheet's prompt. While editing a formula, selecting cells in the sheet (or switching sheets) inserts or replaces the pointed reference at the caret; selecting while editing plain text commits it. The name box selects a typed address or range. Hosts should bind tabs to `sheetView.currentSheetId`. Accessible ToolBar; the error is an AlertMessage. |
| `WorkbookTabs` | Item | `workbook`, `currentSheetId` (host-owned), `tooltip`, `renamingSheetId`, `errorText`, `tabPalette` (theme roles); read-only `sheets`, `visibleSheets`, `hiddenSheets`; `activate(id)`, `addSheet()`, `beginRename(id)`, `commitRename(name)`, `cancelRename()`, `moveBy(id,delta)`, `requestRemove(id)`, `setTabColor(id,color)`, `setHidden(id,hidden)`, `setProtected(id,locked)`, `openMenu(id,anchor)`, `openColorPicker(id,anchor)` | `sheetActivated(sheetId)`, `removeRequested(sheetId,name)` (the host confirms, then calls `Workbook.removeSheet`). objectNames `workbookTabSlot_<id>`, `workbookTab_<id>`, `workbookTabColor`, `workbookTabLock`, `workbookTabEditor`, `workbookTabAdd`, `workbookTabHidden`, `workbookHiddenMenu`, `workbookTabMenu` (`…Rename`, `…Color`, `…Hide`, `…Protect`, `…Remove`), `workbookTabColorPopover`, `workbookTabSwatches`, `workbookTabColorNone`, `workbookTabError`. Hidden sheets leave the strip and return from the hidden-sheets menu; a tab shows its document color and a lock when protected. Double-click or F2 renames inline (Enter/Escape); Ctrl+Shift+Left/Right moves; Delete requests removal; the Menu key, Shift+F10 or a right-click opens the tab menu. Every change is a Workbook undo step; references follow sheet IDs. Accessible PageTabList of PageTabs; a tab's assistive Press activates its sheet, as a click does. |
| <a id="conditionalformateditor"></a>`ConditionalFormatEditor` | T.Control | `workbook`, `sheetId`, `tooltip`, `documentColors`; draft `ruleId`, `ranges`, `type`, `operator`, `value1`, `value2`, `text`, `style`, `minColor`, `midColor`, `maxColor`, `stopIfTrue`; read-only `rules`, `draft`, `check`, `preview`; `newRule(ranges)`, `load(id)`, `apply()`, `remove(id)`, `move(id,priority)`, `setStyle(key,value)` | `applied(ruleId)`. objectNames `cfRuleList` (a `Tk.SheetRuleList`), `cfNew`, `cfRanges`, `cfType`, `cfOperator`, `cfValue1`, `cfValue2`, `cfText`, `cfBold`/`cfItalic`/`cfUnderline`/`cfStrikeout`, `cfTextColor`, `cfFillColor`, `cfClearColors`, `cfMinColor`, `cfThreeColor`, `cfMidColor`, `cfMaxColor`, `cfStopIfTrue`, `cfError`, `cfPreview` (a `Tk.RulePreview`), `cfApply`. Lists the sheet's rules in priority order with move up/down/remove; the draft is checked by the Workbook on every change and `cfPreview` renders the Workbook's own preview of it. Apply commits one undo step. Rule fields live in `Tk.ConditionalRuleForm`. |
| <a id="datavalidationeditor"></a>`DataValidationEditor` | T.Control | `workbook`, `sheetId`, `tooltip`, `pastePolicy`; draft `ruleId`, `ranges`, `type`, `operator`, `value1`, `value2`, `items` (comma or line separated), `allowBlank`, `inputTitle`, `inputMessage`, `errorStyle`, `errorTitle`, `errorMessage`; read-only `rules`, `draft`, `check`; `newRule(ranges)`, `load(id)`, `apply()`, `remove(id)` | `applied(ruleId)`. objectNames `dvRuleList`, `dvNew`, `dvRanges`, `dvType`, `dvOperator`, `dvValue1`, `dvValue2`, `dvItems`, `dvAllowBlank`, `dvInputTitle`, `dvInputMessage`, `dvErrorStyle`, `dvErrorTitle`, `dvErrorMessage`, `dvPastePolicy`, `dvError`, `dvApply`. Fields show for the chosen type; the Workbook checks the draft; apply is one undo step. `pastePolicy` is the host's paste choice (bind it to `SheetView.pastePolicy`). |
| `SheetRuleList` | Item | `rules`, `currentId`, `movable`, `tooltip`, `summary(rule)` | `selected(ruleId)`, `moveRequested(ruleId,priority)`, `removeRequested(ruleId)`. objectNames `sheetRule_<id>`, `sheetRuleUp`, `sheetRuleDown`, `sheetRuleRemove`, `sheetRuleListEmpty`. The rule list of both editors. |
| `RulePreview` | Item | `cells` (`previewConditionalFormat` records), `tooltip` | objectName `rulePreview`. Draws the records with `Tk.GridCellDelegate`, so the preview uses the sheet's own cell rendering. |

The [workbook example](../examples/workbook/Main.qml) combines them over a
two-sheet budget in a `DocumentManager` session, with number formats,
conditional formats, validation, a name, a whole-column reference, a merged
title, fill, undo/redo through the shared `UndoService`, the system
clipboard, save and reopen.

## Docking — see docking.md

`DockHost`, `DockPanel`, `DockFrame`, `DockTabStrip`, `DockDivider`,
`DockFloating`, `DockDropOverlay`, `DockPlacementDialog`. The model is `Tk.DockModel` (C++).
`Tk.DetachedPanelHost` connects that model to `Tk.WindowHost` for real native
panel windows. DockHost's `detachedHost` property registers each declared
content item and its `detachPanelNative`/`reattachPanel` functions move it
between windows while restoring focus to the previously focused panel child.
A detached native window has objectName
`detachedPanelWindow_<panelId>`; see [docking.md](docking.md).
`DockTabStrip` is a named PageTabList; its panel entries are PageTabs with
accessible titles and selected state. Tab focuses an entry, Left/Right moves
and activates across the group, and Home/End selects the first/last panel.
Space/Return/Enter and the accessible press action activate through
`DockModel.activateInGroup`; owner model updates only update selection.
`tabActivated(panelId)` reports accepted user selection. The visible focus
ring is `dockTabFocus_<panelId>`; `tabItem(index)` exposes a current delegate
for focus management by host code. Horizontal pointer drags reorder through
`DockModel.moveInGroup` without activating the dragged panel. A vertical drag
tears it off through the host drag lifecycle. Reordering can recreate the
strip and its delegates; acquire them again after structural model changes.
`DockDivider` is a focusable `Accessible.Splitter` named by its `tooltip` or
“Resize dock panels”. Left/Right moves a vertical seam and Up/Down moves a
horizontal seam by `Theme.space.lg`; accessible increase/decrease uses the
same signed delta path as dragging. It emits `dragged(delta)` and leaves lane
extents and slot shares to `DockZone` and `DockModel`. Its visible focus ring
is `<divider objectName>_focus`.
`DockFloating` exposes eight named `Accessible.Splitter` resize handles
(`dockFloatingResize_<panelId>_<side>`). Arrow keys resize each handle's
applicable axes; accessible increase/decrease and pointer drag share
`DockModel.setFloatingRect` and its minimum-size rules. Focus is outlined with
a themed ring. `Theme.size.seamTarget` supplies expanded edge and corner
targets without changing frame geometry; very small panels shrink corner
targets and disable edge targets that cannot fit without overlap.

Dock headers expose a keyboard-focusable, named Place button
(`dockPlacement_<panelId>`); Shift+F10 on a focused panel/tab opens the same
host-owned `DockPlacementDialog`. Its destination picker supports legal zones,
relative panel placement, tab grouping and explicit tab order. Floating move
uses bounded horizontal/vertical NumberFields; Apply commits through DockModel,
Cancel discards the proposal. `panelPlacementAccepted(panelId)` reports accepted
user requests only. Dialog objectNames: `dockPlacementDialog`,
`dockPlacementDestination`, `dockPlacementX`, `dockPlacementY`, `dialogPrimary`,
`dialogSecondary`. See [docking](docking.md#keyboard-and-accessible-panel-placement)
for bounds, stale-state guards and focus restoration.

<a id="canvasview"></a>
## Spatial canvas

The full contract (coordinate spaces, picking order, gesture lifecycle,
rendering boundary) is in [canvas.md](canvas.md).

| Type | Base | Properties and actions | Stable objectNames and behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `StrokeStyleEditor` | Item | required `style` (`{width, unit, color, alignment, cap, join, miterLimit, dash, dashMode}`), optional `capabilities` lists (`units`, `alignments`, `caps`, `joins`, or supported flags), `tooltip`; `styleChangeRequested(style)` | Every user edit requests a complete canonical descriptor plus `changed`, the fields the user set, so the owner applies exactly those (a value equal to a mixed field's shown value included); owner assignment is silent. Fields named in `style.mixed` show "Mixed" (number fields' placeholder, combo placeholders, a `strokeColorMixed` caption) until set. The width, miter-limit and colour fields show the owner's `style` after every change and every request, so an edit the owner rejects or undoes does not linger. The width is in `style.unit`; choosing a unit requests `{unit}` alone (`CanvasStyleModel` converts, see [stroke units](vector-authoring.md#stroke-units)). Dash presets request `[]`, `[6,3]`, or `[1,2]`; custom input accepts an even list of positive lengths separated by spaces or commas. `capabilities` can tailor the choices; unsupported source values remain in the supplied descriptor. Names `strokeWidth`, `strokeUnits`, `strokeColor`, `strokeAlignment`, `strokeCap`, `strokeJoin`, `strokeMiterLimit`, `strokeDashMode`, `strokeDashPattern`. |
| `FillStyleEditor` | Item | required `fill` (`{kind, settings}`; settings keyed by `none`/`solid`/`gradient`/`pattern`), optional `capabilities`, `patterns` (`[{id,label}]`), `tooltip`; `fillChangeRequested(fill)`, `gradientEditRequested(gradient)` | Changing kind preserves each kind's last settings; solid colour and pattern ID request updated descriptors. Each request carries `changed` (`kind` on a kind change, plus `color` or `patternId`); `fill.mixed` shows a "Mixed" kind placeholder and a `fillColorMixed` caption. The colour field shows the owner's `fill` after every change and request. Gradient Edit requests the current gradient for the host's nested editor. The renderer capability and unsupported metadata are reported while settings remain available. Names `fillKind`, `fillSolidColor`, `fillEditGradient`, `fillPattern`. |
| `ShapePalette` | Item | `definitions` (`[{id,label,category,iconName,imageSource,parameters,enabled,description}]`), `recentIds`, `searchText`, `category`, `columns`, `categoryPicker` (`segmented` default, or `menu`: a combo box for many categories in a narrow panel), `searchAllCategories` (typed text searches every category), `dragEnabled`, `tileSize` (`size.thumbnailHeight`), `tooltip`; read-only `categories`/`visibleDefinitions`/`draggingShapeId`; `shapeRequested(shapeId, parameters)`, `shapeDragStarted(shapeId)`, `shapeDragMoved(shapeId, scenePoint)`, `shapeDragFinished(shapeId, parameters, scenePoint)`, `shapeDragCanceled(shapeId)` | Search matches label and description within a model-derived category (every category with `searchAllCategories`). Selecting an enabled shape requests its stable ID and copied default parameters; the document host creates the object and owns undo. A definition with `imageSource` (the host's own thumbnail: an `image://` provider or data URL) draws as a tile — thumbnail over a two-line caption, hover/focus like a ghost button — instead of an icon button. With `dragEnabled` a drag on a tile or button reports its pointer in scene (window content) coordinates; the host maps the point into its canvas, draws any drop preview and inserts on `shapeDragFinished`; a drag is never also a click (D-302). Recent IDs are presentation state only. Names `shapeSearch`, `shapeCategories`, `shapeCategoryMenu`, `shapeGrid`, `shape_<id>`, `shapeThumbnail`, `shapeCaption`, `shapeNoMatch`. The reference canvas host maps `polygon`, `star` and `rounded-rectangle` requests to `CanvasDocument.addParametricShape` ([parametric shapes](vector-authoring.md#parametric-shapes)). |
| `AppearanceStack` | Item | `effects` (`[{id,name,kind,enabled,supported,parameters,masked,unsupportedReason}]`), `paints` (`[{id,kind,name,present,detail}]`, the fixed fill/pattern/stroke entries in paint order), `tooltip`; `reorderRequested(effectId,newIndex)`, `enabledChangeRequested(effectId,enabled)`, `editRequested(effectId)` | The owner supplies ordered stable-ID records and applies accepted commands; `CanvasAppearanceModel` is the reference canvas owner. Up/down requests a target index; checkbox requests bypass/enable; parameter edit is disabled for unsupported effects. Unsupported records remain visible with their reason. Paint entries are listed first, numbered in paint order, read-only (they are edited in the fill and stroke panels). Names `appearancePaint_<id>`, `appearancePaintDetail_<id>`, `appearanceEffect_<id>`, `appearanceEnabled_<id>`, `appearanceName_<id>`, `appearanceMoveUp_<id>`, `appearanceMoveDown_<id>`, `appearanceEdit_<id>`. |
| `CanvasView` | `CanvasSurface` (C++ QQuickItem) | `document` (`CanvasDocumentAdapter`), `toolController`, `viewState` (shared `CanvasViewState`; null = private), `viewId`, `backend` (`auto`/`software`/`scenegraph`), `zoom`, `viewRotation` (not `rotation`: that turns the item), `centerX`/`centerY`, `fitMode` (`none`/`page`/`content`; refits on resize until the user navigates), `fitPadding` (`space.xl`), `hitTolerance` (`space.xs`, logical px), `wheelZoomStep`, `rotationStep`, `keyboardPanStep`, `touchRotation`, `interactive`, `canvasColor` (`color.canvas`), `accessibleName`; read-only `visibleDocumentRect`, `effectiveDevicePixelRatio`, `hoverHit`, `renderSurface`; `mapToDocument(p)`, `mapFromDocument(p)`, `zoomAbout(x,y,f)`, `zoomIn()`, `zoomOut()`, `setZoomCentered(z)`, `rotateBy(deg[,x,y])`, `panBy(dx,dy)`, `fitDocument()`, `fitRect(r)`, `resetView()`, `hitTest(x,y[,lockPolicy])`, `hitRegion(viewRect[,contain])`, `renderStats()`, `simulateContextLoss([dropCaches])`; `viewChanged`, `hoverChanged`, `frameRendered` | `canvasView` is the root objectName; `canvasOverlay` (hover, selection, tool preview), `canvasOverlaySlot` (default property: app overlays in view coordinates), `canvasFocusRing` are stable parts. Accessible Canvas role named by `accessibleName`; the description reports zoom and rotation; accessible increase/decrease zoom. Input: left/pen/one finger → tool; middle drag, wheel, trackpad, two fingers, native gestures → navigation; Ctrl+wheel zooms about the cursor, Alt+wheel rotates; `+`/`-`, Ctrl+0 fit, Ctrl+1 100 %, R/Shift+R rotate, arrows pan, held Space = temporary pan, Escape cancels the gesture. Navigation never edits the document. |
| `GradientEditor` | `T.Control` | `stops` (host-owned `{id, position, color}` records with normalized positions), `previewSamples` (optional evaluator-produced colors for the ramp), `selectedStopId`, `tooltip`, `keyboardStep`; read-only `previewStop`; `selectionRequested(id)`, `addStopRequested(position)`, `movePreviewRequested(id, position)`, `moveFinished(id, position)`, `moveCanceled(id)`, `deleteStopRequested(id)` | Displays supplied colors and normalized stop positions. Clicking a stop requests selection without finishing an edit. The root owns pointer tracking across host `stops` replacement; drag sends preview, then finish on release, while Escape, lost grab or return to the original position sends cancel. Focusable stop sliders support arrow and accessible increment/decrement requests. Blank track clicks request an added stop; right-click or Delete requests deletion. Stable names `gradientEditor`, `gradientTrack`, `gradientPointer`, `gradientStop_<id>`. The host owns stop data, color meaning/conversion, interpolation, validation, persistence and undo; `GradientEditorModel` supplies that boundary and evaluator-backed preview samples. |
| `CanvasDocument` | `CanvasDocumentAdapter`, `DocumentModel` (C++) | `documentId`, `revision`, `resetGeneration` (decimal string), `objectCount`, `layerCount`, `imageCount`, `pageRect`, `pageColor`, `contentBounds`, `activeLayer` (content layers only), `layers` (paint order with `parent`, `group`, `depth`, `rank`, effective flags), `guides` (`{id, orientation, position}`), `artboards` (`{id, name, rect, background, bleed, exportRect}`), `artboardCount`, `canUndo`/`canRedo`/`undoText`/`redoText`, `historyLimit`, `undoService`, `lastError`; `addGuide(orientation,position)` (new id or ""), `moveGuide(id,position)`, `removeGuide(id)`, `addRect`, `addEllipse`, `addPolyline`, `addPath`, `addParametricShape`, `parametricParameters`, `setParametricParameters`, `pathNodes`, `movePathPart`, `insertPathNode`, `removePathNode`, `setPathClosed`, `exportSvg`, `addLayerNamed`, `removeLayerById`, `addGroupNamed`, `moveLayerTo(id,parent,index)`, `renameLayerById`, `groupLayersById(ids,name)`, `ungroupLayerById`, `removeLayerTreeById`, `moveObjectTo(id,layer,index)`, `childLayerIds(parent)`, `layerObjectIds(layer)`, `addArtboard(name,rect[,background])`, `setArtboardRect`, `renameArtboard`, `setArtboardBackground`, `setArtboardBleed`, `moveArtboard(id,index)`, `removeArtboard`, `artboardInfo`, `artboardAt(x,y)`, `exportArtboardSvg(id,path)`, `setObjectTransform(id,[m11,m12,m21,m22,dx,dy])`, `setObjectVisible`, `setObjectLocked`, `setLayerVisibleById`, `setLayerLockedById`, `moveObjects(ids,dx,dy)`, `removeObject`, `objectInfo(id)`, `undo()`, `redo()`, `clear()`, `populateFixture(count,seed,withImage)`; C++ `commit(CanvasEdit)`/`commitEdit`, `addImage`, `removeImage`, `state()`; `damaged(regions,revision)`, `reset(revision)`, `edited(revision)`, `historyChanged()` | Reference spatial model: stable 64-bit ids, a layer tree of groups and content layers with order/visibility/lock ([layer hierarchy](canvas.md#layer-hierarchy)), document [artboards](canvas.md#artboards), local geometry plus a local→document transform, document-owned tiled images, and a spatial index. Every change is one all-or-nothing `commit` that rejects duplicate targets, stale `before` states, missing layer/image references, non-finite geometry and exceeded limits, and records one undo step (page, layer, image and [guide](canvas.md#document-guides) changes included). History lives in a private `UndoService` until a `DocumentSession` moves it into the shared one ([document models](document-models.md)). Finite singular transforms are accepted and make the object degenerate (not drawn, not picked). Values declared in QML are initial state, not undo steps. |
| `TiledRasterDocument` | QObject (C++ QML type) | Read-only `width`, `height`, stable `layerIds`, composited `imageDataUrl`, `revision`, `canUndo`/`canRedo`, `error`; `brushPreset`; `resizeCanvas`, `createLayer(stableId,name)`, `addMask(layerId)`, `setMaskEnabled`, `updateBrushPreset(preset)`, `beginStroke(layerId,paintMask,color,preset)`, `sampleStroke(x,y,pressure,tiltX,tiltY)`, `endStroke`, `cancelStroke`, `undo`, `redo`, `composite`, `exportPng(path)`, `saveToFile(path)`, `loadFromFile(path)`, `layerInfo(id)`, `brushPreviewDataUrl(preset,color,w,h)`, `placeOnCanvas(canvas,x,y)`, `publishToCanvas(canvas,objectId)`, `canvasToRaster(canvas,objectId,x,y)` | Premultiplied RGBA tiles and optional sparse 8-bit grayscale masks. Samples carry pixel coordinates, normalized pressure and tilt degrees. One begin/sample/end sequence creates at most one bounded undo entry; mask toggles and saved-preset changes are undo entries too; cancellation restores touched tiles. A stroke keeps the preset it began with and never paints a hidden layer or disabled mask. Placement and publishing put the flattened composite on a `CanvasDocument` as one canvas undo step each ([canvas placement](raster-document.md#canvas-placement)). Stable layer order determines compositing. Save/reopen preserves actual sparse tiles, masks, stable IDs and active brush parameters. See [raster-document.md](raster-document.md) for the versioned file schema, brush fields and limits. |
| `RasterStrokeInput` | Item | `document`, stable `layerId`, `paintMask`, `color`, `brushPreset`, `canvasTarget`, optional `pointMapper` (function(localPoint) → raster point), `mousePressure` (default 0.6), `inputEnabled`; `strokeActive`, `lastError` | `strokeCommitted`, `strokeCanceled`, `strokeFailed(message)` | Overlay this on a canvas target sized to its displayed image, or on a `CanvasView` with a `pointMapper` through `mapToDocument` and `canvasToRaster`. It maps local input coordinates to document pixels and brackets one host-owned `TiledRasterDocument` stroke. Pen events preserve Qt tablet pressure and x/y tilt; raw synthesized mouse events are dropped. Unmodified mouse input uses one fallback stream with the configured pressure; touch is ignored. Escape, pointer cancel/grab loss, disabling, or changing document, target, layer, mask target, color or `pointMapper` cancels and rolls back; a `brushPreset` change applies to the next stroke. See [raster-document.md](raster-document.md) for platform limits. |
| `CanvasViewState` | QObject (C++) | `centerX`, `centerY`, `zoom`, `rotation`, `minZoom`, `maxZoom`, `revision`; `setCenter(x, y)` (one pose change) | The shareable navigation pose; views bound to one instance are synchronized at their own sizes and DPR. Values are sanitized: zoom clamped, rotation normalized to (−180, 180], and non-finite pose values reset to safe defaults. A pose change a view did not make itself ends that view's `fitMode` refitting until `fitDocument()`. |
| `ToolController` | QObject (C++) | `document`, `activeTool` (`select`, `lasso`, `pan`, `rectangle`, `ellipse`, `pen`), read-only `effectiveTool`, `tools` (Tk.ToolShelf model; pass `Array.from(...)`), `state` (`idle`/`active`), `gestureActive`, `gestureOwner`, `lastOutcome`, `preview`, `selection`, `fillColor`, `strokeColor`, `strokeWidth`, `hitTolerance`; `begin(viewId,x,y[,device,modifiers,pressure])`, `update(...)`, `end(...)` (document coordinates), `cancel(reason)`, `escape()`, `lostGrab(viewId)`, `viewDetached(viewId)`, `pushTemporaryTool(id)`, `popTemporaryTool()`; `gestureCommitted(toolId,revision)`, `gestureCanceled(toolId,reason)` | One gesture at a time, owned by the view that began it. A gesture commits one edit (one revision and one undo step) or none. Lasso reports polygon hits with Shift-add and Alt containment and does not edit the document. Escape, lost grab, tool switch, document switch or reset, an external edit, a destroyed provider, a destroyed view or a second touch point each cancel it with the reason in `lastOutcome`, leaving the document untouched. |
| `CanvasSelectionModel` | QObject (C++) | `document`, `selectedIds`, `clear()`; `ToolController.selectionModel` binds controllers to it | One authoritative selected-ID list for multiple views of the same canvas document. Deduplicates IDs and prunes deleted, hidden or locked objects after edits. Controllers without a model keep local selection. See [canvas selection](canvas.md#document-adapter). |
| `HitTestService` | QObject (C++) | `document`, `tolerance` (px); `pointAt(x,y,zoom[,lockPolicy,maxResults])`, `regionIds(rect[,contain])` | Results `{objectId, layerId, part (fill/stroke/vertex/segment/imageTile), index, distance, locked}`, topmost first, ties broken by descending id. Lock policies are `skip` (the default), `report` and `block`. Hidden objects are skipped. |
| `CanvasOverlay` | QQuickPaintedItem (C++) | `surface`, `selectionColor`, `hoverColor`, `previewColor`, `previewFill`, `lineWidth`, `outlineLimit` (512) | Draws in view coordinates and changes nothing. CanvasView binds it to `color.accent`, `color.focus`, `color.hover` and `size.border`. |
| `SelectionOverlay` | Item | `surface` (`CanvasView`), document-space `selectionBounds` or four ordered `selectionCorners`, `handlesEnabled`, `accessibleName`, `handleSize`, `rotationHandleDistance`, `outlineWidth`, `autoScroll` (default true), `autoScrollMargin` (`size.dropEdge`), read-only `viewCorners`/`viewBounds`/`autoScrolling`; `autoScrollStep(seconds)`; `transformRequested(handle, startDocumentPoint, currentDocumentPoint, modifiers)`, `transformFinished(handle)`, `transformCanceled(handle)`, `transformNudgeRequested(handle, viewDx, viewDy, modifiers)`, `rotateStepRequested(degrees, modifiers)` | Presentation and gesture-reporting part of WP-041. It maps the selection through the current view pose, draws the frame, and places eight resize handles plus an accessible rotation handle. Arrow keys on a focused handle report its nudge; on the focusable `selectionMoveTarget` (no pointer handler) they report a `move` nudge of `size.controlSm` view pixels. A handle drag held at or past a view edge pans the surface each frame and re-reports the same view point as a new document point. The host validates, snaps, previews and commits geometry. Stable names `selectionOverlay`, `selectionFrame`, `selectionMoveTarget`, `handleTopLeft`, `handleTop`, `handleTopRight`, `handleRight`, `handleBottomRight`, `handleBottom`, `handleBottomLeft`, `handleLeft`, `handleRotate`. |
| `SpatialTransform` | QObject (C++ `CanvasTransformController`) | `document` (`CanvasDocumentAdapter`), `tools` (`ToolController` selection owner), optional `snapModel`, `viewState`, document-space `pivot` (defaults to selection center; editor state, reset on selection change); read-only `selectionBounds`, `previewBounds`, `previewCorners`, `rotation` (primary object's angle), `active`; `update(handle,start,current[,modifiers])`, `finish(handle)`, `cancel([handle])`, `nudge(handle,viewDx,viewDy[,modifiers])`, `rotateBy(degrees[,modifiers])`, `rotateTo(degrees)`, `setBounds(x,y,width,height)`, `resetPivot()`; `gestureFinished(committed)`, `gestureCanceled()` | Captures stable selected objects and a revision, previews document-space group move, eight-handle resize or rotation, and commits one canvas edit on finish; in a session that is one step in the shared history. Rejects locked, hidden, singular and stale groups. Snap queries exclude selected IDs on move/resize; Alt scales about center; Shift keeps a move on the nearest 45-degree document direction, keeps corner aspect or snaps rotation to 15 degrees; keyboard nudges use the view pose. Numeric `setBounds`/`rotateTo` commit the same edit as the handles, unsnapped. Requests are document commands (one undo step each) or shared-selection writes; hidden or locked objects are not selectable. `revealRequested` fires only for selections made elsewhere. See [layer tree model](canvas.md#layer-tree-model). |
| `MaskChannelPanel` | Box | `documentId`, host `channels` (up to 200 `id`, `name`, `kind`, `enabled`, `visible`, optional `linkedTo` records), `activeTargetId`; `requestTarget(id)`, `requestChannelState(id, property)`, `requestRelationship(id,relationship,targetId)`; `acknowledge(requestId,state,message)` | Emits `targetRequested`, `channelStateRequested` and `relationshipChangeRequested` with stable document/channel IDs. Target, visible, enabled and clipping controls are requests; snapshots change only when the host republishes them. Names `maskChannelPanel`, `maskChannelList`, `maskTarget_<id>`, `maskEnabled_<id>`, `maskVisible_<id>`, `maskRelationship_<id>`, `maskChannelStatus`. |
| `BrushPresetView` | Box | Host `presets` (`id`, `name`, `tags`, optional `previewColor`, `shape`, `available`), `selectedPresetId`, `searchText`, `previewSources` (presetId → image URL); `visiblePresets`, `requestPreset(id,action)`, `acknowledge(requestId,state,message)`, `providePreview(requestId,source)`, `pendingPreviewCount()` | Search and bounded list preview host presets; select and duplicate emit `presetRequested(requestId,presetId,action)`. The owner updates selection only through a new snapshot. Previews are lazy: each created row without a preview emits `previewRequested(requestId,presetId)`; a row that leaves the view (no cache buffer) or a view that closes emits `previewCanceled(requestId,presetId)`, and `providePreview` refuses canceled or unknown IDs. Hosts render previews with `TiledRasterDocument.brushPreviewDataUrl`. Names `brushPresetView`, `brushPresetSearch`, `brushPresetList`, `brushPresetPreview_<id>`, `brushPresetPreviewImage_<id>`, `brushPresetSelect_<id>`, `brushPresetStatus`. |
| `BrushDynamicsEditor` | Box | `brushId`, host `parameters` (`id`, `label`, `value`, `minimum`, `maximum`, `step`, `unit`), bounded `workingParameters` (at most 16 descriptors; finite range limited to ±1,000,000), `previewSource` (host sample stroke); `beginEdit`, `setParameterValue`, `previewEdit`, `commitEdit`, `cancelEdit`, `acknowledge` | `dynamicsEditRequested(brushId,requestId,phase,parameters)` with phase `begin`/`preview`/`commit`/`cancel`. Parameter edits clamp to host ranges; changes remain local preview values until a host accepts and republishes a committed snapshot. The host renders the working values with `TiledRasterDocument.brushPreviewDataUrl` into `previewSource` and commits them with `updateBrushPreset` (one undo step). Names `brushDynamicsEditor`, `brushDynamicsParameters`, `brushDynamicsSlider_<id>`, `brushDynamicsPreviewFrame`, `brushDynamicsPreview`, `brushDynamicsStatus`. |
| `AlignDistributePanel` | Item | `selectedIds` (stable decimal strings), `referenceMode` (`selection`, `keyObject`, `page`, or a value from `referenceOptions` such as `artboard`), `referenceOptions` (`[{value,text,available}]`; bind to `CanvasAlignmentModel.referenceOptions`), `keyObjectId`, `spacing`, `spacingStep`, `tooltip`; `operationRequested(operation, selectedIds, referenceMode, keyObjectId, spacing)` | Sends semantic align and distribution requests to the host's scene command layer. One selected object can align to a page or an artboard; selection/key-object references need two. Stable names `alignDistributePanel`, `alignDistributeTitle`, `alignReferenceMode`, `alignSectionTitle`, `alignLeftButton`, `alignHorizontalCenterButton`, `alignRightButton`, `alignTopButton`, `alignVerticalCenterButton`, `alignBottomButton`, `distributeSectionTitle`, `distributeHorizontalButton`, `distributeVerticalButton`, `distributeSpacing`. It does not compute or commit object geometry. |
| `CanvasAlignmentModel` | QObject (C++) | `document`, `selectionModel` (optional shared selection), `artboardId`, read-only `selectedIds` (decimal strings), `referenceOptions` (`[{value,text,available}]`), `lastError`; `apply(operation, selectedIds, referenceMode, keyObjectId, spacing)`; `applied(operation, changedObjects)` | Resolves panel requests against transformed canvas object bounds and commits all moves in one undoable edit. References: selection, key object, page, and the board named by `artboardId` (offered when the document has boards). Exact decimal IDs avoid QML number rounding; invalid, hidden or locked targets reject the whole command. See [alignment commands](canvas.md#alignment-commands). |
| `PathEditor` | Item overlay | `document` (`CanvasDocument`), `surface` (`CanvasView`), decimal-string `objectId`, zero-based `contourIndex`, `tooltip`; read-only `model` (its `CanvasPathEditModel`), `nodes`, `selectedNodeIds`; `insertAfterSelection()`, `deleteSelection()`, `splitSelection()`, `joinSelection()`, `setSelectionType(type)`; `editRejected(message)` | Shows the selected contour’s anchor and Bézier handles in view coordinates, previews drags, then commits one local-coordinate document edit on release. Escape cancels the preview. A click on an anchor selects it by stable node id (Shift adds or removes); selected anchors are solid, the others hollow; Delete removes the selection. Node requests go through the model and a refusal emits `editRejected` with its reason. Stable names `pathEditor`, `pathanchor_<index>`, `pathin_<index>`, `pathout_<index>`; see [vector authoring](vector-authoring.md). |
| `CanvasPathEditModel` | QObject (C++) | `document`, decimal-string `objectId`, read-only `nodes` (`[{id,contour,index,x,y,inX,inY,outX,outY,type,endpoint,selected}]`), `segments` (`[{id,contour,from,to}]`), `contours`, `lastError`, `selectedNodeIds`; `selectNode(id, extend)`, `clearSelection()`, `node(id)`, `insertNode(segmentId, t)`, `deleteNodes(ids)`, `setNodeType(type, ids)`, `splitAt(id)`, `join(a, b)`, `moveNodePart(id, part, x, y)` | Node-level editing of one path with stable node ids (`n<k>`) and segment ids (`s` + start node) that survive its own edits, undo/redo and edits made elsewhere (D-284). Insert splits a cubic exactly; `corner`/`smooth`/`symmetric` set handles; split opens or cuts a contour; join closes a contour or merges two. Each request is one undoable edit; degenerate results are refused with a reason. See [path editing](vector-authoring.md#path-editing). |
| `CanvasStyleModel` | QObject (C++) | `document`, `selectedIds`, `selectionModel` (`CanvasSelectionModel`), `gradientEditor` (`GradientEditorModel`), `strokeUnits`, read-only `selectionStyle`/`strokeStyle`/`fillStyle`, `patterns`, `blendModes`, `lastError`; `apply(selectedIds, style)` with `fill`, `stroke`, `strokeWidth`, `dash`, `cap`, `join`, `fillRule`, `alignment`, `miterLimit`, `opacity`, `blend`, `pattern`; `styleOf(ids)`, `applyStrokeStyle(descriptor)`, `applyFillStyle(descriptor)`, `applyPicker(selectedIds, picker)`, `applyGradient(selectedIds, gradient)`; `applied(changedObjects)`, `selectionStyleChanged()` | Commits appearance changes to selected visible, unlocked objects in one undoable edit. `strokeStyle`/`fillStyle` are the selection in StrokeStyleEditor/FillStyleEditor descriptor shapes, with `mixed` keys, and follow undo; the `apply*Style` methods apply exactly the fields a request's `changed` names (else those that differ from the published descriptor). With `selectionModel` the selection is the canvas's; stroke width is published and accepted in the chosen unit; a fill kind change restores each object's own earlier settings of that kind; kind `gradient` uses the object's earlier gradient or the `gradientEditor`'s (D-285). The typed methods copy the committed picker color or gradient into owned ICC-tagged fills on closed vector objects. A plain fill clears the typed one. Rejects unsupported fields; see [vector authoring](vector-authoring.md#style-binding). |
| `CanvasAppearanceModel` | QObject (C++) | `document`, decimal-string `objectId`, `selectionModel` (the key object is the first selected), read-only `effects` (AppearanceStack records), `paints` (fill, pattern, stroke in paint order), `editable`, `supportedKinds`, `lastError`; `addEffect(kind, parameters)`, `removeEffect(id)`, `moveEffect(id, index)`, `setEffectEnabled(id, enabled)`, `setEffectParameters(id, parameters)`, `effect(id)`; `applied()` | One object's ordered effect stack for `AppearanceStack`; each request is one undoable edit and the list follows every document change. `drop-shadow`, `gaussian-blur` and the nondestructive adjustment `color-adjust` render and export; other kinds are kept with their parameters and reported unsupported. `setEffectParameters` keeps the values a partial map leaves out. See [appearance](vector-authoring.md#appearance). |
| `CurveEditor` | Item | `knots` (host-owned `{id,x,y,label?,inHandle?,outHandle?}` records; `id` is a stable string and every point is normalized 0–1), `samples` (host-evaluated `{x,y}` display polyline), `selectedKnotId`, `tooltip`, `accessibleName`, `plotPadding`, `referenceLine` (default true: the identity diagonal; a time-axis host turns it off); `pixelAt(x,y)`, `normalizedAt(px,py)`, `selectKnot(id)`, `requestMove(id,x,y)`, `finishMove(id)`, `cancelMove(id)`; `knotSelected`, `movePreview`, `moveFinished`, `moveCancelled`, `tangentPreviewRequested(knotId,side,x,y)`, `tangentEditFinished(knotId,side,x,y)`, `tangentEditCanceled(knotId,side,x,y)` | Optional `inHandle` and `outHandle` are absolute normalized `{x,y}` positions in plot coordinates, never deltas from the knot. Present handles draw a connection line and focusable hit target. Tangent request signals carry stable knot ID, side (`"in"`/`"out"`) and normalized absolute handle position; cancel carries the original position. The host supplies and updates records; the control never mutates them. Root pointer ownership survives host knot-array replacement. Click selects without editing; Escape cancels the full active press. Arrow and accessible increment/decrement actions request and finish a small normalized edit. Stable names `curveEditor`, `curvePlot`, `curveKnot_<id>`, `curveTangent_<id>_<side>`. |
| `NavigatorView` | Item | `mainView` (`CanvasView`), `tooltip`, `keyboardStep` (0.1 of the visible extent per arrow; zoom factor 1 + step); read-only `preview`, `viewportOutline`; `fitPreview()`, `navigateAt(point)`, `panBy(fractionX, fractionY)`, `zoomBy(factor)` | Content-fitted, noninteractive preview of the same document on the same render providers with its own view state and coarse level of detail, plus a mapped outline of the main viewport. Press or drag centres the main view (`viewState.setCenter`, one pose change per step); arrows pan it, `+`/`-`, the wheel and accessible increase/decrease zoom it. Navigation never edits the document, selection or tools, the main pose never feeds back into the preview, and a page-fitted main view keeps the navigation. Focusable, with a focus ring. Stable names `navigatorView`, `navigatorPreview`, `navigatorViewportOutline`, `navigatorPointerArea`, `navigatorFocusRing`. Accessible Pane named by `tooltip`. |
| `CompareView` | `T.Control` plus `ImageCompareItem` | `beforeSource`, `afterSource` (local images or bounded PNG data URLs of the same region; resolutions may differ), `mode` (`split`, `blink`, `difference`), `split`, `showAfter`, `zoom`, `panX`, `panY`, `revisionKey`, read-only `lastError`, `displayedRevisionKey`, `stale`, `tooltip`; `setSourcePair(before,after,key)` | Mode buttons, split/zoom sliders, divider drag, blink click, difference pan, middle-button pan and wheel zoom use one viewport transform. Difference is an absolute encoded-sRGB channel delta, not a perceptual or proofing metric. `ImageCompareItem.setImages(QImage,QImage)` and `setImagePair(before,after,key)` support C++ hosts. A pair whose key is not the expected `revisionKey` is refused, so stale previews never mix revisions. objectNames `compareView`, `compareViewport`, `compareImage`, `compareSplitMode`, `compareBlinkMode`, `compareDifferenceMode`, `compareBlinkSource`, `compareSplit`, `compareZoom`, `compareStatus`. See [compare.md](compare.md). |
| `RenderSurfaceAdapter` | QObject (C++, uncreatable; `view.renderSurface`) | `requestedBackend`, `effectiveBackend`, `fallbackReason`, `resourceGeneration`, `tilePixels` (256), `maxTilesPerFrame` (24), `cacheLimitMiB` (96), `colorContext`, `stats`, `pending`; `simulateContextLoss([dropCaches])`, `invalidateAll()` | The PLT-10 boundary: document-space tiles at a power-of-two LOD, damage-driven invalidation, per-frame budget, byte-bounded LRU cache, backend fallback and resource generations. See [canvas.md](canvas.md#render-surface-adapter). |

## Transport

`Tk.TransportBar` is a themed `Tk.ToolBar` bound to `Tk.TransportModel`.
It shows the acknowledged sample position as timecode, frames, samples or
ticks through `TimeMap`. Play, stop, record, locate, loop and punch actions
submit requests to the model; the bar does not advance a media clock. Space,
R, L, P, Home and Left/Right are the keyboard actions. The bar reports engine
capability and device errors in its disabled control tooltips and status text.
Its stable parts are `transportPlay`, `transportStop`, `transportRecord`,
`transportLocateStart`, `transportLoop`, `transportPunch`, `transportTime` and
`transportStatus`. A real media engine must implement the `TransportAdapter`
interface; `TestTransportClock` is a deterministic contract fixture.

`Tk.DeviceSettingsPanel` displays host-enumerated devices and their supported
sample rates, buffer sizes, input layouts and output layouts. The host supplies
stable IDs plus active and pending configuration snapshots. Selector changes
emit `configurationChangeRequested(candidate)`; Apply emits a request ID and
snapshot, and the panel waits for `acknowledgeApply(requestId, state,
configuration, message)`. Accepted, rejected and error outcomes are explicit;
the host remains responsible for actual reconfiguration and updating both
snapshots. It does not infer latency, device availability or successful I/O.
A host that reports `engineState` `"lost"` or `"error"` (with `engineMessage`)
gets the message in the status line and a **Reconnect** action that re-requests
the same stable device ID once that device is available again; the panel never
substitutes another device. `Tk.AudioEngine` is a complete host: bind
`devices`, `activeConfiguration`, `state` and `stateMessage`, forward
`applyRequested` to `applyConfiguration` and `configurationApplied` to
`acknowledgeApply` (see [audio-engine.md](audio-engine.md) and
`examples/audio-session`).

| Type | Base | Host contract | Stable objectNames |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `DeviceSettingsPanel` | T.Control | `devices` (`[{id, name, available, unavailableReason, sampleRates, bufferSizes, inputLayouts, outputLayouts}]`), `activeConfiguration`, `pendingConfiguration`, optional `engineState` (`running`/`stopped`/`lost`/`error`) and `engineMessage`; read-only `applyState`/`applyMessage`/`recoveryAvailable`; `configurationChangeRequested(candidate)`, `applyRequested(requestId, configuration)`, `applyAcknowledged(requestId, state, activeConfiguration, message)`; `acknowledgeApply(requestId, "accepted"/"rejected"/"error", activeConfiguration, message)` | `deviceSettingsPanel`, `deviceSettingsActive`, `deviceSettingsPending`, `deviceSettingsDevice`, `deviceSettingsSampleRate`, `deviceSettingsBufferSize`, `deviceSettingsInputLayout`, `deviceSettingsOutputLayout`, `deviceSettingsStatus`, `deviceSettingsApply`. |

## Mixer and routing

Bound to a session's `Tk.AudioMixerModel` (`session.mixer`), the controls
change a real audio graph ([audio-mixer.md](audio-mixer.md)): operations go
by stable channel, parameter, send and port IDs, the mixer answers each at
once with acceptance or the reason for a refusal (a feedback loop, an
unavailable port), and gain, pan, width and send levels are the mixer's
shared `AudioParameterBinding` transactions. Nothing about routing or the
engine lives in a recycled delegate. Without a mixer, `ChannelStrip`,
`PanningControl` and `RoutingMatrix` keep the host request protocol: the
host validates and acknowledges.

| Type | Base | Contract and requests | Stable objectNames |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `PanningControl` | T.Control | Bound: `mixer` + `channelId` (or `panBinding`/`widthBinding`/`depthBinding`); `speakerLayout` (Mono has no width), `destinationLayout` (Quad or 5.1: spatial, the pad's y is depth over the drawn speakers; read-only `spatial`, `speakers`), `pad`. Canonical pan −1…+1, width 0…2 and depth −1 (rear)…+1 (front) at 0.01; sliders, percent fields and the pad share the transactions, and a pad drag is one undo step for both of its parameters. A gesture stays on the binding it began on: another `channelId` (or binding) or destruction cancels it there. Host: pan/width/depth parameter IDs, `panValue`/`stereoWidth`/`depthValue`, `parameterEditRequested(channelId, parameterId, gestureId, phase, value)` with `begin`/`preview`/`commit`/`cancel`, `acknowledgeEdit(gestureId, accepted, message)` | `panningControl`, `panSlider`, `panField`, `stereoWidthSlider`, `widthField`, `depthSlider`, `depthField`, `panPad`, `panPadHandle`, `panSpeaker`, `panRequestStatus`. |
| `ChannelStrip` | T.Control | Bound: `mixer` + `channelId` and the strip row (`channelName`, `channelKind`, `layout`, `muted`, `soloed`, `armed`, `soloSafe`, `implicitlyMuted`, `output`, `outputLayout`, `sends`, `groupNames`); `detail` adds pad, solo safe, hide, groups and routing; the strip watches its channel's meter (`mixer.watchMeter`) and shows `meterProblem` when a large mix cannot meter it; a surround bus has no panner; commands (`requestCommand("mute"/"solo"/"arm"/"soloSafe"/"hide", on)`) are mixer operations answered in `commandState`/`commandMessage`. Host: `gainParameterId`, `gainValue`/`gainSilent`, optional `AudioParameterMap` and shared `AudioMeterModel` + `meterChannel`; `gainEditRequested(...)` per fader gesture phase, `panEditRequested(...)`, `channelCommandRequested(channelId, requestId, command, enabled)`; `acknowledgeGainEdit`, `acknowledgeCommand` | `channelStrip`, `channelName`, `channelPeakMeter`, `channelMeterProblem`, `channelGainFader`, `channelPanning`, `channelMute`, `channelSolo`, `channelArm`, `channelSoloSafe`, `channelHide`, `channelGroups`, `channelRouting`, `channelRouteSummary`, `channelCommandStatus`, `channelSelectionArea`. |
| `ChannelRoutingPanel` | T.Control | `mixer`, `channelId`, `output`, `sends` (strip row); output selector over `mixer.destinations(channelId)`; per send a level `Knob`, pre/post toggle and removal; add send; `route(id)`, `addSend(busId)`, `removeSend(sendId)`, `setPreFader(sendId, on)` return acceptance and set `status`/`statusIsError` | `channelRoutingPanel`, `channelOutput`, `channelSendRow`, `channelSendLevel`, `channelSendPreFader`, `channelSendRemove`, `channelAddSend`, `channelRoutingStatus`. |
| `MixerView` | T.Control | `mixer`; host-owned `selectedChannelId` with `channelSelected(channelId)`; `bank` (the whole strips that fit the width, stretched to fill it exactly; `bankSize`, `bankCount`), `detail`; read-only `channelCount`, `held`. Left/Right select, Home/End jump, Page Up/Down change bank. While a gesture holds a channel, bank changes wait, the held strip stays in view through a reorder, and a hidden held channel keeps its strip until release | `mixerView`, `mixerBankLabel`, `mixerPreviousBank`, `mixerNextBank`, `mixerGroup`, `mixerGroupMute`, `mixerGroupSolo`, `mixerDetailToggle`, `mixerChannelList`, `mixerStripCell`, `mixerChannelStrip`, `mixerEmptyState`. A horizontal recycled `ListView` over `mixer.strips`, whose rows move instead of resetting, creates strips only for the visible range; scrolling snaps to strip edges, so no strip is ever cut at the edge. |
| `RoutingMatrix` | T.Control | Bound: `mixer` (rows from `mixer.routingInputs`, which move and update in place; destinations and connections from the mixer; each request is `setOutput`, answered at once). Host: `inputPorts`, `outputPorts` (`available`, `reason`), `connections`; `routeRequested(requestId, inputPortId, outputPortId)`, `acknowledgeRoute(requestId, "accepted"/"rejected"/"error", message)`. An unavailable destination is refused with its reason, without a request. Selection is a stable input ID (`selectedInputId`, `inputSelected(inputId)`, `selectInput`, `editInput`), re-found by ID after any row change; Up/Down, Home/End and Page Up/Down select, Return or Enter focuses the row's destination (Up/Down there propose routes), Escape returns to the rows | `routingMatrix`, `routingMatrixStatus`, `routingMatrixInputs`, `routingMatrixRow`, `routingInputLabel`, `routingDestination`, `routingMatrixEmpty`. A connection to a port that is no longer offered stays shown as unavailable, never as another port or disconnected. |

## Plugin host controls

The browser and rack render registry and instance snapshots supplied by an app.
They issue request IDs and expose host acknowledgments; neither claims that a
plugin is installed, loaded, processed, or safely isolated. The host publishes
the resulting descriptor/chain snapshots after accepting an operation.

| Type | Base | Main properties and requests | Notes and stable objectNames |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `PluginBrowser` | T.Control | `host` (`AudioPluginHost`, e.g. `session.plugins`: rows from `browserPlugins`, scan status from its registry, **Add** runs `rackAction("add")` on `targetChannelId` and is acknowledged with the answer), `targetChannelId`, `plugins` (`pluginId`, name/vendor/category/kind, supported layouts, availability, scan state, reason, presets), `query`, `kindFilter`, `categoryFilter`, `scanState`, `scanMessage`; `addRequested(requestId, pluginId, presetId)`, `presetPreviewRequested(requestId, pluginId, presetId)`; `acknowledgeAdd(requestId, state, message)` | Search/category/kind filter registry snapshots. Available entries can be dragged to a rack or explicitly added; unavailable/missing entries remain visible and cannot be added. Preset preview is a separate host job request. Names `pluginBrowser`, `pluginSearch`, `pluginKindFilter`, `pluginCategoryFilter`, `pluginBrowserStatus`, `pluginResults`, `pluginResult_<pluginId>`, `pluginAdd_<pluginId>`. |
| `PluginRack` | T.Control | `host` (`AudioPluginHost`, e.g. `session.plugins`: `instances` follows `host.instances(channelId)` on every host revision, and each request runs `host.rackAction()` and is acknowledged with its answer), ordered host `instances` (`instanceId`, `pluginId`, state/bypass/latency/error, editor availability and stable parameter descriptors); `channelId`; `pluginActionRequested(requestId, instanceId, action, payload)`, `pluginActionAcknowledged(...)`; `acknowledgeAction(requestId, state, message)`, `requestAdd`, `requestReorder`, `requestBypass`, `requestRemove`, `requestEditor`, `requestParameterEdit`, `requestReload` | Drag or arrow buttons request reordering; bypass, remove, editor, reload and parameter actions carry stable instance/parameter IDs. Acknowledgment updates request status only; rows and bypass state continue to reflect the last host snapshot until the host republishes. A parameter row that carries a `binding` (`AudioParameterBinding`, from `AudioSession.plugins.instances(channelId)`) draws a `Knob` on that automation parameter (row height `controlLg`); a `failed` or `missing` instance shows **Reload**. Host side: [audio-plugins.md](audio-plugins.md). Names `pluginRack`, `pluginRackStatus`, `pluginRackListFrame`, `pluginRackInstances`, `pluginRackDropTarget`, `pluginSlot_<instanceId>`, `pluginBypass_<instanceId>`, `pluginParameter_<parameterId>`, `pluginReload_<instanceId>`. |

## Audio measurement

| Type | Base | Main properties and actions | Behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AudioMeterModel` | QObject (C++) | `levels` (one `{channel, name, peakDbfs, rmsDbfs, displayPeakDbfs, heldPeakDbfs, maxPeakDbfs, clipped, silent, invalidSamples}` map per channel), `channelCount`, `metric`, `channelLayout`, `sampleRate`, `blockMilliseconds`, `rmsWindowMilliseconds`, `holdMilliseconds`, `fallDbPerSecond`, `integrationWindow`, `measuredSeconds`, `droppedBlocks`, `lastError`; `submitLevels(peaks,rms,elapsedMilliseconds)`, `submitInterleaved(samples,channels,elapsedMilliseconds)`, `resetClip()`; C++ `configure`, `consume(AudioMeterBlock)`, `publish` | Sample peak per 10 ms block and RMS over a sliding window (300 ms), dBFS floored at −120 with a separate silence flag. The engine path (`AudioEngine.outputMeter`) advances hold and fall by each block's audio duration, so snapshots do not depend on the GUI refresh rate. A sample magnitude of 1 or more latches clip until `resetClip()`, which also clears maxima and invalid counts. `submit*` are the fixture/offline path; see [audio-measurement.md](audio-measurement.md). |
| `AudioParameterMap` | QObject (C++) | `descriptor` (`taper`: `linear`, `logarithmic` or `gainDb`; `minimum`, `maximum`, `unityPosition` for gain); `positionFor(value,silent)`, `valueAt(position)` → `{value,silent}`, `lastError` | Provider-owned conversion between canonical values and control travel. Gain maps an explicit silent state to position 0, finite floor above it and 0 dB to the configured unity position. Knob/Fader gestures can use the same mapping; see [audio-measurement.md](audio-measurement.md). |
| `AudioLoudnessModel` | QObject (C++) | `algorithm` (`ITU-R BS.1770-4`), `unit` (`LUFS`), `channelLayout`, `channelWeights`, `sampleRate`, `momentaryLufs`, `shortTermLufs`, `integratedLufs`, `maxMomentaryLufs`, `maxShortTermLufs`, `measuredSeconds`, `gatedBlocks`, `droppedBlocks`, `historyMetric` (`short-term`/`momentary`), `historyMetricId`, `historyWindow`, `historySeconds`, `history` (`{time, value}`), `summary`, `lastError`; `reset()`, `report()`, `submitInterleaved(samples,channels,rate,layout)` | K-weighted momentary (400 ms), short-term (3 s) and gated integrated loudness (−70 LUFS absolute, −10 LU relative) from the engine's meter blocks (`AudioEngine.outputLoudness`); NaN until measurable, −inf for digital silence; bounded memory for any duration. `report()` is `qindatk.loudness-report/1` with provenance; not a certified meter. |
| `AudioParameterBinding` | QObject (C++) | `session`, `parameterId`; `valid`, `name`, `unit`, `taper`, `minimum`, `maximum`, `step`, `silenceFloor`, `staticValue`, `resetValue`, `unityPosition`, `mapper`, `value`, `silent`, `automated`, `valueText`, `automationMode`, `automationState`, `touched`, `writing`, `gestureActive`, `lastResult`, `lastError`; `begin`, `preview`, `commit`, `cancel`, `setAutomationMode`, `canonical`, `format`; `transactionFinished(result, value, silent)` | Binds Knob/Fader gestures to one `AudioSession` automation parameter the engine reads by ID. While a control holds it, the engine plays the gesture value in every mode. A commit is one history step: the writing pass (`recorded`), or a static-value edit when the mode is off or the lane has no points (`static`); read mode over a curve stores nothing (`live`); cancel stores nothing. A dB gain's range minimum is exact silence. See [audio-measurement.md](audio-measurement.md). |

## Edit timeline

A virtualized multitrack editor over `Tk.TimelineModel`; the exact-time,
transaction, snapping, virtualization, media and transfer contracts are in
[edit-timeline.md](edit-timeline.md). Every document change is a model edit
(one undo step per accepted gesture); delegates hold no placement state.

| Type | Base | Main properties and actions | Signals | objectNames and behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `EditTimeline` | T.Control | `model`, `mediaProvider`, `selectionModel` (internal fallback), `transportModel`, `clipboard` (internal fallback), `playheadTicks`, `snapEnabled`, `snapGrid` (`frames`, `seconds`, `samples`, `none`), `rippleMode`, `displayMode`, `dropFrame`, `headerWidth` (`size.labelWidth × 2`), `snapPixels` (`space.md`), `currentTrackId`, `currentClipId`, `tooltip`, `statusText`; read-only `viewport`, `lanes`, `markerLane`, `editRange`, `ruler`, `selectedClipIds`, `effectivePlayheadTicks`, `dragging`; `seek`, `selectClip`, `moveCurrentTrack`, `moveCurrentClip`, `nudgeSelection`, `trimSelection`, `splitAtPlayhead`, `deleteSelection`, `reorderCurrentTrack`, `copySelection`, `pasteAtPlayhead`, `openTimingEditor`, `openRangeEditor`, `zoomBy`, `cancelActiveEdit` | `seekRequested(ticks)` (owner sets `playheadTicks`, or the transport acknowledges), `clipActivated(clipId)`, `editRejected(reason)`, `editCommitted(transactionId)` | `timelineCorner`, `timelinePlayheadReadout`, `timelineRuler`, `timelineLoopRange`, `markerLane`, `timelineTrackHeaders`, `timelineLanes`, `timelineVerticalScrollBar`, `timelineHorizontalScrollBar`, `timelineTimingAnchor`, `timelineFocusRing`, `timelineTimingEditor`. Accessible Pane whose description names the current track, clip, selection and keys; the lanes are an accessible List of clips and the header column a Grouping of tracks. Keyboard map in [edit-timeline.md](edit-timeline.md#views). |

## Tempo and automation

Tempo/meter and parameter automation lanes on the edit timeline's time axis
(WP-049). Contracts, validation, modes and the engine schedule are in
[tempo-automation.md](tempo-automation.md). Lanes share an `EditTimeline`'s
`viewport`; every change is a model transaction and one undo step.

| Type | Base | Main properties and actions | Signals | objectNames and behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TempoLane` | Item | `viewport`, `tempoModel`, `currentEventId`, `snapEnabled`, `snapGrid` (`beat`, `bar`, `none`), `playheadTicks`, `meterBandHeight` (`size.row`), `tooltip`; read-only `rows`, `tempoRows`, `meterRows`, `currentEvent`, `lowBpm`, `highBpm`, `dragging`; `xOf(row)`, `yForBpm`, `bpmAtY`, `select`, `step`, `moveCurrent(steps, bars)`, `changeValue(delta)`, `addAt(kind, ticks)`, `removeCurrent`, `openEditor`, `cancelDrag` | `eventActivated(eventId)`, `editRejected(reason)` | `tempoLane`, `tempoMeterBand`, `tempoBarGrid` (bar numbers and beat ticks), `tempoGraph` (step line, danger colour while a preview is invalid), `tempoEvent_<id>`, `meterEvent_<id>`, `tempoLabel_<id>`, `meterLabel_<id>`, `tempoFocus_<id>`, `tempoPlayhead`, `tempoLaneError`, `tempoLanePointer`, `tempoEventEditor`. Accessible List of ListItems. Horizontal drag moves an event on the beat grid (meter changes by bars), vertical drag on a tempo event or segment changes BPM (display range frozen while dragging); double-click adds (meter band: meter, tempo band: tempo) or opens numeric entry. Keys: Left/Right choose, Alt+Left/Right move a beat (Shift: bar), Up/Down ±1 BPM (Shift ±10) or ±1 beat per bar, Delete, Return/F2, T and M add at the playhead, Escape cancels a drag. |
| `TempoEventEditor` | Popover | `tempoModel`, `eventId`, `kind`, `errorText`; `openEvent(id)`, `apply()` | `edited(result)` | `tempoEventEditor`, `tempoPositionField` (bar.beat.tick; disabled for the first events), `tempoBpmField`, `tempoNumeratorField`, `tempoDenominatorField`, `tempoEditorError`, `tempoEditorCancel`, `tempoEditorApply`. Untouched fields keep exact values; Apply is one revision-guarded `edit`. |
| `AutomationLane` | Item | `viewport`, `model` (`AutomationModel`), `parameterId`, `currentPointId`, `snapEnabled` (false), `snapGrid`, `snapPixels`, `playheadTicks`, `displayMode`, `dropFrame`, `tooltip`; read-only `parameter`, `writeState`, `visiblePoints`, `currentPoint`, `knots`, `plotPadding`, `dragging`; `addAt(x, y)`, `removeCurrent`, `nudgeValue(fraction)`, `nudgeTime(steps)`, `cycleInterpolation`, `cancelSession` | `editRejected(reason)` | `automationLane`, `automationCurveEditor` (a `CurveEditor` with `referenceLine: false`; knots `curveKnot_<id>`, handles of the current point `curveTangent_<id>_<side>`), `automationWriteBand` (the active record pass), `automationModeBadge` (parameter · mode · writing/latched), `automationValueReadout`, `automationPlayhead`, `automationLanePointer`, `automationPointEditor`. Knot and handle drags use CurveEditor's gestures; an unmoved coordinate stays the stored exact value. Drag near a segment moves both ends; double-click adds. Keys: arrows on a point nudge it, Left/Right choose, Alt+Left/Right move a grid step, Up/Down ±1% of the range, I cycles interpolation, Delete, Return/F2. |
| `AutomationPointEditor` | Popover | `model`, `timeline`, `parameterId`, `pointId`, `displayMode`, `dropFrame`, `errorText`; `openPoint(parameterId, pointId)`, `apply()` | `edited(result)` | `automationPointEditor`, `automationTimeField`, `automationValueField`, `automationInterpolation` (Segmented step/linear/curve), `automationEditorError`, `automationEditorCancel`, `automationEditorApply`. Apply is one revision-guarded `setPoint`. |
| `TempoMapModel` | QObject (C++) | `timeMap`, `timeline`, `events` (rows `{id, kind, tick, microsecondsPerQuarter, bpm, bpmText, numerator, denominator, valueText, initial, position, positionTicks, preview, label}`), `revision`, `minimumBpm`, `maximumBpm`, `capabilities`, history; `beginEdit`/`updateEdit`/`commitEdit`/`cancelEdit`/`applyEdit`, `undo`/`redo`, `toJson`/`loadJson`, `formatPosition`/`parsePosition`, `formatBpm`/`parseBpm`, `snapTick`/`stepTick`, `tempoAt`/`meterAt` | `eventsChanged`, `revisionChanged`, `committed`, `rejected`, … | The only writer of its TimeMap's tempo segments. |
| `AutomationModel` | QObject (C++) | `parameterIds`, `revision`, `returnTicks`, `transportRunning`, `capabilities`, history; `addParameterMap`, `points`, `pointData`, `valueAt`, `sampleLane`, `formatValue`/`parseValue`, transactions, `setModeName`, `writeState`, `setModeAvailability`, `setTransportState`, `setTransportTicks`, `beginGestureAt`/`recordGestureAt`/`endGestureAt`, `toJson`/`loadJson` | `pointsChanged(parameterId)`, `modeChanged`, `writeStateChanged`, `committed`, … | Reference automation engine for read/write/touch/latch. |
