Mock: `schedule`, `positionSample`, `playing`, `blockFrames`, `lastBlock`; `play`, `stop`, `locate`, `process`, `valuesAt`, `render`, `touch`/`move`/`release` | `published`; `processed` | Snapshots are thread-safe and hold no GUI clock; the mock opens no device. |

## Audio controls

Audio controls exchange canonical parameter values and immutable measurement
snapshots with their owner. They do not calculate gain laws, sample peaks,
RMS, loudness, peak hold or decay. Bound to engine models (`binding:
Tk.AudioParameterBinding`, `meter: Tk.AudioMeterModel`, `loudness:
Tk.AudioLoudnessModel`), they read and change the real engine. Otherwise the
host begins one parameter transaction on `gestureStarted`, applies previews on
`valuePreview`, commits on `gestureCommitted` and restores on
`gestureCanceled`. Each gesture is exactly one start/commit pair: a relative
pointer drag, a held arrow key, a wheel burst (ended by
`Tk.Theme.motion.gestureIdle` of quiet or by other input), a numeric entry,
Home/End or a reset. A double-click reset joins the open pointer gesture.

| Type | Host inputs | Requests and behavior | Accessibility and limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Knob` | `parameterId`, `value`, `from`, `to`, `defaultValue`, `stepSize`, `unit`, `taper`, `automationState`, `bipolar`, optional `mapper` or `binding`; read-only `gestureOpen` | Drag (vertical, relative, 200 px per travel; Shift ×0.1, Alt ×0.01), wheel burst, held arrows (Shift coarse, Alt fine), Home/End, Return reset, Escape cancel, double-click reset and `Tk.NumberField` entry are one gesture each. `mapper.positionFor(value, silent)` and `mapper.valueAt(position)` own nonlinear mapping; with `binding` the knob uses the engine parameter's range, unit, taper, reset value and mapper and never assigns `value`. | Slider role, numeric value, name and description (value, taper, automation state) and an editable numeric field. Names `knobDial`, `knobField`, `knobValue`. Signals: `gestureStarted(value,silent)`, `valuePreview(value,silent)`, `gestureCommitted(value,silent)`, `gestureCanceled(value,silent)`. |
| `Fader` | Same parameter descriptor plus `silent`, `silenceMark`, `unityValue`, `scaleMarks`, orientation, optional `mapper` or `binding` | Vertical by default. Pointer drags are relative (never jump on press); wheel, keyboard, reset and numeric entry use the same gesture path as Knob. Scale marks and the unity mark sit where the mapper puts those values under the thumb centre, and follow a mapping that changes after creation (a binding whose parameter arrives late); a label that would overlap another is dropped, its tick stays (unity and the range ends first); with a silence floor the lowest mark reads −∞. `silent` represents negative infinity explicitly, never a finite attenuation. | Slider role and numeric field alternative; value, unity, taper and automation state in the accessible description. Names `faderTrack`, `faderThumb`, `faderScaleLabel`, `faderUnityMark`, `faderField`, `faderValue`. |
| `AudioMeter` | `meter` (`Tk.AudioMeterModel`) or provider `levels` rows `{channel, name, peakDbfs, rmsDbfs, displayPeakDbfs, heldPeakDbfs, maxPeakDbfs, clipped, silent}`; `metric`, `unit`, `integrationWindow`, `minimumDbfs`/`maximumDbfs`, `warningDbfs` (−6), `scaleMarks` | Draws channel names, RMS bar, display peak (warning colour at or above `warningDbfs`), held-peak line and readout, a labelled dBFS scale row aligned with the bars (end labels aligned inward, colliding labels dropped, unit at the right) and the clip latch. **Reset clip** calls `meter.resetClip()` when bound and emits `clipResetRequested()`. The control never resets its own snapshot or fabricates decay. | Accessible per-channel summary: peak, RMS, hold, max, clip, metric and window. Names `meterRow`, `channelTrack`, `levelValue`, `meterScale`, `meterScaleLabel`, `meterScaleUnit`, `meterClipReset`. |
| `LoudnessHistory` | `loudness` (`Tk.AudioLoudnessModel`) or provider `samples` (`time`, `value`), `metricId`, `unit`, `integrationWindow`, `channelLayout`, `targetProfile`, range, `scaleStep`, optional target band | Plots readings against their times with a labelled level scale; bound, it shows the model's history and an M/S/I readout, **Reset history** calls `loudness.reset()`, and **Export report** also emits `reportExported(report)`. `resetHistoryRequested()` and `exportRequested(...)` remain host requests. | Accessible summary names metric, latest reading, unit, window, layout and, when bound, the BS.1770 summary. Unbound readings are never labelled LUFS by the control. Names `historyPlot`, `historyScaleLabel`, `loudnessReadout`, `historyReset`, `historyExport`. |

The fixture `examples/audio-controls/Main.qml` binds its Knob and Fader to one
session gain parameter and its frequency Knob to a logarithmic parameter, and it
feeds the meter and loudness models with generated test tones. The meters on a
running device are `AudioEngine.outputMeter` and `outputLoudness`.

## Video controls

| Type | Base | Properties | Signals | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `CaptionEditor` | T.Control | `sourceId`, `trackId`, host `cues` (stable ID, exact `inTime`/`outTime`, normalized display geometry, text, language, style ID), `timeMarks`, exact paired `moveTargets`, host `styles`, selected cue/text/style/language, `warningText`, `editAcknowledgement` (`status`, `message`), `editingEnabled`, `tooltip` | `cueSelectionRequested`, `cueEditPreviewRequested`, `cueEditCommitRequested`, `cueEditCanceled`, `navigationRequested`, `exportRequested` | Timing drags map to exact host time marks or paired move targets without time arithmetic. Text is accessible separately from video pixels. `CaptionTrackModel` owns cue overlap, sidecar codec, persistence and undo; `CaptionFrameRenderer` burns its supported styles into decoded frames. `VideoFinishing` burns cues into program frames and embeds them as a subtitle stream in exports. objectNames `captionEditor`, `captionTimelineCanvas`, `captionTextEditor`, `captionStyleChoice`, `captionEditorStatus`. |
| `CaptionTrackModel` | QML model | Stable cue IDs, decimal millisecond strings, text/language/style/lane; `editorCues`, exact `timeMarks`/`moveTargets`, selected cue fields, request acknowledgment, JSON `storagePath`, `warnings`/`warningText`, `formats` | `previewCueEdit`, `commitCueEdit`, `cancelCueEdit`, `selectCue`, `undo`, `splitCue`, `mergeCues`, `shiftCues`, `importWebVtt`, `importSrt`, `importEmbedded`, `exportWebVtt`, `exportSrt`, `saveToFile`, `loadFromFile` | Reference backend for CaptionEditor. Validates positive intervals and same-lane overlap; split/merge/shift are single undo steps. WebVTT keeps the track language (`Language:` header) and per-cue languages (`<lang>` spans); SRT keeps timing and text; JSON keeps everything; `importEmbedded` reads a Matroska SubRip stream with its language. `warnings` flags overlaps, short cues, reading rate and line length. Exact times are decimal strings across QML. See [caption-rendering.md](caption-rendering.md). |
| `CaptionFrameRenderer` | QML model / C++ renderer | Host-supplied `QImage`, `CaptionTrackModel`, exact decimal `timeMilliseconds`; read-only rendered PNG data URL, active stable cue IDs, exact time and error; `renderFixture` is a demo helper | `renderFrame(frame, model, timeMilliseconds)`, `saveRenderedFrame(path = defaultOutputPath)` | Burns committed cues active on the half-open interval `[inMs, outMs)` into host decoded pixels. Uses the model's supported `default`/`emphasis` styles and lane placement; it does not add opacity or style fields missing from CaptionTrackModel. PNG saving uses atomic replacement. objectName is fixture-owned. |
| `CaptionSequenceExporter` | QML model / C++ exporter | Host-supplied `QImage` values, `CaptionTrackModel`, matching ordered exact decimal-millisecond strings and a new target directory; read-only output directory, timing manifest, frame paths/count and error | `exportFrames(frames, model, times, targetDirectory)` returns `{ok, outputDirectory, manifestPath, framePaths, frameCount, error}`; `exportFixtureSequence` is demo-only | Caps output at 10,000 frames. Burns each frame through `CaptionFrameRenderer`, writes numbered PNGs and exact input timestamps plus active cue IDs to `timing.json`; stages beside the target and publishes the completed directory by rename. Existing targets are rejected to preserve atomic completion. The manifest is suitable for downstream sequencing; this does not encode video or mux audio. |
| `ColorWheels` | T.Control | `gradeId`, `parameterRevision`, host `wheels` (stable ID, label, current x/y, declared numeric ranges and steps), `selectedWheelId`, `editAcknowledgement`, `editingEnabled`, `statusText`, `tooltip` | `wheelSelectionRequested`, `wheelPreviewRequested`, `wheelEditFinished`, `wheelEditCanceled`, `resetRequested` | Neutral parameter pads visualize host values and ranges. Drag sends preview then finish; Escape cancels; `requestWheelValue(wheelId, x, y)` (numeric entry) and arrow-key nudges clamp to the host range and emit the same preview/finish signals as a drag; Home requests reset. Each pad shows its label and x, y values in a band above the status caption; `wheelGeometry(index)` returns the `centerX`, `centerY`, `radius` that painting, hit testing and drags share. Binds directly to `ColorGradeEditSession.wheels` and its `previewWheel`/`finishWheel`/`resetWheel`. Host still owns parameter ranges, linkage, transactions and undo; `ColorGradeProcessor` supplies the documented linear-sRGB pixel transform. Wheel colors are themed affordances. objectNames `colorWheels`, `colorWheelCanvas`, `colorWheelsStatus`. |
| `ColorGradeProcessor` | C++ image processor | `process(QImage, ColorGradeParameters, ColorContextSnapshot)` with exposure EV and lift/gamma/gain opponent-wheel x/y parameters | Returns tagged output pixels, generation and explicit error | Bounded synchronous CPU transform in linear sRGB. Hosts supply ICC-tagged decoded frames and own undo/session transactions. See [color.md](color.md). |
| `ColorGradePreviewModel` | QML QObject | `beforeImageUrl`, `afterImageUrl`, `beforeScopes`, `afterScopes`, `errorMessage` | `render(parameters)` | Generates a deterministic sRGB bar fixture, runs the CPU grade processor and returns data-URL images plus bounded `VideoScopesModel` snapshots. This fixture adapter does not decode media or export a clip. |
| `ColorGradeEditSession` | QML QObject | Host supplies a tagged `sourceImage` or C++ `setFrame(clipId, frameId, image, context)`; `scope` (`FrameGrade` default, `ClipGrade`, `ProgramGrade`); exposes committed/transient `parameters` (C++ `committedParameters()`, `renderContext()`), `wheels` descriptors for ColorWheels, `exposure`, `gradeSpace`, processed `QImage`, before/after data URLs and scope snapshots | `beginGesture(id)`, `preview(id, parameters)`, `finishGesture(id)`, `cancelGesture(id)`, `previewWheel(id, wheel, x, y)`, `finishWheel(id, wheel, x, y)`, `setWheelValue(wheel, x, y)`, `setExposure(ev)`, `resetWheel(wheel)`, `undo()`, `redo()`, `saveJson()`, `loadJson(json)` | One edit transaction and undo step per gesture; wheel drags and numeric entry build identical parameters. Frame/context changes cancel an open gesture; leaving the `scope` starts a neutral grade, a new frame inside it keeps grade and history; a `ProgramGrade` previews in the program's pipeline context; rejected transforms remain visible in `errorMessage`. `VideoFinishing` carries its grade into program playback and export. See [color.md](color.md#grade-gestures). |
| `MediaFrameView` | QQuickItem (C++) | `player` (`Tk.MediaPlayer`); read-only `displayedFrame`, `displayedFrameKey`, `frameRect`, `frameTransfer` (`none`, `gpu`, `software`), `textureUploads` | `displayedChanged`, `transferChanged` | Draws the player's presented frame letterboxed at the source display aspect. It keeps one shared copy of the frame; its `QSGImageNode` owns the texture made from it and replaces it only when a new frame is presented. `displayedFrame` names the frame whose pixels are shown. See [media-playback.md](media-playback.md#frame-transfer). |
| `MediaPlayer` | QML QObject (C++) | `resolver`, `catalog`, source `resourceId` or program `timeline`, `frameWidth`/`frameHeight`, `audioChannels`, `useProxies`, `maxPreviewWidth`/`maxPreviewHeight`, `finishing` (`Tk.VideoFinishing`: grade and captions on presented and exported frames); read-only `transport` (`Tk.TransportModel`), `state`, `lastError`, `mediaInfo`, `programInfo`, exact decimal `frameCount`, frame-rate and timebase parts, `pixelAspect`, `proxyActive`, `presentedFrame`/`presentedTicks`/`presentedFrameKey`/`presentedTimecode`/`presentedSources`, `missingResourceIds`, `frameError`, `presentedCorrupt`, `targetFrame`, `pending`, `statistics`; `seekFrame`, `stepFrame`, `seekTicks`, `play`, `stop`, `togglePlayback`, `frameStartTicks`, `frameAtTicks`, `exportJob(outputPath, label)`, `reload()` | `sourceChanged`, `stateChanged`, `programChanged`, `frameChanged`, `statisticsChanged` | One `MediaPlaybackEngine` (the transport authority) plus its `TransportModel`. Commands return transport request IDs; `presented*` always describes the frame on screen and may trail `targetFrame` while `pending`. Rebuilds on relinks, proxy switches and committed timeline revisions. See [media-playback.md](media-playback.md). |
| `MediaCatalog` | QAbstractListModel (C++) | `resolver`, `binFilter`, `searchText`, `proxyDirectory`; read-only `bins`, `count`, `totalCount`, `lastError`, `jobs`; roles `assetId`, `resourceId`, `label`, `caption`, `binId`, `binName`, `tags`, `uri`, `fileName`, `availability`, `online`, `metadataState`, `mediaType`, `codec`, `resolution`, `frameRate`, `frameCount`, `duration`, `sourceTimecode`, `audio`, `proxyStatus`, `proxyResourceId`, `proxyActive`, `proxyAvailable`, `error`, `metadata`; `importMedia`, `addAsset`, `removeAsset`, `addBin`, `renameBin`, `removeBin`, `moveToBin`, `setLabel`, `setTags`, `relink`, `relinkMissingFrom`, `createProxy`, `setProxyActive`, `refresh`, `asset`, `assetIdForResource`, `contentMap`, `loadContent`, `generateTestSignal` | `assetChanged(assetId)`, `proxyChanged(resourceId)`, `jobFinished(jobId, kind, assetId, ok, error)`, `binsChanged`, `filterChanged`, `countChanged` | Virtual bins over resolver resources: organizing never changes the resource ID sequences reference, relinking changes only the location, and proxies (same frame count and rate) replace preview pictures only. `NodeGraph.flowMode` can also render host-owned edge `flow` values and node
`inFlow`/`outFlow` totals directly; its default remains off for existing hosts.
## Video edit surfaces

The following controls edit sequences. Stable clip/edit/angle/transition IDs
stay strings, and canonical frame/tick positions cross the QML boundary only
as decimal strings. In host mode they consume host-owned snapshots and their
preview, cut, parameter edit, cancel and commit signals are requests; source
bounds, linked track policy, synchronization, evaluation and persistence
remain in the host. Bound to `Tk.SequenceEditor` (`editor`) or
`Tk.MulticamModel` (`multicam`) they perform real `TimelineModel` edits
instead and do not emit the request signals (see
[sequence-edits.md](sequence-edits.md)).

| Type | Snapshot and actions | Requests | Behavior and limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TrimView` | optional `editor` (`Tk.SequenceEditor`), `side` (`left`/`right` for ripple, slip and slide), `edit` (stable edit/clip IDs, left/right resource IDs, exact head/tail ticks, legal-handle and missing-media flags), `assetProvider`, `document`, `viewId`, rational frame rate (read-only `assetRateNumerator`/`assetRateDenominator`: the tick rate of filmstrip requests, the timeline timebase with `editor`), `mode` (`roll`, `ripple`, `slip`, `slide`), signed decimal-string `deltaTicks`, host preview operation/status and error/status text | `previewRequested(requestId, editId, mode, deltaTicks)`, `commitRequested(requestId, operationId)`, `cancelRequested(requestId, operationId)`, `frameSeekRequested(editId, side, timeTicks)` | Shows outgoing/incoming head and tail frames through two `AssetFilmstrip`s, plus explicit offline source and insufficient-handle states. Preview must be host-acknowledged before Commit enables. Every exact delta stays a string; unavailable handles prevent preview for roll/ripple. With `editor`, the snapshot is `editor.trimEdit` (following the live preview), Preview/Commit/Cancel run `previewTrim`/`commitTrim`/`cancelTrim` as one TimelineModel session, a move needs handle only in its own direction, and offline media is shown without blocking (edits keep recorded source offsets). objectNames `trimView`, `trimOutgoingFrames`, `trimIncomingFrames`, `trimOutgoingMissing`, `trimIncomingMissing`, `trimMode`, `trimSide`, `trimDeltaTicks`, `trimPreview`, `trimCommit`, `trimCancel`, `trimStatus`. |
| `MulticamView` | optional `multicam` (`Tk.MulticamModel`), `angleColumns` (cards per row, default 2; more rows scroll), read-only `assetRateNumerator`/`assetRateDenominator` (the timeline timebase with `multicam`), `angles` (`angleId`, label, `resourceId`, exact `syncOffsetTicks`, frame ID/time, availability, active state), `assetProvider`, `document`, exact `playheadTicks`, rational frame rate, host-confirmed `activeAngleId`, pending request/status/error | `angleSwitchRequested(requestId, angleId, atTicks)`, `seekAngleRequested(angleId, frameId, atTicks)`, `switchAcknowledged(requestId, accepted, activeAngleId, message)` | Synchronized angle grid uses provider-backed thumbnails, marks the host-confirmed program angle, and disables missing or pending choices. A click requests a cut at the supplied exact playhead time; sync mapping and cut creation belong to the host. With `multicam`, angles, playhead and program angle come from the model and a click is an immediate frame-exact `switchAngle` transaction, reported through `switchAcknowledged`. objectNames `multicamView`, `multicamSyncReadout`, `multicamAngleScroll`, `multicamAngleGrid`, `multicamAngle_<angleId>`, `multicamFrame_<angleId>`, `multicamStatus`. |
| `TransitionEditor` | optional `editor` (`Tk.SequenceEditor`), `transition` (stable transition/clip IDs, exact head/tail handle ticks, optional `missingMedia`, parameter descriptors `{id,label,valueText,editable}`), exact positive `durationTicks`, `type` (`dissolve`, `wipe`, `dip`), `alignment` (`start`, `center`, `end`), preview state and status/error | `parameterEditRequested(requestId, transitionId, parameter, value)`, `previewRequested(requestId, transitionId)`, `cancelPreviewRequested(requestId, transitionId)` | Validates displayed duration/alignment against decimal-string handle counts without converting ticks to a QML Number. Parameter fields emit `parameter:<id>` requests with string values; the host validates accepted ranges and updates the next snapshot. Missing media, insufficient handles and host errors remain visible. With `editor`, the descriptor is `editor.transition` for `editor.transitionClipId`, every change is one `setTransition` transaction whose frame-exact result refills the fields, Preview loops the bound player over the window (rendered by `MediaProgram`, as export is), and Remove clears it. objectNames `transitionEditor`, `transitionRemove`, `transitionType`, `transitionDurationTicks`, `transitionAlignment`, `transitionParameter_<id>`, `transitionPreview`, `transitionCancelPreview`, `transitionStatus`. |

The host acknowledgment fixture is `examples/video-edit-surfaces/Main.qml`.
Its request handler demonstrates the seam with offline media and a stub
preview; it is not a sequence edit engine or transition renderer.

## Motion and keyframe surfaces

`AutomationModel` remains the typed curve/key store and transaction owner.
`MotionComposition` resolves a host layer hierarchy at one exact tick time by
sampling animated properties through that model. It composes 2D affine
transforms, visibility and opacity in stable z/ID order. Its versioned v1 JSON
manifest stores exact automation keys, layers and effect parameter records;
`QSaveFile` provides atomic local writes. Source pixels stay external behind
stable `sourceId` values. Ordered effect descriptors travel with each evaluated
source layer; the frame renderer executes its built-in brightness/grayscale
effects and host-registered CPU callbacks before applying that layer’s transform.
Loading validates a detached animation snapshot and the full layer hierarchy
before replacing the live model.
Malformed IDs, missing parent links, hierarchy cycles, invalid exact ticks and
missing animated curves return `{ok:false,error}` rather than partial output.

Each layer samples its curves at its own exact layer time:
`timeRemap(compositionTicks - timeOffsetTicks)`. `timeOffsetTicks` is a signed
decimal string; `timeRemap` is 1–1024 `{compTicks, layerTicks}` decimal pairs
with strictly increasing `compTicks`, evaluated piecewise-linearly with one
floor rounding (`TimelineTime::mulDiv`) and held outside the first/last pair,
so freezes, reversals and speed ramps stay integer-exact at any tick. Parenting
composes space only; every evaluated layer reports its `localTicks`.

Every edit gesture is one transaction. Key, path-key and tangent edits are
AutomationModel transactions; `applyBatch` records the x and y curve changes of
one path gesture as a single undo step. Layer-record edits
(`insertEffect`) use the composition's own bounded history (128 steps), which a
host replacement through `layers`/`loadContent` clears; the host orders the two
histories. `MotionFrameRenderer` caches recent frames per exact tick and size
for the current content revision only: layer, curve (including gesture
previews), source, effect-processor and roto changes invalidate the cache and
emit `contentInvalidated`, so preview surfaces re-request instead of showing
stale composites.

| Type | Snapshot and actions | Requests | Behavior and limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `MotionComposition` | `animationModel` (`Tk.AutomationModel`), `layers` (`layerId`, optional `parentLayerId`, external `sourceId`, integer `zIndex`, visibility, static `transform`, `animatedProperties`, optional `timeOffsetTicks`/`timeRemap`, optional ordered `effects` records), `revision`, `canUndo`/`canRedo`; `evaluate(timeTicks)`, `contentMap()`, `toJson()`, `loadContent()`, `loadJson()`, `saveToFile(localUrl)`, `loadFromFile(localUrl)`, `insertEffect(layerId, effect, index, expectedRevision)`, `undo()`, `redo()`, `pathHandles(layerId, timeTicks)`, `movePathKey(layerId, timeTicks, worldX, worldY)`, `setPathTangent(layerId, timeTicks, side, worldX, worldY)` | Returns evaluated `{ok,timeTicks,layers}` (each layer with `localTicks`), persistence `{ok,error,layerCount,animationParameterCount}` or edit `{ok,error,transactionId}` maps; `historyChanged`, `layerEditCommitted(transactionId, label)` | Manifest schema `qindatk.motion-composition`, version 1. AutomationModel content preserves exact decimal tick strings; ordered effect IDs, enabled flags and parameter maps are persisted and returned on evaluated source layers. Layer/source bytes are not embedded. Loads validate detached animation data, layer IDs/parents, transforms, curve mappings and effect records before replacement. Local writes use `QSaveFile`; local files and `loadJson()` are limited to 64 MiB, 10000 layers and 100000 effect records. `toJson()` serializes current in-memory content. `insertEffect` rejects stale revisions, unknown layers and invalid or duplicate effect records. Path edits convert world (frame pixel) positions through the inverse parent matrix at the layer's local time: `movePathKey` sets (or adds) the x/y keys there, clamped to each curve range; `setPathTangent` sets the `in`/`out` handles of both curves with one shared time-handle x, so the spatial segment is the 2D Bézier through the edited controls. `pathHandles` reports unset handles at CurveEvaluator's 1/3 and 2/3 defaults. |
| `MotionFrameRenderer` | `composition`, `lastError`, `contentRevision`, `frameTicks`, `frameRevision`, `frameCacheLimit` (0–64, default 8), `cachedFrameCount`, `clearFrameCache()`; `rotoModel`, `maskTicksPerFrame` (exact ticks, default 1), `sourceFrame(sourceId)`; `setSourceImage(sourceId, QImage, sourceFrame = -1)` and `registerEffectProcessor(effectId, callback)` for C++ hosts, `loadSource(sourceId, localUrl)` for local fixtures, `render(timeTicks,width,height)`, `exportPng(localUrl)` | `frameChanged`, `errorChanged`, `contentInvalidated`, `cacheChanged`; render returns drawn-layer count, missing source IDs, `cached` and `contentRevision` | CPU source-over frame compositor over evaluated exact-tick transforms. Tagged images convert to encoded sRGB; untagged inputs assume encoded sRGB. Ordered enabled effects run on straight RGBA8 encoded-sRGB pixels before their layer transform and source-over blending. Built-ins are additive `brightness` (`amount` in [-1,1]) and Rec.709 encoded-channel `grayscale`; disabled effects are skipped, and unsupported enabled effects fail the frame with an error. Hosts can register deterministic per-layer CPU callbacks before rendering. Source/output bounds remain 16 megapixels and source storage remains 256 MiB; at most 64 effect records execute per frame, with at most 32 parameters per effect. Callback output must preserve source dimensions. The LRU frame cache also stops at 128 MiB; a failed render keeps the previous frame, whose `frameRevision` then trails `contentRevision`. A layer `maskId` applies a `TrackingRotoModel` mask before the transform, sampled at the source frame given with its pixels, else at `floor(localTicks / maskTicksPerFrame)` (D-142). Rendering is synchronous on the caller's thread. No codec decode or broadcast color pipeline. |
| `CompositionView` | `renderer`, optional exact `timeTicks` with `frameWidth`/`frameHeight`, read-only `frameStale`, `tooltip`; `requestFrame()` | None | `T.Control` that displays the renderer's latest frame while preserving aspect ratio. With `timeTicks` and a frame size it requests that frame and re-requests it after each `contentInvalidated`; `frameStale` marks a displayed frame older than the renderer content. `MotionFrameItem` is its paint item. objectNames `compositionView`, `compositionFrame`. |
| `MotionPathOverlay` | `composition`, stable `layerId`, host-selected decimal `sampleTicks`, optional `keyPoints` (`id`, composition `ticks`), `selectedKeyId`, optional world `frameWidth`/`frameHeight`, `editingEnabled`; read-only-by-convention `pathPoints`, `handles`, `worldView`, `statusText`; `mapPoint()`, `unmapPoint()`, `rebuild()` | `keySelectionRequested(layerId, keyId, timeTicks)`, `pathEditCommitted(transactionId)`, `pathEditRejected(reason)` | Evaluates the composition at each supplied tick and draws translation samples (y down), fitted to the control or, with a frame size, letterboxed exactly like `CompositionView`. The selected key shows its in/out tangent handles. Dragging a key or handle previews locally and calls `movePathKey`/`setPathTangent` once at release, so each gesture is one AutomationModel transaction; Escape cancels. It rebuilds after layer or animation revisions. Samples are capped at 512; key selection stays a request. objectNames `motionPathOverlay`, `motionPathCanvas`. |
| `OnionSkinView` / `MotionOnionSkinItem` | Renderer, exact `previousTicks` / `currentTicks` / `nextTicks`, frame dimensions and neighbor opacity | `refresh()`; `lastError`, `stale` | Renders and retains adjacent/current `MotionFrameRenderer` images, tints the neighbors and paints them beneath the current frame. A changed request or `contentInvalidated` marks the snapshots stale and queues one refresh, which replaces all three only when every render succeeds. Each display refresh is bounded to 4 megapixels, goes through the renderer's frame cache and leaves the renderer at current time. Display-only; never serialized or exported, no composition edits. objectNames `onionSkinView`, `onionSkinComposite`. |
| `KeyframeTimeline` | `model`, ordered exact-string `timeBoundaries`, `playheadTicks`, `parameterIds`, selected property/key IDs, `editingEnabled`; `moveSelectedKey(direction)`, `addKey(parameterId,ticks,value)`, `addAtPlayhead()`, `deleteSelectedKey()`, `setSelectedInterpolation(mode)` | `selectionRequested(parameterId, pointId)`, `editRejected(reason)`, `editCommitted(transactionId)` | Displays host-provided exact-time grid positions and stable AutomationModel point IDs. Add/move/delete/interpolation changes use one `AutomationModel.applyEdit` transaction each; keyboard Left/Right moves to the adjacent supplied tick and Delete removes the selected key. It does not compute pixels-to-ticks or keep a shadow key list. objectNames `keyframeTimeline`, `keyframeAddAtPlayhead`, `keyframeParameterPicker`, `keyframeDelete`, `keyframeTracks`, `keyframeLane_<parameterId>`, `keyframeAxis_<parameterId>`, `keyframeKey_<pointId>`, `keyframeStatus`. |
| `DopeSheet` | Host `layers` with stable exposure IDs, exact start/end ticks and artwork IDs; ordered `frameBoundaries`, selected layer/exposure IDs, playhead, pending request and status/error | `selectionRequested(layerId, exposureId)`, `exposureEditRequested(requestId, action, layerId, exposureIds, startTicks, endTicks)` with actions `insertHold`, `retime`, `delete` | Frame-aligned cells are a view of existing artwork references; edits are requests and wait for the next host snapshot. Retime shifts the selected exposure by one boundary while preserving its frame count. It never duplicates, moves or discards artwork itself. objectNames `dopeSheet`, `dopeSheetInsertHold`, `dopeSheetDelete`, `dopeSheetRows`, `dopeSheetLayer_<layerId>`, `dopeSheetExposure_<exposureId>`, `dopeSheetStatus`. |
| `EffectBrowser` | Effect registry descriptors (`effectId`, label, category, description, compatibility and reason), target layer, document/revision IDs, selected effect, pending preview and status/error; search/category filters | `effectSelectionRequested(effectId)`, `effectPreviewRequested(requestId, effectId, targetLayerId)`, `effectInsertRequested(requestId, effectId, targetLayerId, expectedRevision)`, `previewCancelRequested(requestId, pendingRequestId)` | Compatible effects can request preview or insert against the captured host revision. Incompatible rows remain visible with reasons and reject preview/insertion. The host owns registry capabilities, jobs and parameter UI; for a `MotionComposition` it binds `revision` to `composition.revision` and answers an insert request with one `insertEffect(targetLayerId, {effectId}, -1, expectedRevision)` transaction (undo through `composition.undo()`). objectNames `effectBrowser`, `effectSearch`, `effectCategory`, `effectResults`, `effectResult_<effectId>`, `effectPreview`, `effectCancelPreview`, `effectInsert`. |

The fixtures are `examples/motion-overlays/Main.qml` (sampled path and adjacent frames) and `examples/motion-persistence/Main.qml` (Save, Change key and Reopen against a local v1 manifest). `examples/motion-authoring/Main.qml` composites two local
SVG sources at exact ticks, uses real undoable `AutomationModel` curves, and
leaves effect preview requests with the host. The `MotionSection` gallery
demonstrates keyframe, exposure and effect request surfaces separately.

### ScreenWorkArea

`Tk.ScreenWorkArea` is a nonvisual C++ observer. Set `anchor` to a Quick item
and `anchorPoint` to a point in that item’s coordinates (default origin).
Read `availableGeometry` in global logical coordinates. The screen containing
the anchor point is selected among the window’s virtual siblings; a point in
a desktop gap falls back to the window’s screen. A detached or destroyed
anchor reports an empty rectangle. `refresh()` synchronously rechecks it.

The observer tracks ancestor movement, reparenting, window movement, screen
changes, work-area notifications and DPI changes. Set `trackTransforms` when
arbitrary ancestor transforms must be checked after each GUI frame. `Menu`
enables that check only while visible, supplies its parent and popup position,
refreshes before opening, and caps its implicit height before native window
creation. Qt retains responsibility for native popup placement and flipping.
Qt reports geometry in logical coordinates; never multiply this rectangle
by device pixel ratio. The platform may not supply reserved work areas on
multi-monitor X11; see the [monitor qualification note](universal-toolkit/implementation/evidence/WP-007-FND-09-monitor.md).

## SceneView3D

`Tk.SceneView3D` renders a `Tk.SceneModel3D` through optional Qt Quick 3D. It supports primitive picking, on-object transform handles (an embedded `Tk.TransformGizmo3D`), a toolbar for constrained translation, world/local axis rotation and uniform scale, snapping, Escape cancellation and arrow-key movement. `transformMode`, `transformConstraint`, `transformSpace`, `rotationSnap` and `scaleSnap` configure the gesture; `translationSnap` retains its original world-grid behavior in free mode. Right drag orbits around `cameraTarget`, middle drag pans, and the wheel zooms; hosts can call `orbitCamera(dx,dy)`, `panCamera(dx,dy)` and `zoomCamera(steps)`. `meshDocument` optionally uploads a `Tk.MeshRigDocument` as posed, UV-textured triangles; `meshRay(x,y)` (a CPU perspective ray using `cameraFieldOfView`, identical to the GPU camera), `pickMeshFace(x,y)`, `meshPicked(worldPosition)` and `meshFacePicked(faceId, uv)` support host paint and selection gestures. `scene`, `cameraPosition`, `cameraEulerDegrees`, `rendererAvailable`, `tooltip` and `entityPicked(id, worldPosition)` remain the primitive surface. `viewRay(x,y)` returns a world-space `{origin, direction}`; read-only `renderGeneration` counts renderer instances, and `resetRenderer()` replaces the renderer, which the view also does when Qt invalidates its window's scene graph (device loss, non-persistent hide) after cancelling any gesture. Internal objectNames: `sceneView3D`, `sceneRenderer3D`, `sceneTransformBar3D`, `sceneMode3D_<mode>`, `sceneAxis3D_<constraint>`, `sceneSpace3D`, `sceneEntity3D_<UUID>`, `sceneMeshRig3D`. See [3D scene model and viewport](scene-3d.md) for hierarchy, persistence and renderer limits.

`Tk.TransformGizmo3D` (Item) draws screen-constant handles for `scene.selectedId` over any view that implements `projectWorldPosition(vector3d)` and `viewRay(x,y)` (`view`): X/Y/Z arrows and XY/XZ/YZ squares (`mode: "translate"`), axis rings (`"rotate"`) or a uniform-scale circle (`"scale"`), in `space` `world` or `local`. One drag is one `SceneModel3D` transaction (axis, plane, rotation or uniform-scale preview, commit on release, cancel on Escape or lost grab); presses away from a handle fall through. Properties: `scene`, `view`, `mode`, `space`, `translationSnap`, `rotationSnap`, `scaleSnap`, `armLength`, `hitTolerance`, `tooltip`; read-only `handles`, `activeHandle`, `hoveredHandle`; `refresh()`, `relayout()`, `handleAt(x,y)`, `nudge(handle, steps)`, `cancelGesture()`. ObjectNames: `transformGizmo3D`, `transformGizmoCanvas3D`, `transformGizmoPointer3D`, `transformGizmoHandle3D_<x|y|z|uniform>` (accessible Button targets with press/increase/decrease actions). See [scene-3d.md](scene-3d.md#transform-gizmo).

`Tk.MaterialPreview` is a `T.Control` bound through its `scene` property to a `SceneModel3D` and an
`entityId` (defaulting to the scene selection). Its optional Quick3D renderer
shows a shaded sphere from the entity material; the inspector commits base
color, roughness, metalness and emission through `SceneModel3D.setMaterial`.
Properties: `scene`, `entityId`, `materialData`, `rendererAvailable`, `tooltip`.
ObjectNames: `materialPreview`, `materialPreviewSurface`,
`materialPreviewRenderer`, `materialPreviewSphere`, `materialBaseColor`,
`materialRoughness`, `materialMetalness`, `materialEmissiveColor`.

## Rig, UV and texture authoring

`MeshRigDocument` is the host-owned source of mesh and rig snapshots. These
controls consume stable IDs and emit edit requests; they do not keep a second
authoritative copy of geometry. `SceneView3D` renders a `MeshRigDocument` when
supplied as `meshDocument`, including its roughness and metalness channels;
`meshRay(x, y)` returns the CPU camera ray for a view pixel, `pickMeshFace(x, y)`
the stable face under it, and a mesh click emits `meshFacePicked(faceId, uv)`
so a host can synchronize UV selection. `cameraFieldOfView` (degrees, vertical)
is shared by the GPU camera and those rays. `meshTool` (`""`, `"select"`,
`"paint"`) hands left clicks on the mesh to a CPU layer (`sceneMeshLayer3D`,
drawn by `sceneMeshOverlay3D`, input `sceneMeshGesture3D`; toolbar buttons
`sceneMeshTool3D_select`/`_paint`) that works without an RHI renderer:
`"select"` emits `meshBonePicked(boneId)` near a posed bone and otherwise
`meshFacePicked`; `"paint"` emits `meshStrokeBegan()`,
`meshPaintRayRequested(origin, direction)` per sample through the pixel under
the pointer, and `meshStrokeFinished()`/`meshStrokeCancelled()`.
`selectedMeshFaceId` and `selectedBoneId` highlight the host's selection over
the mesh, and `showMeshBones` draws the posed skeleton (D-292). `RigEditor`
independently draws a 2D orthographic skeleton projection.

| Type | Snapshot | Requests | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `RigEditor` | `bones` (`boneId`, `name`, `parentId`, `head`, `tail`, `minimum`, `maximum`, `pose`, `sourcePose`, `evaluatedPose`, `animated`, `constraints`, `keys`), `vertices` (`vertexId`, `weights`), `selectedBoneId`, `selectedVertexId`, `mode` (`pose`, `weights`, `author`), `currentFrame`, `constraintInfluence` | `boneSelected(boneId)`, `vertexSelected(vertexId)`, `bonePoseRequested(boneId, eulerDegrees)`, `vertexWeightsRequested(vertexId, weights)`, `resetPoseRequested()`, `constraintAddRequested(boneId, type, targetId, influence)`, `constraintRemoveRequested(constraintId)`, `poseKeyRequested(boneId, frame, eulerDegrees)`, `poseKeyRemoveRequested(boneId, frame)`, `frameRequested(frame)`, `editGestureStarted()`, `editGestureFinished()`, and in author mode the `RigAuthoringPanel` requests | Draws an orthographic XY projection of rest bone segments with accessible bone/vertex lists. Axis sliders edit `sourcePose` (the static pose, or the keyed value at the frame) within the bone limits, never the constrained `evaluatedPose`, so constraint output is not baked; the caption shows the evaluated rotation. Key/Remove key (K) key the selected bone at `currentFrame`; Copy rotation requests a `copyRotation` constraint from the chosen bone (the document refuses loops). Reset requests a static-pose reset (keys are kept). Weight mode edits one selected bone influence and proportionally redistributes the others before emitting a full stable-ID weight map. Pose and weight slider drags are bracketed by `editGestureStarted`/`editGestureFinished`; a host forwards them to `MeshRigDocument.beginEditGesture`/`endEditGesture` so one drag is one undo step. The mode switch (Pose, Weights, Rig) keeps authoring and posing explicit. The host validates and commits every request. objectNames `rigEditor`, `rigEditMode`, `rigFrame`, `rigProjection`, `rigBone_<boneId>`, `rigVertex_<vertexId>`, `rigPoseAxis_X|Y|Z`, `rigSelectedWeight`, `rigKey`, `rigRemoveKey`, `rigConstraintTarget`, `rigAddConstraint`, `rigConstraint_<constraintId>`, `rigEvaluatedPose`. |
| `RigAuthoringPanel` | `bones`, `selectedBoneId`; read-only `selectedBone`, `parentCandidates` (no descendants of the selected bone) | `boneAddRequested(name, parentId, head, tail)`, `boneRemoveRequested(boneId)`, `boneRenameRequested(boneId, name)`, `boneParentRequested(boneId, parentId)`, `boneRestRequested(boneId, head, tail)`, `boneLimitsRequested(boneId, minimum, maximum)` | RigEditor's author mode, usable alone: name field, parent picker, Add child (continues the selected bone by its own length; Add bone makes a root), Remove, and head/tail/min/max X-Y-Z fields. It edits rig structure only, never poses or keys; the host commits through `addBone`, `removeBone`, `renameBone`, `setBoneParent`, `setBoneRest`, `setBoneLimits`, which refuse cycles, dangling references and limits that exclude stored poses. objectNames `rigAuthoringPanel`, `rigBoneName`, `rigBoneParent`, `rigAddBone`, `rigRemoveBone`, `rigAuthor_<head|tail|min|max><X|Y|Z>`. |
| `UVEditor` | `faces` (`faceId`, `vertexIds`, `uvIds`, `uv` corners, `seams`), `vertices`, `uvIslands`, `selectedFaceId`, `seamMode`, `stitchMode`, `packPreview` (island `bounds` from `packUvLayout`), `packMargin`; read-only `packHovered` | `faceSelected(faceId)`, `uvCornerMoveRequested(faceId, corner, normalizedUv)`, `uvIslandTranslateRequested(faceId, delta)`, `uvIslandTransformRequested(faceId, translation, rotationDegrees, scale)`, `seamToggleRequested(vertexA, vertexB)`, `seamStitchRequested(vertexA, vertexB)`, `uvUnwrapRequested(faceId)`, `uvPackRequested(margin)` | Displays triangle UVs in normalized `[0,1]` space with `v=0` at the bottom. Drag a corner to request its new coordinate; drag elsewhere in a triangle to translate its island. Seam mode click cuts/clears the nearest edge (S toggles seam mode); with Stitch checked it requests a weld of a seam edge instead. Rotate (R) requests a 90° island rotation; Unwrap requests a planar projection of the selected island; Pack requests packing, and a host that binds `packPreview` while `packHovered` shows the dashed packed rectangles first. The host applies each request and republishes a snapshot. objectNames `uvEditor`, `uvEditMode`, `uvStitchMode`, `uvRotate`, `uvUnwrap`, `uvPack`, `uvSurface`. |
| `TexturePaintView` | `textureDataUrl`, `textureWidth`, `textureHeight`, `channels`, `channel`, brush color/radius/opacity/`brushHardness`, `paintEnabled`; read-only `textureRect` | `strokeBegan(channel)`, `paintUvRequested(uv, color, radiusPixels, opacity, hardness)`, `strokeFinished()`, `strokeCancelled()`, `channelSelected(channel)` | Shows the selected material channel (`baseColor`, `roughness`, `metalness`) aspect-fitted in `textureRect` and paints in texture space: `uvAt(point)` maps the pointer to the UV of the texel drawn under it (v up), and a stroke is routed to `MeshRigDocument.beginPaint(channel)/paintUv/endPaint`. The document centres each dab on the surface point with that UV, so it is a sphere on the mesh and continues across UV seams. Camera painting happens where the camera view is shown: `SceneView3D` with `meshTool: "paint"`, using this view's brush and channel (D-292). With `paintEnabled` false it is a preview. objectNames `texturePaintView`, `texturePaintChannels`, `texturePaintSurface`, `texturePreview`, `texturePaintBrush`. |

The mock-snapshot fixture is `examples/rig-uv/Main.qml`. It shows the
projection and request boundary without presenting mock data as evaluated
mesh output.

## Video tracking and masks

| Type | Base | Main properties and requests | Internal objectNames and behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TrackingRotoModel` | C++ `QObject` | Read-only `tracks`, `masks`, `analysisRunning`, `analysisProgress`, `loadingFrames`, `frameLoadProgress`, `loadedFrames`, `lastError`, `canUndo`, `canRedo`; `loadMediaFrames(uri,firstFrame,count)`, `cancelFrameLoad`, `addTrack`, `setTrackOptions(trackId,radius,searchRadius,motion)`, `startAnalysis(trackId,startFrame,endFrame,occlusionFrames)`, `cancelAnalysis`, `acceptAnalysis`, `correctPoint`, `offsetTrack`, `rebaseTrack`, `addMask`, `setMaskKeyframe`, `removeMaskKeyframe`, `setMaskFeather`, `evaluateMask`, `previewTrackedMask`, `previewPropagation(maskId,sourceFrame,trackIds,fromFrame,toFrame)`, `applyPropagation(...)`, `applyTrackToMask`, `undo`, `redo`; `frameLoadFinished(ok,error)` | Owns tracking jobs, accepted edits, and keyframed path state. Tracks solve translation or, with motion `similarity`, rotation and scale; four tracks corner-pin a mask. Previews are separate from accepted edits: undo keeps them, and replacing a measured frame discards them. Mask keys carry `source` `user`/`generated`; propagation never overwrites a user key. Analysis and frame decoding run on a worker and cancel immediately; lost frames hold the last trusted position and occlusion search can reacquire. Frame IDs are source frame indices. Track positions use source-pixel coordinates; roto path points use normalized `[0,1]` coordinates. `TrackingPanel` and `RotoscopeEditor` only present snapshots and emit requests. See [tracking and roto model](tracking-roto.md) for formats and behavior. |
| `TrackingPanel` | `T.Control` | `tracks` (host snapshots), `selectedTrackId`, `startFrame`, `endFrame`, `currentFrame`, `occlusionFrames`, `analysisProgress`, `loadingFrames`, `frameLoadProgress`, `targets` (`[{maskId,label}]`), `targetMaskId`, `maskSourceFrame`, `offsetX`, `offsetY`, `tooltip`; read-only `lostCount`, `running`; `setOptions(radius,searchRadius,motion)`, `rebase()`, `offset(dx,dy)`, `applyToMask()`; `trackSelected(trackId)`, `startRequested(trackId,startFrame,endFrame,occlusionFrames)`, `cancelRequested(trackId)`, `acceptRequested(trackId)`, `correctionRequested(trackId,frame,x,y)`, `optionsChangeRequested(trackId,radius,searchRadius,motion)`, `rebaseRequested(trackId,frame)`, `offsetRequested(trackId,dx,dy,fromFrame,toFrame)`, `targetSelected(maskId)`, `applyToMaskRequested(trackId,maskId,sourceFrame,fromFrame,toFrame)` | `trackingTrackSelect`, `trackingRange`, `trackingStartFrame`, `trackingEndFrame`, `trackingOcclusion`, `trackingOptions`, `trackingPatchRadius`, `trackingSearchRadius`, `trackingMotion`, `trackingProgressRow`, `trackingProgress`, `trackingProgressText`, `trackingFeatureList`, `trackingFeature_<frame>`, `trackingRun`, `trackingCancel`, `trackingAccept`, `trackingRebase`, `trackingOffsetX`, `trackingOffsetY`, `trackingOffset`, `trackingApplyRow`, `trackingTargetSelect`, `trackingApplyMask`, `trackingStatus`. In/Out/occlusion fields set the analysis range; patch, search and motion edit the selected track's options; a progress bar shows analysis or frame decoding while a job runs. Rebase uses the accepted, nonlost point at `currentFrame`; Offset shifts accepted points in In–Out; Apply to mask maps the track onto the chosen target over In–Out from `maskSourceFrame`. Edits are disabled while the track runs. Consumes `TrackingRotoModel.tracks`; positions are source-pixel x/y values and confidence remains host snapshot data. State `lost` is shown in danger color with explicit unapplied-result copy; a finished track with lost (occluded, then reacquired) points shows a warning badge and says those frames hold their last trusted position. `correctPoint(frame,x,y)` emits source-pixel coordinates (empty frame uses `currentFrame`) from the host viewer interaction. Run/cancel/accept emit requests without optimistic state changes. |
| `RotoscopeEditor` | `T.Control` | `masks`, `maskId`, `currentFrame`, `sourceFrame`, `sourceFrameUrl`, `sourceAspectRatio`, `trackingTrackId`, `propagationPreview`, `propagationFrom`, `propagationTo`, `generatedPreview`, `feather`, `tooltip`; read-only `keySource`, `rangePreview`, `previewPath`; `maskSelected(maskId)`, `propagateRequested(maskId,sourceFrame,targetFrame,trackId)`, `propagateRangeRequested(maskId,sourceFrame,fromFrame,toFrame,trackId)`, `acceptRangeRequested(maskId,sourceFrame,fromFrame,toFrame,trackIds)`, `acceptPreviewRequested(maskId,frame,points)`, `cancelPreviewRequested(maskId,frame)`, `pointEditPreviewRequested(maskId,frame,points)`, `keyframeEditRequested(maskId,frame,points)`, `keyframeRemoveRequested(maskId,frame)`, `featherChangeRequested(maskId,feather)`, `editCancelled(maskId,frame)`; `previewPoint`, `movePoint`, `addPoint`, `removePoint`, `requestRangePropagation()`, `cancelEdit()` | `rotoMaskSelect`, `rotoStatus`, `rotoKeySource`, `rotoPropagate`, `rotoPropagateRange`, `rotoAcceptPreview`, `rotoCancelPreview`, `rotoRemoveKeyframe`, `rotoFeather`, `rotoCanvas`, `rotoFrameSurface`, `rotoPath`, `rotoPoint_<pointId>`. Consumes keyframe snapshots with stable mask/frame/point IDs and normalized coordinates. The host supplies a frame URL and its aspect ratio so the path overlays the source image without stretching. Propagation only requests a host preview; set `propagationPreview` from `TrackingRotoModel.previewTrackedMask` to show its dashed path. Accept or discard it explicitly; acceptance asks the host to commit that path as a keyframe. With `propagationFrom`/`propagationTo` set, Range requests a whole-range preview (set `propagationPreview` from `previewPropagation`; the status counts its keys and the dashed path shows the current frame's key) and accepting asks the host to `applyPropagation`. `rotoKeySource` shows whether the current key is a user key or a generated (tracked) key, and generated keys draw in the warning colour. Drag previews submit full point snapshots; pointer release commits one keyframe request. Point count stays at 3–256. Escape cancels an in-progress drag; Delete/Backspace removes a focused point. Generated preview styling is explicit and display-only. |

## MeshRigDocument

`Tk.MeshRigDocument` is a C++ QML document model for triangulated rest geometry, stable vertex/face/UV-corner/bone IDs, bounded hierarchical bone poses with copy-rotation constraints and keyed animation, linear blend skinning, UV seams, islands, stitch, unwrap and pack, and three camera-painted texture channels (`baseColor`, `roughness`, `metalness`). Read-only `vertices`, `faces`, `bones`, `uvIslands`, `textureDataUrl`, `textureDataUrls`, `textureChannels`, `textureWidth`, `textureHeight`, `painting`, `canUndo`, `canRedo`, `documentId`, `revision`, `undoService`, `lastError` and read-write `currentFrame` expose state; edits are steps in one `UndoService` history (adoptable into a shared service). Geometry/rig/UV commands, bracketed paint strokes, undo/redo, versioned save/reopen and OBJ/PNG export are described in [mesh-rig.md](mesh-rig.md). The optional `Tk.MeshRigGeometry` uploads its posed triangles to `SceneView3D`; host apps can also use the OBJ/PNG output with another renderer.

## Raster companion — QindaTK.Raster

| Type | Base | Contract |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `RasterView` | `Tk.Viewport` | `source`, `documentSize`, `displayRect`, `selectionRect`, `paintingEnabled`, `smooth`; native stylus `strokeBegan/Moved/Ended(sample)` and `strokeCanceled()`. Input and overlays use original document coordinates regardless of display proxy/tile resolution. Disabling painting enables left-button pan. Stable names and details: [raster.md](raster.md). |
| `Checkerboard` | Item | Theme-derived `lightColor`, `darkColor`, `cellSize`; one tiny repeated texture independent of document dimensions. Stable name `rasterCheckerboard`. |
